namespace SilverlightContrib.Xaml
{
	public static class Formatter
	{
		public static string Format(this bool value)
		{
			if (!value)
			{
				return "false";
			}
			return "true";
		}

		public static string Format(this short value)
		{
			return value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		public static string Format(this int value)
		{
			return value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		public static string Format(this double value)
		{
			return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0:0.###}", value);
		}

		public static string Format(this Enum value)
		{
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this System.Windows.Point value)
		{
			return $"{value.X.Format()},{value.Y.Format()}";
		}

		public static string Format(this Rect value)
		{
			return $"{value.X.Format()},{value.Y.Format()},{value.Width.Format()},{value.Height.Format()}";
		}

		public static string Format(this System.Windows.Size value)
		{
			return $"{value.Width.Format()},{value.Height.Format()}";
		}

		public static string Format(this Thickness value)
		{
			if (value.Left == value.Right && value.Right == value.Top && value.Top == value.Bottom)
			{
				return value.Left.Format();
			}
			if (value.Left == value.Right && value.Top == value.Bottom)
			{
				return $"{value.Left.Format()},{value.Top.Format()}";
			}
			return $"{value.Left.Format()},{value.Top.Format()},{value.Right.Format()},{value.Bottom.Format()}";
		}

		public static string Format(this CornerRadius value)
		{
			if (value.BottomLeft == value.BottomRight && value.BottomRight == value.TopLeft && value.TopLeft == value.TopRight)
			{
				return value.BottomLeft.Format();
			}
			return $"{value.TopLeft.Format()},{value.TopRight.Format()},{value.BottomRight.Format()},{value.BottomLeft.Format()}";
		}

		public static string Format(this GridLength value)
		{
			if (value.GridUnitType == GridUnitType.Auto)
			{
				return "Auto";
			}
			if (value.GridUnitType == GridUnitType.Pixel)
			{
				return value.Value.Format();
			}
			if (value.Value == 1.0)
			{
				return "*";
			}
			return $"{value.Value.Format()}*";
		}

		public static string Format(this Cursor value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "Default";
			}
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this System.Windows.Media.Color value)
		{
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this Uri value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this System.Windows.Media.FontFamily value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
			return value.Source.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this FontStretch value)
		{
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this System.Windows.FontStyle value)
		{
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this FontWeight value)
		{
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this TextDecorationCollection value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "None";
			}
			return "Underline";
		}

		public static string Format(this DoubleCollection value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (int i = 0; i < value.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(value[i].Format());
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string Format(this PointCollection value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("value");
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (int i = 0; i < value.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(" ");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(value[i].Format());
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}
	public class XamlWriter : IDisposable
	{
		public const string NamespaceClient = "http://schemas.microsoft.com/client/2007";

		public const string NamespaceXaml = "http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml";

		public const string PrefixXaml = "x";

		private static readonly System.Windows.Media.Color DefaultColor = System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue);

		private static readonly CornerRadius DefaultCornerRadius = default(CornerRadius);

		private static readonly System.Windows.Point DefaultPoint = default(System.Windows.Point);

		private static readonly Rect DefaultRect = default(Rect);

		private static readonly System.Windows.Size DefaultSize = default(System.Windows.Size);

		private static readonly Thickness DefaultThickness = default(Thickness);

		private XmlWriter writer;

		private XamlWriterSettings settings;

		private bool isXamlComplete = true;

		private bool isXamlTruncated;

		private int elementCount;

		private bool disposed;

		public bool IsXamlComplete => isXamlComplete;

		public bool IsXamlTruncated => isXamlTruncated;

		public int ElementCount => elementCount;

		public XamlWriterSettings Settings => settings;

		public XamlWriter(XmlWriter writer, XamlWriterSettings settings)
		{
			if (writer == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("writer");
			}
			if (settings == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("settings");
			}
			this.writer = writer;
			this.settings = settings;
		}

		~XamlWriter()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		private void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (!disposed)
			{
				writer.Close();
				writer = null;
				disposed = true;
			}
		}

		protected void WriteStartElement(string name)
		{
			writer.WriteStartElement(name, "http://schemas.microsoft.com/client/2007");
		}

		protected void WriteEndElement()
		{
			writer.WriteEndElement();
		}

		protected virtual string WriteStartElement(object o)
		{
			string name = o.GetType().Name;
			WriteStartElement(name);
			return name;
		}

		protected virtual string WriteStartElement(DependencyObject o)
		{
			string result = WriteStartElement((object)o);
			string value = (string)o.GetValue(FrameworkElement.NameProperty);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("x", "Name", "http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml", value);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool WriteElement(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (elementCount++ == settings.MaxUIElements)
			{
				WriteComment("XAML truncated, reached the specified maximum number of {0} UI elements", settings.MaxUIElements);
				isXamlComplete = false;
				isXamlTruncated = true;
				return false;
			}
			string text = WriteStartElement(element);
			if (element.Parent is Canvas)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Canvas.Left", Canvas.GetLeft(element), 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("Canvas.Top", Canvas.GetTop(element), 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("Canvas.ZIndex", Panel.GetZIndex(element), 0.0);
			}
			else if (element.Parent is Grid)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Grid.Row", Grid.GetRow(element), 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("Grid.Column", Grid.GetColumn(element), 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("Grid.RowSpan", Grid.GetRowSpan(element), 1.0);
				WriteAttribute("Grid.ColumnSpan", Grid.GetColumnSpan(element), 1.0);
			}
			WriteAttribute("Width", element.Width, 0.0);
			WriteAttribute("Height", element.Height, 0.0);
			WriteAttribute("MinWidth", element.MinWidth, 0.0);
			WriteAttribute("MaxWidth", element.MaxWidth, double.PositiveInfinity);
			WriteAttribute("MinHeight", element.MinHeight, 0.0);
			WriteAttribute("MaxHeight", element.MaxHeight, double.PositiveInfinity);
			WriteAttribute("Margin", element.Margin, DefaultThickness);
			WriteAttribute("HorizontalAlignment", element.HorizontalAlignment, HorizontalAlignment.Stretch);
			WriteAttribute("VerticalAlignment", element.VerticalAlignment, VerticalAlignment.Stretch);
			WriteAttribute("Opacity", element.Opacity, 1.0);
			WriteAttribute("Visibility", element.Visibility, Visibility.Visible);
			WriteAttribute("IsHitTestVisible", element.IsHitTestVisible, defaultValue: true);
			WriteAttribute("RenderTransformOrigin", element.RenderTransformOrigin, DefaultPoint);
			WriteAttribute("Cursor", element.Cursor, Cursors.Arrow);
			Panel panel = null;
			Control control = null;
			if (element is Border border)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(border, text);
			}
			else if (element is Panel panel2)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(panel2, text);
			}
			else if (element is Shape shape)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(shape, text);
			}
			else if (element is TextBox textBox)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(textBox, text);
			}
			else if (element is Control control2)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(control2, text);
			}
			else if (element is TextBlock textBlock)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(textBlock, text);
			}
			else if (element is System.Windows.Controls.Image image)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(image, text);
			}
			else if (element is MediaElement element2)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(element2, text);
			}
			else if (element is Glyphs glyphs)
			{
				WriteInnerElement(glyphs, text);
			}
			else if (element.GetType().IsPublic)
			{
				WriteComment("XAML for {0} not implemented yet", text);
				isXamlComplete = false;
			}
			if (element.Triggers != null && element.Triggers.Count > 0)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{text}.Triggers");
				WriteElement(element.Triggers);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (element.Clip != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{text}.Clip");
				WriteElement(element.Clip);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (element.OpacityMask != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{text}.OpacityMask");
				WriteElement(element.OpacityMask);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (element.RenderTransform != null && (!(element.RenderTransform is MatrixTransform matrixTransform) || (matrixTransform != null && !matrixTransform.Matrix.IsIdentity)))
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{text}.RenderTransform");
				WriteElement(element.RenderTransform);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			bool result = WriteContent(element);
			WriteEndElement();
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual bool WriteContent(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(element);
			bool result = true;
			for (int i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++)
			{
				FrameworkElement element2 = (FrameworkElement)VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(element, i);
				if (!WriteElement(element2))
				{
					result = false;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void WriteElement(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is FrameworkElement)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as FrameworkElement);
			}
			else if (obj is System.Windows.Media.Brush)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as System.Windows.Media.Brush);
			}
			else if (obj is Geometry)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as Geometry);
			}
			else if (obj is Inline)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as Inline);
			}
			else if (obj is PathFigure)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as PathFigure);
			}
			else if (obj is PathSegment)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as PathSegment);
			}
			else if (obj is Stroke)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as Stroke);
			}
			else if (obj is Timeline)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as Timeline);
			}
			else if (obj is Transform)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as Transform);
			}
			else if (obj is TriggerCollection)
			{
				WriteElement(obj as TriggerCollection);
			}
		}

		private void WriteElement(TriggerCollection triggers)
		{
			foreach (EventTrigger trigger in triggers)
			{
				WriteStartElement(trigger);
				foreach (BeginStoryboard action in trigger.Actions)
				{
					WriteStartElement(action);
					WriteElement(action.Storyboard);
					writer.WriteEndElement();
				}
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private void WriteElement(Timeline timeline)
		{
		}

		private void WriteElement(Stroke stroke)
		{
			string arg = WriteStartElement(stroke);
			WriteStartElement($"{arg}.DrawingAttributes");
			WriteStartElement("DrawingAttributes");
			WriteAttribute("Color", stroke.DrawingAttributes.Color, Colors.Black);
			WriteAttribute("Width", stroke.DrawingAttributes.Width, 3.0);
			WriteAttribute("Height", stroke.DrawingAttributes.Height, 3.0);
			WriteEndElement();
			WriteEndElement();
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		private void WriteElement(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			string arg = WriteStartElement(brush);
			WriteAttribute("Opacity", brush.Opacity, 1.0);
			if (brush is GradientBrush gradientBrush)
			{
				if (gradientBrush is LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush)
				{
					WriteAttribute("StartPoint", linearGradientBrush.StartPoint, DefaultPoint);
					WriteAttribute("EndPoint", linearGradientBrush.EndPoint, new System.Windows.Point(1.0, 1.0));
				}
				if (gradientBrush is RadialGradientBrush radialGradientBrush)
				{
					WriteAttribute("Center", radialGradientBrush.Center, new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5));
					WriteAttribute("GradientOrigin", radialGradientBrush.GradientOrigin, new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5));
					WriteAttribute("RadiusX", radialGradientBrush.RadiusX, 0.5);
					WriteAttribute("RadiusY", radialGradientBrush.RadiusY, 0.5);
				}
				WriteAttribute("ColorInterpolationMode", gradientBrush.ColorInterpolationMode, ColorInterpolationMode.SRgbLinearInterpolation);
				WriteAttribute("MappingMode", gradientBrush.MappingMode, BrushMappingMode.RelativeToBoundingBox);
				WriteAttribute("SpreadMethod", gradientBrush.SpreadMethod, GradientSpreadMethod.Pad);
				foreach (GradientStop gradientStop in gradientBrush.GradientStops)
				{
					WriteStartElement(gradientStop);
					WriteAttribute("Color", gradientStop.Color, DefaultColor);
					WriteAttribute("Offset", gradientStop.Offset, 0.0);
					WriteEndElement();
				}
			}
			else if (brush is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Color", solidColorBrush.Color, DefaultColor);
			}
			else if (brush is TileBrush tileBrush)
			{
				if (tileBrush is ImageBrush { ImageSource: BitmapImage imageSource })
				{
					WriteAttribute("ImageSource", imageSource.UriSource);
				}
				WriteAttribute("AlignmentX", tileBrush.AlignmentX, AlignmentX.Center);
				WriteAttribute("AlignmentY", tileBrush.AlignmentY, AlignmentY.Center);
				WriteAttribute("Stretch", tileBrush.Stretch, Stretch.Fill);
			}
			if (brush.Transform != null && (!(brush.Transform is MatrixTransform) || !(brush.Transform as MatrixTransform).Matrix.IsIdentity))
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{arg}.Transform");
				WriteElement(brush.Transform);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (brush.RelativeTransform != null && (!(brush.RelativeTransform is MatrixTransform) || !(brush.RelativeTransform as MatrixTransform).Matrix.IsIdentity))
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{arg}.RelativeTransform");
				WriteElement(brush.RelativeTransform);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		public void WriteElement(Transform transform)
		{
			if (transform == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("transform");
			}
			string arg = WriteStartElement(transform);
			if (transform is MatrixTransform matrixTransform)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{arg}.Matrix");
				WriteStartElement("Matrix");
				WriteAttribute("M11", matrixTransform.Matrix.M11, 1.0);
				WriteAttribute("M12", matrixTransform.Matrix.M12, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("M21", matrixTransform.Matrix.M21, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("M22", matrixTransform.Matrix.M22, 1.0);
				WriteAttribute("OffsetX", matrixTransform.Matrix.OffsetX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("OffsetY", matrixTransform.Matrix.OffsetY, 0.0);
				WriteEndElement();
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			else if (transform is RotateTransform rotateTransform)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Angle", rotateTransform.Angle, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("CenterX", rotateTransform.CenterX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("CenterY", rotateTransform.CenterY, 0.0);
			}
			else if (transform is ScaleTransform scaleTransform)
			{
				WriteAttribute("ScaleX", scaleTransform.ScaleX, 1.0);
				WriteAttribute("ScaleY", scaleTransform.ScaleY, 1.0);
				WriteAttribute("CenterX", scaleTransform.CenterX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("CenterY", scaleTransform.CenterY, 0.0);
			}
			else if (transform is SkewTransform skewTransform)
			{
				WriteAttribute("AngleX", skewTransform.AngleX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("AngleY", skewTransform.AngleY, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("CenterX", skewTransform.CenterX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("CenterY", skewTransform.CenterY, 0.0);
			}
			else if (transform is TransformGroup transformGroup)
			{
				foreach (Transform child in transformGroup.Children)
				{
					WriteElement(child);
				}
			}
			else if (transform is TranslateTransform translateTransform)
			{
				WriteAttribute("X", translateTransform.X, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("Y", translateTransform.Y, 0.0);
			}
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		private void WriteElement(Geometry geometry)
		{
			string arg = WriteStartElement(geometry);
			if (geometry is EllipseGeometry ellipseGeometry)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Center", ellipseGeometry.Center, DefaultPoint);
				WriteAttribute("RadiusX", ellipseGeometry.RadiusX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("RadiusY", ellipseGeometry.RadiusY, 0.0);
			}
			else if (geometry is GeometryGroup geometryGroup)
			{
				WriteAttribute("FillRule", geometryGroup.FillRule, FillRule.EvenOdd);
				foreach (Geometry child in geometryGroup.Children)
				{
					WriteElement(child);
				}
			}
			else if (geometry is LineGeometry lineGeometry)
			{
				WriteAttribute("StartPoint", lineGeometry.StartPoint, DefaultPoint);
				WriteAttribute("EndPoint", lineGeometry.EndPoint, DefaultPoint);
			}
			else if (geometry is PathGeometry pathGeometry)
			{
				WriteAttribute("FillRule", pathGeometry.FillRule, FillRule.EvenOdd);
				WriteComment("XAML for Path Data is not available due to Silverlight runtime limitations");
				isXamlComplete = false;
			}
			else if (geometry is RectangleGeometry rectangleGeometry)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Rect", rectangleGeometry.Rect, DefaultRect);
				WriteAttribute("RadiusX", rectangleGeometry.RadiusX, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("RadiusY", rectangleGeometry.RadiusY, 0.0);
			}
			if (geometry.Transform != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{arg}.Transform");
				WriteElement(geometry.Transform);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		private void WriteElement(PathFigure figure)
		{
			WriteStartElement(figure);
			WriteAttribute("StartPoint", figure.StartPoint, DefaultPoint);
			WriteAttribute("IsClosed", figure.IsClosed, defaultValue: false);
			WriteAttribute("IsFilled", figure.IsFilled, defaultValue: true);
			foreach (PathSegment segment in figure.Segments)
			{
				WriteElement(segment);
			}
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		private void WriteElement(PathSegment segment)
		{
			WriteStartElement(segment);
			if (segment is ArcSegment arcSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("IsLargeArc", arcSegment.IsLargeArc, defaultValue: false);
				WriteAttribute("Point", arcSegment.Point, DefaultPoint);
				WriteAttribute("RotationAngle", arcSegment.RotationAngle, 0.0);
				WriteAttribute("Size", arcSegment.Size, DefaultSize);
				WriteAttribute("SweepDirection", arcSegment.SweepDirection, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise);
			}
			else if (segment is BezierSegment bezierSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Point1", bezierSegment.Point1, DefaultPoint);
				WriteAttribute("Point2", bezierSegment.Point2, DefaultPoint);
				WriteAttribute("Point3", bezierSegment.Point3, DefaultPoint);
			}
			else if (segment is LineSegment lineSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Point", lineSegment.Point, DefaultPoint);
			}
			else if (segment is PolyBezierSegment polyBezierSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Points", polyBezierSegment.Points);
			}
			else if (segment is PolyLineSegment polyLineSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Points", polyLineSegment.Points);
			}
			else if (segment is PolyQuadraticBezierSegment polyQuadraticBezierSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Points", polyQuadraticBezierSegment.Points);
			}
			else if (segment is QuadraticBezierSegment quadraticBezierSegment)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Point1", quadraticBezierSegment.Point1, DefaultPoint);
				WriteAttribute("Point2", quadraticBezierSegment.Point2, DefaultPoint);
			}
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(Border border, string typeName)
		{
			if (TryParseColorValue(border.Background, out var value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Background", value.Value, DefaultColor);
			}
			if (TryParseColorValue(border.BorderBrush, out var value2))
			{
				WriteAttribute("BorderBrush", value2.Value, DefaultColor);
			}
			WriteAttribute("BorderThickness", border.BorderThickness, DefaultThickness);
			WriteAttribute("CornerRadius", border.CornerRadius, DefaultCornerRadius);
			WriteAttribute("Padding", border.Padding, DefaultThickness);
			if (!value.HasValue && border.Background != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Background");
				WriteElement(border.Background);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (!value2.HasValue && border.BorderBrush != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.BorderBrush");
				WriteElement(border.BorderBrush);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void WriteInnerElement(Control control, string typeName)
		{
			if (TryParseColorValue(control.Background, out var value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Background", value.Value, Colors.Black);
			}
			if (TryParseColorValue(control.Foreground, out var value2))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Foreground", value2.Value, Colors.Black);
			}
			if (TryParseColorValue(control.BorderBrush, out var value3))
			{
				WriteAttribute("BorderBrush", value3.Value, DefaultColor);
			}
			WriteAttribute("BorderThickness", control.BorderThickness, DefaultThickness);
			WriteAttribute("FontFamily", control.FontFamily, "Portable User Interface");
			WriteAttribute("FontSize", control.FontSize, 11.0);
			WriteAttribute("FontStretch", control.FontStretch, FontStretches.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("FontStyle", control.FontStyle, FontStyles.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("FontWeight", control.FontWeight, FontWeights.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("HorizontalContentAlignment", control.HorizontalContentAlignment, HorizontalAlignment.Left);
			WriteAttribute("VerticalContentAlignment", control.VerticalContentAlignment, VerticalAlignment.Top);
			WriteAttribute("Padding", control.Padding, DefaultThickness);
			WriteAttribute("IsTabStop", control.IsTabStop, defaultValue: true);
			WriteAttribute("TabIndex", control.TabIndex, -1.0);
			if (!value.HasValue && control.Background != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Background");
				WriteElement(control.Background);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (!value2.HasValue && control.Foreground != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Foreground");
				WriteElement(control.Foreground);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (!value3.HasValue && control.BorderBrush != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.BorderBrush");
				WriteElement(control.BorderBrush);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			WriteCustomProperties(control, typeName, typeof(Control));
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(Glyphs glyphs, string typeName)
		{
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(System.Windows.Controls.Image image, string typeName)
		{
			if (image.Source is BitmapImage bitmapImage)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Source", bitmapImage.UriSource);
			}
			WriteAttribute("Stretch", image.Stretch, Stretch.Uniform);
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(MediaElement element, string typeName)
		{
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(TextBlock textBlock, string typeName)
		{
			if (TryParseTextValue(textBlock, out var value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Text", value);
			}
			WriteAttribute("FontFamily", textBlock.FontFamily, "Portable User Interface");
			WriteAttribute("FontSize", textBlock.FontSize, 14.666);
			WriteAttribute("FontStretch", textBlock.FontStretch, FontStretches.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("FontStyle", textBlock.FontStyle, FontStyles.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("FontWeight", textBlock.FontWeight, FontWeights.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("TextDecorations", textBlock.TextDecorations);
			WriteAttribute("TextWrapping", textBlock.TextWrapping, TextWrapping.NoWrap);
			if (TryParseColorValue(textBlock.Foreground, out var value2))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Foreground", value2.Value, Colors.Black);
			}
			else if (textBlock.Foreground != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Foreground");
				WriteElement(textBlock.Foreground);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (value != null || textBlock.Inlines == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (textBlock.Inlines.Count == 1 && textBlock.Inlines.FirstInline is Run)
			{
				writer.WriteString(((Run)textBlock.Inlines.FirstInline).Text);
				return;
			}
			foreach (Inline inline in textBlock.Inlines)
			{
				WriteElement(inline);
			}
		}

		private void WriteElement(Inline inline)
		{
			string arg = WriteStartElement(inline);
			string text = null;
			if (inline is Run run)
			{
				text = run.Text;
			}
			WriteAttribute("FontFamily", inline.FontFamily, "Portable User Interface");
			WriteAttribute("FontSize", inline.FontSize, 14.666);
			WriteAttribute("FontStretch", inline.FontStretch, FontStretches.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("FontStyle", inline.FontStyle, FontStyles.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("FontWeight", inline.FontWeight, FontWeights.Normal);
			WriteAttribute("TextDecorations", inline.TextDecorations);
			if (TryParseColorValue(inline.Foreground, out var value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Foreground", value.Value, Colors.Black);
				if (text != null)
				{
					writer.WriteString(text);
				}
			}
			else if (inline.Foreground != null)
			{
				if (text != null)
				{
					WriteAttribute("Text", text);
				}
				WriteStartElement($"{arg}.Foreground");
				WriteElement(inline.Foreground);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			WriteEndElement();
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(TextBox textBox, string typeName)
		{
			WriteCustomProperties(textBox, typeName, typeof(TextBox));
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(Panel panel, string typeName)
		{
			if (TryParseColorValue(panel.Background, out var value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Background", value.Value, DefaultColor);
			}
			if (panel is Canvas element)
			{
				WriteCustomProperties(element, typeName, typeof(Canvas));
			}
			else if (panel is Grid grid)
			{
				WriteAttribute("ShowGridLines", grid.ShowGridLines, defaultValue: false);
				WriteCustomProperties(grid, typeName, typeof(Grid));
				if (grid.ColumnDefinitions.Count > 0)
				{
					WriteStartElement("Grid.ColumnDefinitions");
					foreach (ColumnDefinition item in (IEnumerable<ColumnDefinition>)grid.ColumnDefinitions)
					{
						WriteStartElement("ColumnDefinition");
						WriteAttribute("Width", item.Width, new GridLength(1.0));
						WriteAttribute("MinWidth", item.MinWidth, 0.0);
						WriteAttribute("MaxWidth", item.MaxWidth, double.PositiveInfinity);
						WriteEndElement();
					}
					WriteEndElement();
				}
				if (grid.RowDefinitions.Count > 0)
				{
					WriteStartElement("Grid.RowDefinitions");
					foreach (RowDefinition item2 in (IEnumerable<RowDefinition>)grid.RowDefinitions)
					{
						WriteStartElement("RowDefinition");
						WriteAttribute("Height", item2.Height, new GridLength(1.0));
						WriteAttribute("MinHeight", item2.MinHeight, 0.0);
						WriteAttribute("MaxHeight", item2.MaxHeight, double.PositiveInfinity);
						WriteEndElement();
					}
					WriteEndElement();
				}
			}
			else if (panel is StackPanel stackPanel)
			{
				WriteAttribute("Orientation", stackPanel.Orientation, Orientation.Vertical);
				WriteCustomProperties(stackPanel, typeName, typeof(StackPanel));
			}
			else
			{
				WriteCustomProperties(panel, typeName, typeof(Panel));
			}
			if (!value.HasValue && panel.Background != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Background");
				WriteElement(panel.Background);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private void WriteInnerElement(Shape shape, string typeName)
		{
			if (TryParseColorValue(shape.Fill, out var value))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Fill", value.Value, DefaultColor);
			}
			if (TryParseColorValue(shape.Stroke, out var value2))
			{
				WriteAttribute("Stroke", value2.Value, DefaultColor);
			}
			WriteAttribute("StrokeDashArray", shape.StrokeDashArray);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeDashCap", shape.StrokeDashCap, PenLineCap.Flat);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeDashOffset", shape.StrokeDashOffset, 0.0);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeEndLineCap", shape.StrokeEndLineCap, PenLineCap.Flat);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeLineJoin", shape.StrokeLineJoin, PenLineJoin.Miter);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeMiterLimit", shape.StrokeMiterLimit, 10.0);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeStartLineCap", shape.StrokeStartLineCap, PenLineCap.Flat);
			WriteAttribute("StrokeThickness", shape.StrokeThickness, 1.0);
			WriteAttribute("Stretch", shape.Stretch, Stretch.None);
			if (!(shape is Ellipse))
			{
				if (shape is Line line)
				{
					WriteAttribute("X1", line.X1, 0.0);
					WriteAttribute("Y1", line.Y1, 0.0);
					WriteAttribute("X2", line.X2, 0.0);
					WriteAttribute("Y2", line.Y2, 0.0);
				}
				else if (shape is System.Windows.Shapes.Path path)
				{
					WriteStartElement("Path.Data");
					WriteElement(path.Data);
					WriteEndElement();
				}
				else if (shape is Polygon polygon)
				{
					WriteAttribute("FillRule", polygon.FillRule, FillRule.EvenOdd);
					WriteAttribute("Points", polygon.Points);
				}
				else if (shape is Polyline polyline)
				{
					WriteAttribute("FillRule", polyline.FillRule, FillRule.EvenOdd);
					WriteAttribute("Points", polyline.Points);
				}
				else if (shape is System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle)
				{
					WriteAttribute("RadiusX", rectangle.RadiusX, 0.0);
					WriteAttribute("RadiusY", rectangle.RadiusY, 0.0);
				}
			}
			if (!value.HasValue && shape.Fill != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Fill");
				WriteElement(shape.Fill);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
			if (!value2.HasValue && shape.Stroke != null)
			{
				WriteStartElement($"{typeName}.Stroke");
				WriteElement(shape.Stroke);
				WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private void WriteCustomProperties(FrameworkElement element, string typeName, Type baseType)
		{
			IsDerivableCoreType(element.GetType());
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, string value)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value);
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, string value, string defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value != defaultValue)
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value);
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string prefix, string name, string ns, string value)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(prefix, name, ns, value);
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, double value, double defaultValue)
		{
			if (!double.IsInfinity(value) && !double.IsNaN(value) && (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value != defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, DoubleCollection value)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || (value != null && value.Count > 0))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, Enum value, Enum defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, bool value, bool defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value != defaultValue)
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, System.Windows.Point value, System.Windows.Point defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, PointCollection value)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || (value != null && value.Count > 0))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, Rect value, Rect defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, System.Windows.Size value, System.Windows.Size defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());

		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, Thickness value, Thickness defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, CornerRadius value, CornerRadius defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, GridLength value, GridLength defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, Cursor value, Cursor defaultValue)
		{
			if (value != null && (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value != defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, System.Windows.Media.FontFamily value, string defaultValue)
		{
			if (value != null && (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value.Format() != defaultValue))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, FontStretch value, FontStretch defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))

				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, System.Windows.FontStyle value, System.Windows.FontStyle defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || !value.Equals(defaultValue))

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, FontWeight value, FontWeight defaultValue)
		{

			}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, TextDecorationCollection value)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value != null)
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, System.Windows.Media.Color value, System.Windows.Media.Color defaultValue)
		{
			if (settings.WriteDefaultValues || value != defaultValue)
			{
				WriteAttribute(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		protected void WriteAttribute(string name, Uri value)
		{
			if (value != null)
			{
				WriteAttribute(name, value.Format());
			}
		}

		private static bool TryParseColorValue(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush, out System.Windows.Media.Color? value)
		{
			value = null;
			bool result = false;
			if (brush is SolidColorBrush { Opacity: 1.0, RelativeTransform: null, Transform: null } solidColorBrush)
			{
				value = solidColorBrush.Color;
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool TryParseTextValue(TextBlock textBlock, out string value)
		{
			value = null;
			bool result = false;
			if (textBlock.Inlines != null && textBlock.Inlines.Count == 1 && textBlock.Inlines.FirstInline is Run run && run.Text == textBlock.Text && run.Text.IndexOf('\n') == -1)
			{
				value = run.Text;
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void WriteComment(string comment, params object[] o)
		{
			writer.WriteComment(" " + string.Format(comment, o) + " ");
		}

		private static void WriteException(StringBuilder b, Exception e)
		{
			b.AppendLine();
			b.AppendLine("<!--");
			b.AppendLine("Error while writing XAML. Details:");
			b.AppendLine(e.ToString());
			b.AppendLine("-->");
		}

		private static bool IsDerivableCoreType(Type type)
		{
			if (type == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("type");
			}
			if (!(type == typeof(Control)) && !(type == typeof(UserControl)) && !(type == typeof(ItemsControl)) && !(type == typeof(TextBox)) && !(type == typeof(Panel)) && !(type == typeof(Canvas)) && !(type == typeof(Grid)))
			{
				return type == typeof(StackPanel);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static string Save(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			return Save(element, new XamlWriterSettings());
		}

		public static string Save(FrameworkElement element, XamlWriterSettings settings)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			try
			{
				using XamlWriter xamlWriter = CreateWriter(stringBuilder, newLineOnAttributes: false, settings);
				xamlWriter.WriteElement(element);
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				WriteException(stringBuilder, e);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string Save(object obj)
		{
			return Save(obj, new XamlWriterSettings());
		}

		public static string Save(object obj, XamlWriterSettings settings)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("obj");
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			try
			{
				using XamlWriter xamlWriter = CreateWriter(stringBuilder, newLineOnAttributes: false, settings);
				xamlWriter.WriteElement(obj);
			}
			catch (Exception e)
			{
				WriteException(stringBuilder, e);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static XamlWriter CreateWriter(StringBuilder output, bool newLineOnAttributes, XamlWriterSettings settings)
		{
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			xmlWriterSettings.NewLineOnAttributes = newLineOnAttributes;
			xmlWriterSettings.ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Fragment;
			return new XamlWriter(XmlWriter.Create(output, xmlWriterSettings), settings);
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls
{
	public class PropertyValue : IPropertyValue, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private object _value;

		private string _property;

		public string Property => _property;

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_value != value)
				{
					_value = value;
					this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(Property));
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public PropertyValue(string property, object value)
		{
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			}
			_property = property;
			_value = value;
		}
	}
	public class PropertyValueCollection : IPropertyValueCollection, ICollection<IPropertyValue>, IEnumerable<IPropertyValue>, IEnumerable, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private Dictionary<string, IPropertyValue> _entries = new Dictionary<string, IPropertyValue>();

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _propertyChanged;

		public int Count => _entries.Count;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public IList<string> PropertyNames => new List<string>(_entries.Keys);

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public PropertyValueCollection()
		{
			_propertyChanged = PropertyValueChanged;
		}

		public PropertyValueCollection(IEnumerable<IPropertyValue> collection)
			: this()
		{
			foreach (IPropertyValue item in collection)
			{
				Add(item);
			}
		}

		public PropertyValueCollection(params IPropertyValue[] collection)
			: this()
		{
			foreach (IPropertyValue item in collection)
			{
				Add(item);
			}
		}

		private void PropertyValueChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
		}

		public object Value(string propertyName)
		{
			if (!_entries.TryGetValue(propertyName, out var value))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return value.Value;
		}

		public T Value<T>(string propertyName) where T : class
		{
			return Value(propertyName) as T;
		}

		public bool Contains(string propertyName)
		{
			return _entries.ContainsKey(propertyName);
		}

		public bool Remove(string propertyName)
		{
			return Remove(_entries[propertyName]);
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string name)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
		}

		public void Add(IPropertyValue item)
		{
			_entries.Add(item.Property, item);
			item.PropertyChanged += _propertyChanged;
			RaisePropertyChanged(item.Property);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			using (IEnumerator<IPropertyValue> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					IPropertyValue current = enumerator.Current;
					if (!list.Contains(current.Property))
					{
						list.Add(current.Property);
					}
					current.PropertyChanged -= _propertyChanged;
				}
			}
			_entries.Clear();
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged(item);
			}
		}

		public bool Contains(IPropertyValue item)
		{
			return _entries.ContainsKey(item.Property);
		}

		public void CopyTo(IPropertyValue[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			_entries.Values.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
		}

		public bool Remove(IPropertyValue item)
		{
			if (item == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool num = _entries.Remove(item.Property);
			if (num)
			{
				item.PropertyChanged -= _propertyChanged;
				RaisePropertyChanged(item.Property);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public IEnumerator<IPropertyValue> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return _entries.Values.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return ((IEnumerable)_entries).GetEnumerator();
		}
	}
	public class StateCollection : ObservableCollection<string>, IStateCollection, IList<string>, ICollection<string>, IEnumerable<string>, IEnumerable, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private EventHandler<StatePropertyChangedEventArgs> _statePropertyChangedHandler;

		public IStatePropertyValuesDictionary Index { get; private set; }

		public StateCollection(IStatePropertyValuesDictionary index)
		{
			Index = index;
			if (Index != null)
			{
				if (_statePropertyChangedHandler == null)
				{
					_statePropertyChangedHandler = Index_StatePropertyChanged;
				}
				Index.StatePropertyChanged += _statePropertyChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private void Index_StatePropertyChanged(object sender, StatePropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Contains(e.StateName))
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged(e.PropertyName);
			}
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		public object Value(string propertyName)
		{
			for (int num = base.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				IPropertyValueCollection propertyValueCollection = Index[base[num]];
				if (propertyValueCollection != null)
				{
					object obj = propertyValueCollection.Value(propertyName);
					if (obj != null)
					{
						return obj;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public T Value<T>(string propertyName) where T : class
		{
			return Value(propertyName) as T;
		}

		public IEnumerable Values(string propertyName)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				IPropertyValueCollection propertyValueCollection = Index[current];
				if (propertyValueCollection != null && propertyValueCollection.Contains(propertyName))
				{
					list.Add(propertyValueCollection.Value(propertyName));
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public IEnumerable<T> Values<T>(string propertyName) where T : class
		{
			List<T> list = new List<T>();
			using IEnumerator<string> enumerator = GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				string current = enumerator.Current;
				IPropertyValueCollection propertyValueCollection = Index[current];
				if (propertyValueCollection != null && propertyValueCollection.Contains(propertyName))
				{
					object obj = propertyValueCollection.Value(propertyName);
					if (obj is T)
					{
						list.Add((T)obj);
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
			{
				foreach (object newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					if (!Index.ContainsKey(newItem.ToString()))
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (string propertyName in Index[newItem.ToString()].PropertyNames)
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
				foreach (object oldItem in e.OldItems)
				{
					if (!Index.ContainsKey(oldItem.ToString()))
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (string propertyName2 in Index[oldItem.ToString()].PropertyNames)
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName2);
					}
				}
				{
					foreach (object newItem2 in e.NewItems)
					{
						if (!Index.ContainsKey(newItem2.ToString()))
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (string propertyName3 in Index[newItem2.ToString()].PropertyNames)
						{
							RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName3);
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
			{
				foreach (object oldItem2 in e.OldItems)
				{
					if (!Index.ContainsKey(oldItem2.ToString()))
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (string propertyName4 in Index[oldItem2.ToString()].PropertyNames)
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName4);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			}
			foreach (object key in ((IDictionary)Index).Keys)
			{
				foreach (string propertyName5 in (Index[key] as IPropertyValueCollection).PropertyNames)
				{
					RaisePropertyChanged(propertyName5);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class StateManager : IStateManager, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private Dictionary<string, object> _cache = new Dictionary<string, object>();

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _propertyChangedHander;

		private IStateManager _parent;

		public IStateCollection States { get; private set; }

		public IStatePropertyValuesDictionary StatePropertyValues { get; private set; }

		public IStateManager Parent
		{
			get
			{
				return _parent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_parent != value)
				{
					if (_parent != null)
					{
						_parent.PropertyChanged -= _propertyChangedHander;
					}
					_parent = value;
					if (_parent != null)
					{
						StatePropertyValues = _parent.StatePropertyValues;
						_parent.PropertyChanged += _propertyChangedHander;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public StateManager(IStatePropertyValuesDictionary statePropertyValues)
		{
			_propertyChangedHander = StateList_PropertyChanged;
			StatePropertyValues = statePropertyValues;
			Init();
		}

		public StateManager(IStateManager parent)
		{
			_propertyChangedHander = StateList_PropertyChanged;
			Parent = parent;
			Init();
		}

		private void Init()
		{
			States = new StateCollection(StatePropertyValues);
			States.PropertyChanged += _propertyChangedHander;
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

		private void StateList_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_cache.TryGetValue(e.PropertyName, out var value))
			{
				object obj = NonCachedValue(e.PropertyName);
				_cache[e.PropertyName] = obj;
				if (obj != value)
				{
					RaisePropertyChanged(e);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged(e);
			}
		}

		public object Value(string propertyName)
		{
			if (_cache.TryGetValue(propertyName, out var value) && value != null)
			{
				return value;
			}
			return NonCachedValue(propertyName);
		}

		private object NonCachedValue(string propertyName)
		{
			object obj = null;
			for (IStateManager stateManager = this; stateManager != null; stateManager = stateManager.Parent)
			{
				obj = stateManager.States.Value(propertyName);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					_cache[propertyName] = obj;
					return obj;
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public T Value<T>(string propertyName) where T : class
		{
			return Value(propertyName) as T;
		}

		public IEnumerable Values(string propertyName)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			for (IStateManager stateManager = this; stateManager != null; stateManager = stateManager.Parent)
			{
				list.InsertRange(0, stateManager.States.Values<object>(propertyName));
			}
			return list;
		}

		public IEnumerable<T> Values<T>(string propertyName) where T : class
		{
			List<T> list = new List<T>();
			for (IStateManager stateManager = this; stateManager != null; stateManager = stateManager.Parent)
			{
				list.InsertRange(0, stateManager.States.Values<T>(propertyName));
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class StatePropertyValuesDictionary : IStatePropertyValuesDictionary, INotifyStatePropertyChanged, IDictionary<string, IPropertyValueCollection>, ICollection<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>>, IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>>, IEnumerable, IOrderedDictionary, IDictionary, ICollection
	{
		private List<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>> _entries = new List<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>>();

		private Dictionary<string, IPropertyValueCollection> _entriesDict = new Dictionary<string, IPropertyValueCollection>();

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _propertyValuesChanged;

		public ICollection<string> Keys => GetKeys();

		public ICollection<IPropertyValueCollection> Values => GetValues();

		public IPropertyValueCollection this[string key]
		{
			get
			{
				return _entriesDict[key];
			}
			set
			{
				int num = Find(key);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					InternalAdd(new KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>(key, value));
					return;
				}
				this[num] = value;
				_entriesDict[key] = value;
			}
		}

		public int Count => _entries.Count;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public object this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				if (index < 0 || index >= Count)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _entries[index].Value;
			}
			set
			{
				KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> keyValuePair = _entries[index];
				IList<string> propertyNames = keyValuePair.Value.PropertyNames;
				IPropertyValueCollection propertyValueCollection = (IPropertyValueCollection)value;
				_entries[index] = new KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>(keyValuePair.Key, propertyValueCollection);
				_entriesDict[keyValuePair.Key] = propertyValueCollection;
				if (keyValuePair.Value != propertyValueCollection)
				{
					keyValuePair.Value.PropertyChanged -= _propertyValuesChanged;
					propertyValueCollection.PropertyChanged += _propertyValuesChanged;
				}
				foreach (string propertyName in propertyValueCollection.PropertyNames)
				{
					if (!propertyNames.Contains(propertyName))
					{
						propertyNames.Add(propertyName);
					}
				}
				RaiseStatePropertyChanged(keyValuePair.Key, propertyNames);
			}
		}

		public bool IsFixedSize => false;

		ICollection IDictionary.Keys => GetKeys();

		ICollection IDictionary.Values => GetValues();

		public object this[object key]
		{
			get
			{
				return this[(string)key];
			}
			set
			{
				this[(string)key] = (PropertyValueCollection)value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSynchronized => ((ICollection)_entries).IsSynchronized;

		public object SyncRoot => ((ICollection)_entries).SyncRoot;

		bool IDictionary.IsFixedSize => IsFixedSize;

		bool IDictionary.IsReadOnly => IsReadOnly;

		object IDictionary.this[object key]
		{
			get
			{
				return this[(string)key];
			}
			set
			{
				this[(string)key] = (PropertyValueCollection)value;
			}
		}

		int ICollection.Count => _entries.Count;

		bool ICollection.IsSynchronized => ((ICollection)_entries).IsSynchronized;

		object ICollection.SyncRoot => ((ICollection)_entries).SyncRoot;

		public event EventHandler<StatePropertyChangedEventArgs> StatePropertyChanged;

		public StatePropertyValuesDictionary()
		{
			_propertyValuesChanged = PropertyValuesChanged;
		}

		private void PropertyValuesChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> entry in _entries)
			{
				if (entry.Value == sender)
				{
					RaiseStatePropertyChanged(entry.Key, e.PropertyName);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RaiseStatePropertyChanged(string attribute, string property)
		{
			this.StatePropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new StatePropertyChangedEventArgs(attribute, property));
		}

		private void RaiseStatePropertyChanged(string attribute, IEnumerable<string> properties)
		{
			EventHandler<StatePropertyChangedEventArgs> eventHandler = this.StatePropertyChanged;
			if (eventHandler == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string property in properties)
			{
				eventHandler(this, new StatePropertyChangedEventArgs(attribute, property));
			}
		}

		private int Find(string key)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _entries.Count; i++)
			{
				if (string.Equals(_entries[i].Key, key))
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		private List<string> GetKeys()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> entry in _entries)
			{
				list.Add(entry.Key);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private List<IPropertyValueCollection> GetValues()
		{
			List<IPropertyValueCollection> list = new List<IPropertyValueCollection>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> entry in _entries)
			{
				list.Add(entry.Value);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void InternalAdd(KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item)
		{
			_entries.Add(item);
			_entriesDict[item.Key] = item.Value;
			item.Value.PropertyChanged += _propertyValuesChanged;
			RaiseStatePropertyChanged(item.Key, item.Value.PropertyNames);
		}

		private bool InternalRemove(KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item)
		{
			if (_entries.Remove(item))
			{
				_entriesDict.Remove(item.Key);
				item.Value.PropertyChanged -= _propertyValuesChanged;
				RaiseStatePropertyChanged(item.Key, item.Value.PropertyNames);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void InternalInsert(int index, string key, IPropertyValueCollection value)
		{
			_entries.Insert(index, new KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>(key, value));
			_entriesDict[key] = value;
			value.PropertyChanged += _propertyValuesChanged;
			RaiseStatePropertyChanged(key, value.PropertyNames);
		}

		public void Add(string key, IPropertyValueCollection value)
		{
			InternalAdd(new KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>(key, value));
		}

		public bool ContainsKey(string key)
		{
			return _entriesDict.ContainsKey(key);
		}

		public bool Remove(string key)
		{
			int num = Find(key);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item = _entries[num];
			InternalRemove(item);
			return true;
		}

		public bool TryGetValue(string key, out IPropertyValueCollection value)
		{
			return _entriesDict.TryGetValue(key, out value);
		}

		public void Add(KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item)
		{
			InternalAdd(item);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			List<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>> entries = _entries;
			_entries = new List<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>>();
			_entriesDict = new Dictionary<string, IPropertyValueCollection>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item in entries)
			{
				RaiseStatePropertyChanged(item.Key, item.Value.PropertyNames);
			}
		}

		public bool Contains(KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item)
		{
			return _entries.Contains(item);
		}

		public void CopyTo(KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			_entries.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
		}

		public bool Remove(KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> item)
		{
			return InternalRemove(item);
		}

		public IEnumerator<KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return _entries.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return _entries.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IDictionaryEnumerator IOrderedDictionary.GetEnumerator()
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public void Insert(int index, object key, object value)
		{
			InternalInsert(index, (string)key, (IPropertyValueCollection)value);
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> keyValuePair = _entries[index];
			_entries.RemoveAt(index);
			_entriesDict.Remove(keyValuePair.Key);
			RaiseStatePropertyChanged(keyValuePair.Key, keyValuePair.Value.PropertyNames);
		}

		public void Add(object key, object value)
		{
			InternalAdd(new KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection>((string)key, (PropertyValueCollection)value));
		}

		public bool Contains(object key)
		{
			return Contains((string)key);
		}

		IDictionaryEnumerator IDictionary.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return ((IDictionary)_entries).GetEnumerator();
		}

		public void Remove(object key)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPropertyValueCollection> entry in _entries)
			{
				if (object.Equals(entry.Key, key))
				{
					InternalRemove(entry);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void CopyTo(Array array, int index)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _entries.Count; i++)
			{
				array.SetValue(_entries[i].Value, i + index);
			}
		}

		void IDictionary.Add(object key, object value)
		{
			Add((string)key, (PropertyValueCollection)value);
		}

		void IDictionary.Clear()
		{
			Clear();
		}

		bool IDictionary.Contains(object key)
		{
			return Contains((string)key);
		}

		void IDictionary.Remove(object key)
		{
			Remove((string)key);
		}

		void ICollection.CopyTo(Array array, int index)
		{
			CopyTo(array, index);
		}
	}

			: base(propertyName)

			StateName = stateName;
		}

	public delegate object ObjectConverter(object actualValue, DependencyProperty property);
	public class StateModelViewModelSupport
	{
		private DependencyObject _viewModel;

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _modelPropertyChanged;

		private ISupportsStateManager _model;

		private Dictionary<string, DependencyProperty> _properties = new Dictionary<string, DependencyProperty>();

		private Dictionary<string, string> _propertyMaps = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private string _globalUpdate;

		public ISupportsStateManager Model
		{
			get
			{
				return _model;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_model == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_model != null)
				{
					if (_model is INotifyPropertyChanged notifyPropertyChanged)
					{
						notifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged -= _modelPropertyChanged;
					}
					if (_model.StateManager != null)
					{
						_model.StateManager.PropertyChanged -= _modelPropertyChanged;
					}
				}
				_model = value;
				if (_model != null)
				{
					if (_model is INotifyPropertyChanged notifyPropertyChanged2)
					{
						notifyPropertyChanged2.PropertyChanged += _modelPropertyChanged;
					}
					if (_model.StateManager != null)
					{
						_model.StateManager.PropertyChanged += _modelPropertyChanged;
					}
					UpdateProperties();
				}
			}
		}

		public ObjectConverter ObjectConverter { get; set; }

		public StateModelViewModelSupport(DependencyObject viewModel, params DependencyProperty[] linkedProperties)
		{
			_viewModel = viewModel;
			_modelPropertyChanged = ModelPropertyChanged;
			AddLinkedProperties(linkedProperties);
		}

		public void AddLinkedProperties(params DependencyProperty[] linkedProperties)
		{
			if (linkedProperties != null)
			{
				foreach (DependencyProperty dependencyProperty in linkedProperties)
				{
					_properties.Add(dependencyProperty.Name, dependencyProperty);
				}
			}
			UpdateProperties();
		}

		public StateModelViewModelSupport(DependencyObject viewModel, string globalUpdatePropertyName, params DependencyProperty[] linkedProperties)
			: this(viewModel, linkedProperties)
		{
			_globalUpdate = globalUpdatePropertyName;
		}

		private void ModelPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			DependencyProperty value;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_globalUpdate) && e.PropertyName == _globalUpdate)
			{
				UpdateProperties();
			}
			else if (_properties.TryGetValue(e.PropertyName, out value) || (_propertyMaps.ContainsKey(e.PropertyName) && _properties.TryGetValue(_propertyMaps[e.PropertyName], out value)))
			{
				UpdateProperty(e.PropertyName, value);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateProperties()
		{
			if (_model == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, DependencyProperty> property in _properties)
			{
				UpdateProperty(property.Key, property.Value);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateProperty(string key, DependencyProperty property)
		{
			if (_model == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			object obj = GetValue(key);
			if (obj == null && _propertyMaps.TryGetValue(key, out key))
			{
				obj = GetValue(key);
			}
			if (obj != null)
			{
				if (obj.GetType() != property.PropertyType && ObjectConverter != null)
				{
					obj = ObjectConverter(obj, property);
				}
				_viewModel.SetValue(property, obj);
			}
			else
			{
				_viewModel.SetValue(property, property.GetMetadata(_viewModel).DefaultValue);
			}
		}

		private object GetValue(string key)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (_model.StateManager != null)
			{
				obj = _model.StateManager.Value<object>(key);
			}
			if (obj == null)
			{
				obj = _model.LocalValue(key);
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public void AddLinkedProperty(string modelPropertyName, DependencyProperty viewModelProperty)
		{
			_properties.Add(viewModelProperty.Name, viewModelProperty);
			if (!string.Equals(viewModelProperty.Name, modelPropertyName))
			{
				_propertyMaps[modelPropertyName] = viewModelProperty.Name;
				_propertyMaps[viewModelProperty.Name] = modelPropertyName;
			}
			UpdateProperty(modelPropertyName, viewModelProperty);
		}
	}
}

		}

	public interface IActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem> : IList<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, ICollection<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, IEnumerable<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, IEnumerable

	public interface IActionMenuCollection : IList<IActionMenu>, ICollection<IActionMenu>, IEnumerable<IActionMenu>, IEnumerable

	}
	public class ActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem> : List<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, IActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem>, IList<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, ICollection<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, IEnumerable<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>>, IEnumerable, IActionMenuCollection, IList<IActionMenu>, ICollection<IActionMenu>, IEnumerable<IActionMenu>
	{
		IActionMenu IList<IActionMenu>.this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return base[index];
			}
			set
			{
				base[index] = value as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>;
			}
		}

		bool ICollection<IActionMenu>.IsReadOnly => ((IList)this).IsReadOnly;

		int IList<IActionMenu>.IndexOf(IActionMenu item)
		{
			return IndexOf(item as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>);
		}

		void IList<IActionMenu>.Insert(int index, IActionMenu item)
		{
			Insert(index, item as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>);
		}

		void IList<IActionMenu>.RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		void ICollection<IActionMenu>.Add(IActionMenu item)
		{
			Add(item as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>);
		}

		bool ICollection<IActionMenu>.Contains(IActionMenu item)
		{
			return Contains(item as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>);
		}

		void ICollection<IActionMenu>.CopyTo(IActionMenu[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			CopyTo(array as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>[], arrayIndex);
		}

		bool ICollection<IActionMenu>.Remove(IActionMenu item)
		{
			return Remove(item as ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>);
		}

		IEnumerator<IActionMenu> IEnumerable<IActionMenu>.GetEnumerator()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < base.Count; i++)
			{
				yield return base[i];
			}
		}
	}
	public class GetActionsEventArgs<TSelectedItem> : EventArgs
	{
		private IActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem> _dictionary;

		private IList<TSelectedItem> _selected;

		public IActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem> Actions
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dictionary == null)
				{
					_dictionary = new ActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem>();
				}
				return _dictionary;
			}
		}

		public bool AnyActions
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dictionary != null)
				{
					return _dictionary.Count > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public IList<TSelectedItem> Selected => _selected;

		public GetActionsEventArgs(IList<TSelectedItem> selected)
		{
			_selected = selected ?? new SelectedItemCollection<TSelectedItem>();
		}

		public GetActionsEventArgs()
			: this((IList<TSelectedItem>)null)
		{
		}

		public GetActionsEventArgs(TSelectedItem selected)
			: this((IList<TSelectedItem>)new SelectedItemCollection<TSelectedItem>(selected))
		{
		}
	}
	public class DoActionEventArgs<TSelectedItem> : EventArgs
	{
		private IList<TSelectedItem> _selected;

		private Rect _area;

		private ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> _menu;

		public IList<TSelectedItem> Selected => _selected;

		public Rect ScreenArea => _area;

		public ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> Menu => _menu;

		public DoActionEventArgs(Rect screenArea)
			: this((ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>)null, (IList<TSelectedItem>)null, screenArea)
		{
		}

		public DoActionEventArgs(IList<TSelectedItem> selected, Rect screenArea)
			: this((ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>)null, selected, screenArea)
		{
		}

		public DoActionEventArgs(TSelectedItem selected, Rect screenArea)
			: this((ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>)null, (IList<TSelectedItem>)new SelectedItemCollection<TSelectedItem>(selected), screenArea)
		{
		}

		public DoActionEventArgs(ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> menu, IList<TSelectedItem> selected, Rect screenArea)
		{
			_menu = menu;
			_selected = selected;
			_area = screenArea;
		}

		public DoActionEventArgs(ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> menu, TSelectedItem selected, Rect screenArea)
			: this(menu, (IList<TSelectedItem>)new SelectedItemCollection<TSelectedItem>(selected), screenArea)
		{
		}
	}

	public interface IActionMenu
	{
		string Text { get; }

		string Id { get; }

		ImageSource Image { get; }

		bool Enabled { get; }

		bool IsChecked { get; }

		bool HasSubitems { get; }

		IActionMenuCollection Subitems { get; }

		void InvokeHandler(object sender, IList selection, Rect screenArea);
	}
	public class ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> : IActionMenu
	{
		private string _caption;

		private string _id;

		private ImageSource _image;

		private ActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem> _subItems;

		private DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem> _handler;

		private bool _enabled;

		private bool _checked;

		private object _tag;

		public string Text => _caption;

		public string Id => _id;

		public ImageSource Image => _image;

		public bool HasSubitems
		{
			get
			{
				if (_subItems != null)
				{
					return _subItems.Count > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem> Handler => _handler;

		public bool Enabled
		{
			get
			{
				return _enabled;
			}
			set
			{
				_enabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _checked;
			}
			set
			{
				_checked = value;
			}
		}

		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return _tag;
			}
			set
			{
				_tag = value;
			}
		}

		public IActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem> Subitems
		{
			get
			{
				if (_subItems == null)
				{
					_subItems = new ActionMenuCollection<TSelectedItem>();
				}
				return _subItems;
			}
		}

		IActionMenuCollection IActionMenu.Subitems => Subitems as IActionMenuCollection;

		public ActionMenu()
			: this((string)null, (string)null, (ImageSource)null, (DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem>)null, enabled: true)
		{
		}

		public ActionMenu(string text, string id)
			: this(text, id, (ImageSource)null, (DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem>)null, enabled: true)
		{
		}

		public ActionMenu(string text, string id, ImageSource image)
			: this(text, id, image, (DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem>)null, enabled: true)
		{
		}

		public ActionMenu(string text, string id, DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem> handler)
			: this(text, id, (ImageSource)null, handler, enabled: true)
		{
		}

		public ActionMenu(string text, string id, ImageSource image, DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem> handler)
			: this(text, id, image, handler, enabled: true)
		{
		}

		public ActionMenu(string text, string id, DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem> handler, bool enabled)
			: this(text, id, (ImageSource)null, handler, enabled)
		{
		}

		public ActionMenu(string text, string id, ImageSource image, DoActionEventHandler<TSelectedItem> handler, bool enabled)
		{
			_caption = text;
			_id = id;
			_image = image;
			_enabled = enabled;
			_handler = handler;
		}

		void IActionMenu.InvokeHandler(object sender, IList selection, Rect screenArea)
		{
			if (Handler != null)
			{
				Handler(sender, new DoActionEventArgs<TSelectedItem>(this, (IList<TSelectedItem>)selection, screenArea));
			}
		}
	}
	public static class ActionSupport<TSelectedItem>
	{
		public static ContextMenu BuildWpfContextMenu(IList<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>> menu, RoutedEventHandler handler)
		{
			ContextMenu contextMenu = new ContextMenu();
			AddMenus(contextMenu.Items, menu, handler);
			return contextMenu;
		}

		public static ContextMenu BuildWpfContextMenu(IList<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>> menu, IList<TSelectedItem> selection, Rect screenArea)
		{
			return BuildWpfContextMenu(menu, delegate(object menuSender, RoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (args.Source is MenuItem { Tag: ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> { Handler: not null } tag })
				{
					tag.Handler(menuSender, new DoActionEventArgs<TSelectedItem>(tag, selection, screenArea));
					args.Handled = true;
				}
			});
		}

		public static ContextMenu DoContextMenu(GetActionsEventHandler<TSelectedItem> getHandler, object sender, IList<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>> hostActions, IList<TSelectedItem> selection, Rect screenArea, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ContextMenu contextMenu = null;
			GetActionsEventArgs<TSelectedItem> e2 = new GetActionsEventArgs<TSelectedItem>(selection);
			if (hostActions != null && hostActions.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> hostAction in hostActions)
				{
					e2.Actions.Add(hostAction);
				}
			}
			if (getHandler != null)
			{
				if (e != null)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
				getHandler(sender, e2);
			}
			if (e2.AnyActions)
			{
				contextMenu = BuildWpfContextMenu(e2.Actions, selection, screenArea);
				contextMenu.Placement = PlacementMode.Absolute;
				contextMenu.HorizontalOffset = screenArea.Left;
				contextMenu.VerticalOffset = screenArea.Top;
				contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
				contextMenu.StaysOpen = true;
			}
			return contextMenu;
		}

		public static void AddToContextMenu(ItemCollection menu, GetActionsEventHandler<TSelectedItem> getHandler, object sender, IList<TSelectedItem> selection, Rect screenArea)
		{
			if (getHandler == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			GetActionsEventArgs<TSelectedItem> e = new GetActionsEventArgs<TSelectedItem>(selection);
			getHandler(sender, e);
			if (!e.AnyActions)
			{
				return;
			}
			AddMenus(menu, e.Actions, delegate(object menuSender, RoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (args.Source is MenuItem { Tag: ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> { Handler: not null } tag })
				{
					tag.Handler(sender, new DoActionEventArgs<TSelectedItem>(tag, selection, screenArea));
					args.Handled = true;
				}
			});
		}

		private static void AddMenus(ItemCollection items, IList<ActionMenu<TSelectedItem>> menuList, RoutedEventHandler handler)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (ActionMenu<TSelectedItem> menu in menuList)
			{
				if (menu == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(menu.Text) || menu.Text == "-")
				{
					if (items.Count > 0)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					items.Add(new Separator());
					flag = false;
				}
				MenuItem menuItem = new MenuItem
				{
					Icon = menu.Image,
					Header = menu.Text,
					IsEnabled = menu.Enabled,
					IsChecked = menu.IsChecked,
					Tag = menu
				};
				menuItem.Click += handler;
				items.Add(menuItem);
				if (menu.HasSubitems)
				{
					AddMenus(menuItem.Items, menu.Subitems, handler);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public enum AspenLanguageSelection
	{
		None,
		English,
		French,
		Italian,
		German,
		Spanish,
		Japanese,
		Korean,
		Russian,
		ChineseSimplified,
		PortugueseBrazilian
	}
	public static class AspenHelpers
	{
		public static string AspenLanguageSelectionString
		{
			get
			{
				List<Tuple<string, string>> obj = new List<Tuple<string, string>>
				{
					new Tuple<string, string>("HKEY_CURRENT_USER\\Software\\AspenTech\\Engineering\\Language", "CurrentLang"),
					new Tuple<string, string>("HKEY_CURRENT_USER\\Software\\AspenTech\\Engineering\\Language", string.Empty),
					new Tuple<string, string>("HKEY_CURRENT_USER\\Software\\Wow6432Node\\AspenTech\\Engineering\\Language", "CurrentLang"),
					new Tuple<string, string>("HKEY_CURRENT_USER\\Software\\Wow6432Node\\AspenTech\\Engineering\\Language", string.Empty)
				};
				string text = string.Empty;
				foreach (Tuple<string, string> item in obj)
				{
					text = Registry.GetValue(item.Item1, item.Item2, string.Empty) as string;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (text == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return text.ToUpperInvariant();
			}
		}

		public static AspenLanguageSelection AspenLanguageSelection
		{
			get
			{
				string aspenLanguageSelectionString = AspenLanguageSelectionString;
				AspenLanguageSelection result = AspenLanguageSelection.None;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(aspenLanguageSelectionString))
				{
					switch (aspenLanguageSelectionString.ToUpperInvariant())
					{
					case "ENG":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.English;
						break;
					case "FRA":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.French;
						break;
					case "ITA":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.Italian;
						break;
					case "DEU":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.German;
						break;
					case "SPA":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.Spanish;
						break;
					case "JPN":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.Japanese;
						break;
					case "CHS":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.ChineseSimplified;
						break;
					case "KOR":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.Korean;
						break;
					case "RUS":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.Russian;
						break;
					case "PTB":
						result = AspenLanguageSelection.PortugueseBrazilian;
						break;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public static void ApplyAspenLanguageSelection()
		{
			AspenLanguageSelection aspenLanguageSelection = AspenLanguageSelection;
			if (aspenLanguageSelection == AspenLanguageSelection.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = string.Empty;
			switch (aspenLanguageSelection)
			{
			case AspenLanguageSelection.English:
				text = "en-US";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.French:
				text = "fr-FR";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.Italian:
				text = "it-IT";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.German:
				text = "de-DE";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.Spanish:
				text = "es-ES";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.Japanese:
				text = "ja-JP";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.ChineseSimplified:
				text = "zh-Hans";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.Korean:
				text = "ko-KR";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.Russian:
				text = "ru-RU";
				break;
			case AspenLanguageSelection.PortugueseBrazilian:
				text = "pt-BR";
				break;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				CultureInfo cultureInfo = null;
				try
				{
					cultureInfo = new CultureInfo(CultureInfo.CreateSpecificCulture(text).Name, useUserOverride: true);
				}
				catch (CultureNotFoundException)
				{
				}
				if (cultureInfo != null)
				{
					Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = cultureInfo;
					Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentUICulture = cultureInfo;
				}
			}
		}
	}
