	[Flags]
	public enum ColorContrastSetStrategy
	{
		None = 0,
		NotBlackOrWhite = 1,
		NotGreyScale = 2,
		NotDark = 4,
		NotLight = 8,
		NotMidTone = 0x10,
		AlwaysAvoid = 0x20,
		HighContrast = 0x40
	}
	public interface IColorContrastSet
	{
		IList<System.Windows.Media.Color> Colours { get; }

		System.Windows.Media.Color Next();

		System.Windows.Media.Color Find(string item);

		void Reset();

		void Reset(ColorContrastSetStrategy strategy);

		void Reset(ColorContrastSetStrategy strategy, params System.Windows.Media.Color[] avoid);
	}
	public class ColorContrastSet : IColorContrastSet
	{
		private static readonly byte[] _colourPoints = new byte[9] { 0, 255, 128, 64, 192, 96, 224, 32, 160 };

		private const byte LOW_LIMIT = 48;

		private const byte HIGH_LIMIT = 208;

		private const byte MID_LOW_LIMIT = 80;

		private const byte MID_HIGH_LIMIT = 176;

		private const int AVOID_LIMIT = 1024;

		private List<System.Windows.Media.Color> _colours = new List<System.Windows.Media.Color>();

		private ColorContrastSetStrategy _strategy;

		private System.Windows.Media.Color[] _avoid;

		private Dictionary<string, System.Windows.Media.Color> _cache;

		public IList<System.Windows.Media.Color> Colours
		{
			get
			{
				if (_colours.Count == 0)
				{
					BuildColours();
				}
				return _colours;
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Color Find(string item)
		{
			if (_cache == null)
			{
				_cache = new Dictionary<string, System.Windows.Media.Color>();
			}
			if (!_cache.TryGetValue(item, out var value))
			{
				value = Next();
				_cache.Add(item, value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Color Next()
		{
			if (_colours.Count == 0)
			{
				BuildColours();
			}
			IList<System.Windows.Media.Color> colours = Colours;
			System.Windows.Media.Color result = colours[0];
			colours.RemoveAt(0);
			return result;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			BuildColours();
			_cache = null;
		}

		public void Reset(ColorContrastSetStrategy strategy)
		{
			_strategy = strategy;
			_avoid = null;
			Reset();
		}

		public void Reset(ColorContrastSetStrategy strategy, params System.Windows.Media.Color[] avoid)
		{
			_strategy = strategy;
			_avoid = avoid;
			Reset();
		}

		public ColorContrastSet(ColorContrastSetStrategy strategy)
		{
			Reset(strategy);
		}

		public ColorContrastSet(ColorContrastSetStrategy strategy, params System.Windows.Media.Color[] avoid)
		{
			Reset(strategy, avoid);
		}

		private void BuildColours()
		{
			bool flag = (_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.NotBlackOrWhite) != 0;
			bool flag2 = (_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.NotGreyScale) != 0;
			bool flag3 = (_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.NotDark) != 0;
			bool flag4 = (_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.NotLight) != 0;
			bool flag5 = (_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.NotMidTone) != 0;
			bool flag6 = (_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.AlwaysAvoid) != ColorContrastSetStrategy.None && _avoid != null && _avoid.Length != 0;
			_colours.Clear();
			int num = _colourPoints.Length - 1;
			if ((_strategy & ColorContrastSetStrategy.HighContrast) != ColorContrastSetStrategy.None)
			{
				num -= 4;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
			{
				byte b = _colourPoints[i];
				for (int j = 0; j <= num; j++)
				{
					byte b2 = _colourPoints[j];
					for (int k = 0; k <= num; k++)
					{
						byte b3 = _colourPoints[k];
						if ((flag && (b == 0 || b == byte.MaxValue) && b == b2 && b == b3) || (flag2 && b == b2 && b == b3) || (flag3 && b < 48 && b2 < 48 && b3 < 48) || (flag4 && b > 208 && b2 > 208 && b3 > 208) || (flag5 && b > 80 && b < 176 && b2 > 80 && b2 < 176 && b3 > 80 && b3 < 176))
						{
							continue;
						}
						System.Windows.Media.Color color = System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, b, b2, b3);
						if (flag6)
						{
							bool flag7 = false;
							System.Windows.Media.Color[] avoid = _avoid;
							foreach (System.Windows.Media.Color b4 in avoid)
							{
								if (Distance(color, b4) <= 1024)
								{
									flag7 = true;
									break;
								}
							}
							if (flag7)
							{
								continue;
							}
						}
						_colours.Add(color);
					}
				}
			}
			System.Windows.Media.Color color2 = ((_avoid == null || _avoid.Length < 1) ? Colors.Gray : _avoid[0]);
			System.Windows.Media.Color color3 = ((_avoid == null || _avoid.Length < 2) ? color2 : _avoid[1]);
			for (int m = 0; m < _colours.Count - 1; m++)
			{
				int num2 = -1;
				int num3 = int.MinValue;
				for (int n = m; n < _colours.Count; n++)
				{
					System.Windows.Media.Color b5 = _colours[n];
					int num4 = Distance(color2, b5) + Distance(color3, b5);
					if (num4 > num3)
					{
						num3 = num4;
						num2 = n;
					}
				}
				if (num3 <= 2048)
				{
					_colours.RemoveRange(m, _colours.Count - m);
					break;
				}
				if (num2 != m)
				{
					System.Windows.Media.Color value = _colours[m];
					_colours[m] = _colours[num2];
					_colours[num2] = value;
				}
				color2 = color3;
				color3 = _colours[m];
			}
			if (_colours.Count == 0)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("ColourContrastSet has no valid entries.");
			}
		}

		private int Distance(System.Windows.Media.Color a, System.Windows.Media.Color b)
		{
			return Sqr(a.R, b.R) + Sqr(a.G, b.G) + Sqr(a.B, b.B);
		}

		private int Sqr(byte a, byte b)
		{
			int num = a - b;
			return num * num;
		}
	}
	public class CommandCanExecuteEventManager : WeakEventManager
	{
		public static CommandCanExecuteEventManager CurrentManager
		{
			get
			{
				CommandCanExecuteEventManager commandCanExecuteEventManager = WeakEventManager.GetCurrentManager(typeof(CommandCanExecuteEventManager)) as CommandCanExecuteEventManager;
				if (commandCanExecuteEventManager == null)
				{
					commandCanExecuteEventManager = new CommandCanExecuteEventManager();
					WeakEventManager.SetCurrentManager(typeof(CommandCanExecuteEventManager), commandCanExecuteEventManager);
				}
				return commandCanExecuteEventManager;
			}
		}

		public static void AddListener(ICommand source, IWeakEventListener listener)
		{
			CurrentManager.ProtectedAddListener(source, listener);
		}

		public static void RemoveListener(ICommand source, IWeakEventListener listener)
		{
			CurrentManager.ProtectedRemoveListener(source, listener);
		}

		protected override void StartListening(object source)
		{
			if (source is ICommand command)
			{
				command.CanExecuteChanged += command_CanExecuteChanged;
			}
		}

		protected override void StopListening(object source)
		{
			if (source is ICommand command)
			{
				command.CanExecuteChanged -= command_CanExecuteChanged;
			}
		}

		private void command_CanExecuteChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			DeliverEvent(sender, e);
		}
	}

	[ValueConversion(typeof(object), typeof(UIElement))]
	public class ObjectUIElementConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			object result = value;
			if (value != null && targetType == typeof(UIElement) && (result = value as UIElement) == null)
			{
				string content = string.Format(culture, "{0}", value);
				result = new Label
				{
					Content = content,
					Padding = new Thickness(0.0)
				};
			}
			return result;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			object result = value;
			if (value != null && targetType == typeof(UIElement) && (result = value as UIElement) == null)
			{
				string content = string.Format(culture, "{0}", value);
				result = new Label
				{
					Content = content,
					Padding = new Thickness(0.0)
				};
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(Visibility), typeof(bool?))]
	public class VisibilityToBooleanConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return new BooleanToVisibilityConverter().ConvertBack(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return new BooleanToVisibilityConverter().Convert(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(Enum), typeof(bool?))]
	public class EnumConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == parameter || (value != null && parameter != null && value.ToString() == parameter.ToString()))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string[] names = Enum.GetNames(targetType);
			for (int i = 0; i < names.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = names[i];
				if (text == parameter || (parameter != null && text == parameter.ToString()))
				{
					return Enum.GetValues(targetType).GetValue(i);
				}
			}
			return value;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(string), typeof(bool?))]
	public class ConditionalConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value != null && parameter != null && value.ToString() == parameter.ToString();
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if ((bool?)value == true)
			{
				return parameter;
			}
			return Binding.DoNothing;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(string), typeof(bool?))]
	public class InverseConditionalConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value != null && parameter != null && value.ToString() != parameter.ToString();
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && (bool?)value == false)
			{
				return parameter;
			}
			return Binding.DoNothing;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(string), typeof(bool?))]
	public class GTEConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (double.TryParse(value.ToString(), out var result) && double.TryParse(parameter.ToString(), out var result2))
			{
				return result > result2;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (double.TryParse(parameter.ToString(), out var _) && (bool?)value == true)
			{
				return parameter;
			}
			return Binding.DoNothing;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(System.Windows.Media.Color))]
	public class BrushColourConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is System.Windows.Media.Color)
			{
				return new SolidColorBrush((System.Windows.Media.Color)value);
			}
			if (value is SolidColorBrush)
			{
				return ((SolidColorBrush)value).Color;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return Convert(value, targetType, parameter, culture);
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(string), typeof(Visibility))]
	public class TextVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (value == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ToString())) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	[Flags]
	public enum DateTimeStepElements
	{
		None = 0,
		Milliseconds = 1,
		Seconds = 2,
		Minutes = 4,
		Hours = 8,
		Days = 0x10,
		Months = 0x20,
		Years = 0x40,
		Centuries = 0x80
	}
	public class DateTimeStep
	{
		private DateTimeStepElements _element;

		private int _amount;

		private static int[] _steps7 = new int[6] { 1, 3, 7, 14, 28, 56 };

		private static int[] _steps10 = new int[12]
		{
			1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200, 500, 1000,
			2000, 5000
		};

		private static int[] _steps24 = new int[8] { 1, 2, 4, 6, 12, 24, 48, 96 };

		private static int[] _steps60 = new int[11]
		{
			1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 30, 60, 120, 240, 480,
			960
		};

		public DateTimeStepElements Unit => _element;

		public string Format => Unit switch
		{
			DateTimeStepElements.Centuries => "yyyy", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Years => "yy", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Months => "MMM", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Days => "dd", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Hours => "HH:mm", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Minutes => "mm:ss", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Seconds => "ss.f", 
			DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds => ".fff", 
			DateTimeStepElements.None => "", 
			_ => "g", 
		};

		public double Step => _amount;

		public TimeSpan ApproximateDuration
		{
			get
			{
				DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
				return Add(now) - now;
			}
		}

		public void Double()
		{
			int[] array = StepSizes(_element);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i] > _amount)
				{
					_amount = array[i];
					break;
				}
			}
			switch (_element)
			{
			case DateTimeStepElements.Years:
				if (_amount >= 100)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Centuries;
				}
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Months:
				if (_amount >= 10)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Years;
				}
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Days:
				if (_amount >= 100)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Months;
				}
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Hours:
				if (_amount >= 20)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Days;
				}
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Minutes:
				if (_amount >= 100)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Hours;
				}
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Seconds:
				if (_amount >= 100)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Minutes;
				}
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds:
				if (_amount >= 1000)
				{
					_amount = 1;
					_element = DateTimeStepElements.Seconds;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public void Halve()
		{
			int[] array = StepSizes(_element);
			for (int num = array.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (array[num] < _amount)
				{
					_amount = array[num];
					return;
				}
			}
			switch (_element)
			{
			case DateTimeStepElements.Centuries:
				_amount = 50;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Years;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Years:
				_amount = 5;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Months;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Months:
				_amount = 6;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Days;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Days:
				_amount = 10;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Hours;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Hours:
				_amount = 12;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Minutes;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Minutes:
				_amount = 30;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Seconds;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Seconds:
				_amount = 30;
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds:
				_amount = 500;
				break;
			}
		}

		public DateTime Add(DateTime value)
		{
			return Add(value, 1);
		}

		public DateTime Add(DateTime value, int numberSteps)
		{
			return _element switch
			{
				DateTimeStepElements.Centuries => value.AddYears(numberSteps * _amount * 100), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Years => value.AddYears(numberSteps * _amount), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Months => value.AddMonths(numberSteps * _amount), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Days => value.AddDays(numberSteps * _amount), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Hours => value.AddHours(numberSteps * _amount), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Minutes => value.AddMinutes(numberSteps * _amount), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Seconds => value.AddSeconds(numberSteps * _amount), 
				DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds => value.AddMilliseconds(numberSteps * _amount), 
				_ => throw new InvalidOperationException(), 
			};
		}

		public DateTime RoundUp(DateTime value)
		{
			DateTime dateTime = Round(value);
			if (dateTime < value)
			{
				return Add(dateTime);
			}
			return dateTime;
		}

		public DateTime RoundDown(DateTime value)
		{
			DateTime dateTime = Round(value);
			if (dateTime > value)
			{
				return Add(dateTime, -1);
			}
			return dateTime;
		}

		public DateTime Round(DateTime value)
		{
			int num = value.Year;
			int num2 = value.Month;
			int num3 = value.Day;
			int num4 = value.Hour;
			int num5 = value.Minute;
			int num6 = value.Second;
			int num7 = value.Millisecond;
			int amount = _amount;
			switch (_element)
			{
			case DateTimeStepElements.Centuries:
				num = (int)Math.Round((double)num / (100.0 * (double)amount)) * amount * 100;
				num2 = (num3 = 1);
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Years:
				num = (int)Math.Round((double)num / (double)amount) * amount;
				num2 = (num3 = 1);
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Months:
				num2 = (int)Math.Round((double)num2 / (double)amount) * amount;
				num3 = 1;
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Days:
				num3 = (int)Math.Round((double)num3 / (double)amount) * amount;
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Hours:
				num4 = (int)Math.Round((double)num4 / (double)amount) * amount;
				num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Minutes:
				num5 = (int)Math.Round((double)num5 / (double)amount) * amount;
				num6 = (num7 = 0);
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Seconds:
				num6 = (int)Math.Round((double)num6 / (double)amount) * amount;
				num7 = 0;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds:
				num7 = (int)Math.Round((double)num7 / (double)amount) * amount;
				break;
			}
			if (num > 1 || num2 > 1 || num3 > 1 || num4 > 0 || num5 > 0 || num6 > 0 || num7 > 0)
			{
				return new DateTime(num, 1, 1).AddMonths(num2 - 1).AddDays(num3 - 1).AddHours(num4)
					.AddMinutes(num5)
					.AddSeconds(num6)
					.AddMilliseconds(num7);
			}
			return DateTime.MinValue;
		}

		public DateTimeStep(DateTimeStepElements interval, int amount)
		{
			_element = interval;
			_amount = amount;
		}

		public DateTimeStep(TimeSpan duration, int divisor)
		{
			long num = duration.Milliseconds / divisor;
			if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365 * 100)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Centuries;
			}
			else if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Years;
			}
			else if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 20)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Months;
			}
			else if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Days;
			}
			else if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000 * 60 * 60)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Hours;
			}
			else if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000 * 60)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Minutes;
			}
			else if ((_amount = (int)(num / 1000)) > 0)
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Seconds;
			}
			else
			{
				_element = DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds;
			}
		}

		public static int Base(DateTimeStepElements element)
		{
			switch (element)
			{
			case DateTimeStepElements.Seconds:
			case DateTimeStepElements.Minutes:
				return 60;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Hours:
				return 24;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Days:
				return 7;
			case DateTimeStepElements.None:
				return 0;
			default:
				return 10;
			}
		}

		public static int[] StepSizes(DateTimeStepElements element)
		{
			return Base(element) switch
			{
				7 => _steps7, 
				24 => _steps24, 
				60 => _steps60, 
				_ => _steps10, 
			};
		}
	}

	public static class DateTimeAxisHelpers
	{
		private static DateTimeStepElements Compare(DateTime left, DateTime right)
		{
			DateTimeStepElements dateTimeStepElements = DateTimeStepElements.None;
			if (left.Year / 100 == right.Year / 100)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Centuries;
			}
			if (left.Year == right.Year)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Years;
			}
			if (left.Month == right.Month)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Months;
			}
			if (left.Day == right.Day)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Days;
			}
			if (left.Hour == right.Hour)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Hours;
			}
			if (left.Minute == right.Minute)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Minutes;
			}
			if (left.Second == right.Second)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Seconds;
			}
			if (left.Millisecond == right.Millisecond)
			{
				dateTimeStepElements |= DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds;
			}
			return dateTimeStepElements;
		}

		private static bool Match(DateTimeStepElements value, DateTimeStepElements test)
		{
			return (value & test) == test;
		}

		public static DateTimeAxisRecommendations RecommendAxis(DateTimeRange range, int pixelWidth)
		{
			DateTimeAxisRecommendations recommendation = new DateTimeAxisRecommendations();
			DateTime start = range.Start;
			DateTime end = range.End;
			DateTimeStepElements majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.None;
			DateTimeStepElements minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.None;
			int num = 0;
			IdentifyCommonInformation(ref recommendation, start, end, out var last, ref majorStep, ref minorStep);
			List<DateTime> list = new List<DateTime>();
			while (TryStep(null, start, end, majorStep, 1) <= 2 && majorStep > DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds)
			{
				majorStep = (DateTimeStepElements)((int)majorStep / 2);
				minorStep = (DateTimeStepElements)((int)minorStep / 2);
			}
			int[] array = DateTimeStep.StepSizes(majorStep);
			double num2 = 10.0;
			int[] array2 = array;
			foreach (int num3 in array2)
			{
				TryStep(list, start, end, majorStep, num3);
				if (num3 > 2)
				{
					minorStep = majorStep;
				}
				if ((double)list.Count < num2 && list.Count > 1)
				{
					recommendation.MajorTimeStep = new DateTimeStep(majorStep, num3);
					break;
				}
			}
			if (recommendation.MajorTimeStep == null)
			{
				recommendation.MajorTimeStep = new DateTimeStep(majorStep, 1);
			}
			if (minorStep > majorStep)
			{
				minorStep = majorStep;
			}
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				recommendation.Major = new DateTime[1];
				recommendation.Major[0] = start;
				recommendation.Minor = new DateTime[1];
				recommendation.Minor[0] = start;
			}
			else
			{
				recommendation.Major = list.ToArray();
				List<DateTime> list2 = new List<DateTime>();
				List<DateTime> list3 = new List<DateTime>();
				for (int j = 1; j < list.Count; j++)
				{
					DateTime dateTime = list[j - 1];
					DateTime dateTime2 = list[j];
					if (num == 0)
					{
						while (TryStep(null, dateTime, dateTime2, minorStep, 1) <= 2 && minorStep > DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds)
						{
							minorStep = (DateTimeStepElements)((int)minorStep / 2);
						}
						array2 = DateTimeStep.StepSizes(minorStep);
						foreach (int num4 in array2)
						{
							if ((double)TryStep(list2, dateTime, dateTime2, minorStep, num4) < num2)
							{
								num = num4;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						TryStep(list2, dateTime, dateTime2, minorStep, num);
					}
					foreach (DateTime item in list2)
					{
						if (item > dateTime && item < dateTime2)
						{
							list3.Add(item);
						}
					}
				}
				if (list[0] > start)
				{
					TryStep(list2, start, list[0], minorStep, num);
					int num5 = 0;
					foreach (DateTime item2 in list2)
					{
						if (item2 > start && item2 < list[0])
						{
							list3.Insert(num5, item2);
							num5++;
						}
					}
				}
				if (list[list.Count - 1] < end)
				{
					TryStep(list2, list[list.Count - 1], end, minorStep, num);
					foreach (DateTime item3 in list2)
					{
						if (item3 > list[list.Count - 1] && item3 < end)
						{
							list3.Add(item3);
						}
					}
				}
				recommendation.Minor = list3.ToArray();
			}
			recommendation.MinorTimeStep = new DateTimeStep(minorStep, num);
			recommendation.Format = BuildRecommendationAnnotations(ref recommendation, last);
			recommendation.DragStep = new DateTimeStep(minorStep, num);
			if (pixelWidth > 0)
			{
				while (recommendation.DragStep.ApproximateDuration.TotalMilliseconds > 10.0)
				{
					double num6 = (double)pixelWidth / (range.Milliseconds / recommendation.DragStep.ApproximateDuration.TotalMilliseconds);
					if (num6 < 1.0)
					{
						recommendation.DragStep.Double();
						continue;
					}
					if (!(num6 > 3.0))
					{
						break;
					}
					recommendation.DragStep.Halve();
				}
			}
			return recommendation;
		}

		private static string BuildRecommendationAnnotations(ref DateTimeAxisRecommendations recommendation, DateTime last)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			recommendation.Annotation = new string[recommendation.Major.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < recommendation.Major.Length; i++)
			{
				DateTime dateTime = recommendation.Major[i];
				DateTimeStepElements value = Compare(last, dateTime);
				if (last == dateTime)
				{
					recommendation.Annotation[i] = string.Empty;
					continue;
				}
				last = dateTime;
				string text3;
				if (i > 0 && Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Seconds | DateTimeStepElements.Minutes | DateTimeStepElements.Hours | DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
				{
					text3 = "{0:HH:mm:ss.fff}";
					text2 = ".fff";
					text = ":ss";
				}
				else if (i > 0 && Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Minutes | DateTimeStepElements.Hours | DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
				{
					text3 = "{0:HH:mm:ss}";
					text2 = ".fff";
					text = ":ss";
				}
				else if (i > 0 && Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Hours | DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
				{
					text3 = "{0:HH:mm}";
					text = ":ss";
				}
				else if (i > 0 && Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
				{
					text3 = "{0:HH:mm}";
				}
				else
				{
					text3 = "{0:d}";
					if ((i > 1 && !Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Minutes | DateTimeStepElements.Hours)) || dateTime.Hour != 0 || dateTime.Minute != 0)
					{
						text3 += "\n{0:HH:mm}";
					}
				}
				recommendation.Annotation[i] = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, text3, dateTime);
			}
			return DateTimeFormatInfo.CurrentInfo.ShortDatePattern + " HH:mm" + text + text2;
		}

		private static void IdentifyCommonInformation(ref DateTimeAxisRecommendations recommendation, DateTime left, DateTime right, out DateTime last, ref DateTimeStepElements majorStep, ref DateTimeStepElements minorStep)
		{
			DateTimeStepElements value = Compare(left, right);
			if (Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Minutes | DateTimeStepElements.Hours | DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
			{
				last = new DateTime(left.Year, left.Month, left.Day, left.Hour, left.Minute, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Seconds;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = last.ToString("d t", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			else if (Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Hours | DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
			{
				last = new DateTime(left.Year, left.Month, left.Day, left.Hour, 0, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Minutes;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Seconds;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = last.ToString("d t", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			else if (Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Days | DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
			{
				last = new DateTime(left.Year, left.Month, left.Day, 0, 0, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Hours;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Minutes;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = last.ToString("d", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			else if (Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Months | DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
			{
				last = new DateTime(left.Year, left.Month, 1, 0, 0, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Days;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Hours;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = last.ToString("d", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			else if (Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Years | DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
			{
				last = new DateTime(left.Year, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Months;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Days;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = last.ToString("yyyy", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			else if (Match(value, DateTimeStepElements.Centuries))
			{
				last = new DateTime(100 * (left.Year / 100), 1, 1, 0, 0, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Years;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Months;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = last.ToString("yyyy", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			else
			{
				last = new DateTime(left.Year, left.Month, left.Day, left.Hour, 0, 0);
				majorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Centuries;
				minorStep = DateTimeStepElements.Years;
				recommendation.CommonTitle = string.Empty;
			}
		}

		private static int TryStep(List<DateTime> steps, DateTime start, DateTime end, DateTimeStepElements position, int amount)
		{
			int num = start.Year;
			int num2 = start.Month;
			int num3 = start.Day;
			int num4 = start.Hour;
			int num5 = start.Minute;
			int num6 = start.Second;
			int num7 = start.Millisecond;
			int num8 = 0;
			steps?.Clear();
			switch (position)
			{
			case DateTimeStepElements.Centuries:
				amount *= 100;
				goto case DateTimeStepElements.Years;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Years:
				num = Math.Max(1, num / amount * amount);
				num2 = (num3 = 1);
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Months:
				num2 = (num2 - 1) / amount * amount + 1;
				num3 = 1;
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Days:
				num3 = (num3 - 1) / amount * amount + 1;
				num4 = (num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0)));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Hours:
				num4 = num4 / amount * amount;
				num5 = (num6 = (num7 = 0));
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Minutes:
				num5 = num5 / amount * amount;
				num6 = (num7 = 0);
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Seconds:
				num6 = num6 / amount * amount;
				num7 = 0;
				break;
			case DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds:
				if (amount == 0)
				{
					amount = 1;
				}
				num7 = num7 / amount * amount;
				break;
			}
			DateTime dateTime = new DateTime(num, num2, num3, num4, num5, num6, num7);
			while (position != DateTimeStepElements.None && !(dateTime > end))
			{
				if (dateTime >= start)
				{
					num8++;
					steps?.Add(dateTime);
				}
				switch (position)
				{
				case DateTimeStepElements.Years:
				case DateTimeStepElements.Centuries:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddYears(amount);
					break;
				case DateTimeStepElements.Months:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddMonths(amount);
					break;
				case DateTimeStepElements.Days:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddDays(amount);
					break;
				case DateTimeStepElements.Hours:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddHours(amount);
					break;
				case DateTimeStepElements.Minutes:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddMinutes(amount);
					break;
				case DateTimeStepElements.Seconds:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddSeconds(amount);
					break;
				case DateTimeStepElements.Milliseconds:
					dateTime = dateTime.AddMilliseconds(amount);
					break;
				}
			}
			return num8;
		}
	}
	public class DayOfTheWeek
	{
		private CultureInfo _cultureInfo;

		private DateTime _baseDate;

		public DayOfWeek DayOfWeek { get; private set; }

		public bool IsWorkday { get; set; }

		public string Name => ToString();

		private DayOfTheWeek()
		{
		}

		public DayOfTheWeek(DayOfWeek dayOfWeek, CultureInfo cultureInfo, bool isWorkday)
		{
			DayOfWeek = dayOfWeek;
			_cultureInfo = cultureInfo;
			_baseDate = DateTime.Today;
			while (_baseDate.DayOfWeek != DayOfWeek)
			{
				_baseDate = _baseDate.AddDays(1.0);
			}
			IsWorkday = isWorkday;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return _baseDate.ToString("dddd", _cultureInfo);
		}
	}
	public class DaysOfTheWeek : ObservableCollection<DayOfTheWeek>
	{
		private static DaysOfTheWeek _daysOfTheWeek;

		public static DaysOfTheWeek Days
		{
			get
			{
				if (_daysOfTheWeek == null)
				{
					_daysOfTheWeek = Create(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				}
				return _daysOfTheWeek;
			}
		}

		private DaysOfTheWeek()
		{
		}

		public static DaysOfTheWeek Create(CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			DaysOfTheWeek daysOfTheWeek = new DaysOfTheWeek();
			for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++)
			{
				daysOfTheWeek.Add(new DayOfTheWeek((DayOfWeek)i, cultureInfo, i != 0 && i != 6));
			}
			return daysOfTheWeek;
		}

		public static DaysOfTheWeek Create()
		{
			return Create(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
		}

		public new void Add(DayOfTheWeek item)
		{
			base.Add(item);
		}

		public bool IsDateWorkday(DateTime date)
		{
			using (IEnumerator<DayOfTheWeek> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					DayOfTheWeek current = enumerator.Current;
					if (current.DayOfWeek == date.DayOfWeek)
					{
						return current.IsWorkday;
					}
				}
			}
			throw new ArgumentException("Invalid date.", "date");
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeCalendar : IList<IDateTimeManager>, ICollection<IDateTimeManager>, IEnumerable<IDateTimeManager>, IEnumerable
	{
		private Collection<IDateTimeManager> _list;

		private EventHandler _dataChanged;

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public IDateTimeManager this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return _list[index];
			}
			set
			{
				IDateTimeManager dateTimeManager = _list[index];
				if (dateTimeManager != value)
				{
					if (dateTimeManager != null)
					{
						dateTimeManager.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dataChanged;
					}
					if (value != null)
					{
						value.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dataChanged;
					}
					_list[index] = value;
					DataChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
				}
			}
		}

		public int Count => _list.Count;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public event EventHandler DateTimeRangeChanged;

		public DateTimeRangeCalendar()
		{
			_list = new Collection<IDateTimeManager>();
			_dataChanged = DataChanged;
		}

		public DateTimeRangeCalendar(IList<IDateTimeManager> list)
		{
			_list = new Collection<IDateTimeManager>(list);
			_dataChanged = DataChanged;
			foreach (IDateTimeManager item in _list)
			{
				item.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dataChanged;
			}
		}

		public DateTimeRangeCalendar(params IDateTimeManager[] list)
		{
			_list = new Collection<IDateTimeManager>(list);
			_dataChanged = DataChanged;
			foreach (IDateTimeManager item in _list)
			{
				item.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dataChanged;
			}
		}

		private void DataChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			this.DateTimeRangeChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public int IndexOf(IDateTimeManager item)
		{
			return _list.IndexOf(item);
		}

		public void Insert(int index, IDateTimeManager item)
		{
			_list.Insert(index, item);
			item.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dataChanged;
			DataChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			IDateTimeManager dateTimeManager = _list[index];
			_list.RemoveAt(index);
			dateTimeManager.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dataChanged;
			DataChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public void Add(IDateTimeManager item)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				item.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dataChanged;
			}
			_list.Add(item);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			foreach (IDateTimeManager item in _list)
			{
				item.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dataChanged;
			}
			_list.Clear();
		}

		public bool Contains(IDateTimeManager item)
		{
			return _list.Contains(item);
		}

		public void CopyTo(IDateTimeManager[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			_list.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
		}

		public bool Remove(IDateTimeManager item)
		{
			if (_list.Remove(item))
			{
				item.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dataChanged;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public IEnumerator<IDateTimeManager> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return _list.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return ((IEnumerable)_list).GetEnumerator();
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeSet : DateTimeRangeCollection, IDateTimeRangeCollection, IList<DateTimeRange>, ICollection<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable, IDateTimeManager
	{
		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush Color { get; set; }

		public DateTimeRangeSet()
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeSet(IEnumerable<DateTimeRange> collection)
			: base(collection)
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeSet(params DateTimeRange[] collection)
			: base(collection)
		{
		}

		public IDateTimeRangeCollection GetTimeRanges(DateTime from, DateTime to, bool overlap)
		{
			DateTimeRangeCollection dateTimeRangeCollection = new DateTimeRangeCollection();
			using (IEnumerator<DateTimeRange> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					DateTimeRange current = enumerator.Current;
					if (!current.IsValid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (overlap)
					{
						if (current.End > from && current.Start < to)
						{
							dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(current.Start, current.End);
						}
					}
					else if (current.Start >= from && current.End <= to)
					{
						dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(current.Start, current.End);
					}
				}
			}
			dateTimeRangeCollection.Conflate();
			return dateTimeRangeCollection;
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeUnion : DateTimeRangeCalendar, IDateTimeManager
	{
		public System.Windows.Media.Brush Color { get; set; }

		public DateTimeRangeUnion()
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeUnion(IList<IDateTimeManager> list)
			: base(list)
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeUnion(params IDateTimeManager[] list)
			: base(list)
		{
		}

		public IDateTimeRangeCollection GetTimeRanges(DateTime from, DateTime to, bool overlap)
		{
			DateTimeRangeCollection dateTimeRangeCollection = new DateTimeRangeCollection();
			using (IEnumerator<IDateTimeManager> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					IDateTimeManager current = enumerator.Current;
					dateTimeRangeCollection.AddRange(current.GetTimeRanges(from, to, overlap));
				}
			}
			dateTimeRangeCollection.Conflate();
			return dateTimeRangeCollection;
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeRepeat : DateTimeRangeCollection, IDateTimeRangeCollection, IList<DateTimeRange>, ICollection<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable, IDateTimeManager
	{
		private TimeSpan _repeatInterval;

		public TimeSpan RepeatInterval
		{
			get
			{
				return _repeatInterval;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_repeatInterval != value)
				{
					_repeatInterval = value;
					DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush Color { get; set; }

		public DateTimeRangeRepeat()
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeRepeat(IEnumerable<DateTimeRange> collection)
			: base(collection)
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeRepeat(params DateTimeRange[] collection)
			: base(collection)
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRangeRepeat(TimeSpan repeatInterval)
		{
			RepeatInterval = repeatInterval;
		}

		public DateTimeRangeRepeat(TimeSpan repeatInterval, IEnumerable<DateTimeRange> collection)
			: base(collection)
		{
			RepeatInterval = repeatInterval;
		}

		public DateTimeRangeRepeat(TimeSpan repeatInterval, params DateTimeRange[] collection)
			: base(collection)
		{
			RepeatInterval = repeatInterval;
		}

		public IDateTimeRangeCollection GetTimeRanges(DateTime from, DateTime to, bool overlap)
		{
			DateTimeRangeCollection dateTimeRangeCollection = new DateTimeRangeCollection();
			long ticks = RepeatInterval.Ticks;
			if (ticks > 0)
			{
				using (IEnumerator<DateTimeRange> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
				{
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						DateTimeRange current = enumerator.Current;
						if (!current.IsValid)
						{
							continue;
						}
						TimeSpan timeSpan = TimeSpan.Zero;
						if (current.Start > from)
						{
							long num = (current.Start - from).Ticks / ticks + 1;
							timeSpan = new TimeSpan(num * ticks);
						}
						else if (current.End < from)
						{
							long num2 = (from - current.End).Ticks / ticks;
							timeSpan = new TimeSpan(num2 * ticks);
						}
						DateTime dateTime = current.Start - timeSpan;
						DateTime dateTime2 = current.End - timeSpan;
						while (true)
						{
							if (overlap)
							{
								if (dateTime >= to)
								{
									break;
								}
								if (dateTime2 > from)
								{
									dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTime, dateTime2);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								if (dateTime2 > to)
								{
									break;
								}
								if (dateTime >= from)
								{
									dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTime, dateTime2);
								}
							}
							dateTime += RepeatInterval;
							dateTime2 += RepeatInterval;
						}
					}
				}
				Sort();
				Conflate();
			}
			return dateTimeRangeCollection;
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeInvert : IDateTimeManager
	{
		private IDateTimeManager _child;

		private EventHandler _dateChanged;

		public IDateTimeManager Child
		{
			get
			{
				return _child;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_child != value)
				{
					if (_child != null)
					{
						_child.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dateChanged;
					}
					if (value != null)
					{
						value.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dateChanged;
					}
					_child = value;
					DateChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
				}
			}
		}

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush Color { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler DateTimeRangeChanged;

		public DateTimeRangeInvert()
		{
			_dateChanged = DateChanged;
		}

		public DateTimeRangeInvert(IDateTimeManager child)
		{
			_dateChanged = DateChanged;
			Child = child;
		}

		private void DateChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			this.DateTimeRangeChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public IDateTimeRangeCollection GetTimeRanges(DateTime from, DateTime to, bool overlap)
		{
			DateTimeRangeCollection dateTimeRangeCollection = new DateTimeRangeCollection();
			if (Child == null)
			{
				dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(from, to);
			}
			else
			{
				DateTime dateTime = from;
				foreach (DateTimeRange timeRange in Child.GetTimeRanges(from, to, overlap))
				{
					if (timeRange.Start > dateTime)
					{
						dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTime, timeRange.Start);
					}
					dateTime = timeRange.End;
				}
				if (dateTime < to)
				{
					dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTime, to);
				}
			}
			return dateTimeRangeCollection;
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeIntersect : IDateTimeManager
	{
		private EventHandler _dateChanged;

		private IDateTimeManager _left;

		private IDateTimeManager _right;

		public IDateTimeManager Left
		{
			get
			{
				return _left;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_left != value)
				{
					if (value != null)
					{
						value.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dateChanged;
					}
					if (_left != null)
					{
						_left.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dateChanged;
					}
					_left = value;
					DateChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
				}
			}
		}

		public IDateTimeManager Right
		{
			get
			{
				return _right;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_right != value)
				{
					if (value != null)
					{
						value.DateTimeRangeChanged += _dateChanged;
					}
					if (_right != null)
					{
						_right.DateTimeRangeChanged -= _dateChanged;
					}
					_right = value;
					DateChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
				}
			}
		}

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush Color { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler DateTimeRangeChanged;

		public DateTimeRangeIntersect()
		{
			_dateChanged = DateChanged;
		}

		public DateTimeRangeIntersect(IDateTimeManager left, IDateTimeManager right)
		{
			_dateChanged = DateChanged;
			Left = left;
			Right = right;
		}

		private void DateChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			this.DateTimeRangeChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		public IDateTimeRangeCollection GetTimeRanges(DateTime from, DateTime to, bool overlap)
		{
			if (Left == null)
			{
				if (Right == null)
				{
					return new DateTimeRangeCollection();
				}
				return Right.GetTimeRanges(from, to, overlap);
			}
			if (Right == null)
			{
				return Left.GetTimeRanges(from, to, overlap);
			}
			IDateTimeRangeCollection timeRanges = Left.GetTimeRanges(from, to, overlap);
			IDateTimeRangeCollection timeRanges2 = Right.GetTimeRanges(from, to, overlap);
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			DateTimeRangeCollection dateTimeRangeCollection = new DateTimeRangeCollection();
			while (num < timeRanges.Count && num2 < timeRanges2.Count)
			{
				DateTimeRange dateTimeRange = timeRanges[num];
				DateTimeRange dateTimeRange2 = timeRanges2[num2];
				if (dateTimeRange.Start < dateTimeRange2.Start)
				{
					if (dateTimeRange.End < dateTimeRange2.Start)
					{
						num++;
					}
					else if (dateTimeRange2.End < dateTimeRange.End)
					{
						dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTimeRange2.Start, dateTimeRange2.End);
						num2++;
					}
					else
					{
						dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTimeRange2.Start, dateTimeRange.End);
						num++;
					}
				}
				else if (dateTimeRange2.End < dateTimeRange.Start)
				{
					num2++;
				}
				else if (dateTimeRange.End < dateTimeRange2.End)
				{
					dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTimeRange.Start, dateTimeRange.End);
					num++;
				}
				else
				{
					dateTimeRangeCollection.Add(dateTimeRange.Start, dateTimeRange2.End);
					num2++;
				}
			}
			return dateTimeRangeCollection;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	[TypeConverter(typeof(DateTimeRangeConverter))]
	public class DateTimeRange : IComparable<DateTimeRange>, IEquatable<DateTimeRange>, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private DateTime _start;

		private DateTime _end;

		public DateTime Start
		{
			get
			{
				return _start;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_start != value)
				{
					bool isSingular = IsSingular;
					bool num = !IsEmpty;
					_start = value;
					if (!num)
					{
						_end = value;
						RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
						RaisePropertyChanged("End");
						RaisePropertyChanged("IsValid");
					}
					else
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
					}
					RaisePropertyChanged("Duration");
					RaisePropertyChanged("Milliseconds");
					if (isSingular != IsSingular)
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged("Singular");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTime End
		{
			get
			{
				return _end;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_end != value)
				{
					bool isSingular = IsSingular;
					bool num = !IsEmpty;
					_end = value;
					if (!num)
					{
						_start = value;
						RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
						RaisePropertyChanged("End");
						RaisePropertyChanged("IsValid");
					}
					else
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged("End");
					}
					RaisePropertyChanged("Duration");
					RaisePropertyChanged("Milliseconds");
					if (isSingular != IsSingular)
					{
						RaisePropertyChanged("Singular");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsEmpty
		{
			get
			{
				if (Start == default(DateTime))
				{
					return End == default(DateTime);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSingular
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsEmpty)
				{
					return _start == _end;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsValid
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsEmpty)
				{
					return Start <= End;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public TimeSpan Duration
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsEmpty)
				{
					return _end - _start;
				}
				return TimeSpan.Zero;
			}
		}

		public double Milliseconds
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsEmpty)
				{
					return (_end - _start).TotalMilliseconds;
				}
				return 0.0;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public DateTimeRange()
		{
		}

		public DateTimeRange(DateTime start, DateTime end)
		{
			_start = Min(start, end);
			_end = Max(start, end);
		}

		public DateTimeRange(DateTime start, TimeSpan duration)
			: this(start, start + duration)
		{
		}

		public void Include(DateTime value)
		{
			if (IsEmpty)
			{
				_start = (_end = value);
				RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
				RaisePropertyChanged("End");
				RaisePropertyChanged("IsValid");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Duration");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Milliseconds");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Singular");
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool isSingular = IsSingular;
			if (value < _start)
			{
				_start = value;
				flag = true;
			}
			if (value > _end)
			{
				_end = value;
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("End");
			}
			if (flag2 || flag)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("Duration");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Milliseconds");
				if (isSingular != IsSingular)
				{
					RaisePropertyChanged("Singular");
				}
			}
		}

		public void Include(DateTimeRange range)
		{
			if (!(range != null) || !range.IsValid)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsEmpty)
			{
				_start = range.Start;
				_end = range.End;
				RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
				RaisePropertyChanged("End");
				RaisePropertyChanged("IsValid");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Duration");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Milliseconds");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Singular");
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool isSingular = IsSingular;
			if (range.Start < _start)
			{
				_start = range.Start;
				flag = true;
			}
			if (range.End > _end)
			{
				_end = range.End;
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("Start");
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("End");
			}
			if (flag2 || flag)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("Duration");
				RaisePropertyChanged("Milliseconds");
				if (isSingular != IsSingular)
				{
					RaisePropertyChanged("Singular");
				}
			}
		}

		public DateTimeRange Union(DateTime value)
		{
			if (IsEmpty)
			{
				return new DateTimeRange(value, value);
			}
			return new DateTimeRange(Min(Start, value), Max(End, value));
		}

		public DateTimeRange Union(DateTimeRange value)
		{
			if (IsEmpty)
			{
				return new DateTimeRange(value.Start, value.End);
			}
			return new DateTimeRange(Min(Start, value.Start), Max(End, value.End));
		}

		public void Shift(TimeSpan delta)
		{
			if (!IsEmpty && delta != TimeSpan.Zero)
			{
				Start += delta;
				End += delta;
			}
		}

		public DateTimeRange ShiftNew(TimeSpan delta)
		{
			return new DateTimeRange(Start + delta, End + delta);
		}

		public DateTimeRange Expand(double factor)
		{
			return Expand(factor, TimeSpan.MaxValue, TimeSpan.MinValue);
		}

		public DateTimeRange Expand(double factor, TimeSpan maxDuration, TimeSpan minDuration)
		{
			if (factor <= 0.0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("factor", "Parameter factor must be greater than zero.");
			}
			if (IsEmpty || IsSingular)
			{
				return this;
			}
			long ticks = Duration.Ticks;
			DateTime dateTime = Start + new TimeSpan(ticks / 2);
			long num = Math.Max(minDuration.Ticks, Math.Min(maxDuration.Ticks, (long)((double)ticks * factor)));
			TimeSpan timeSpan = new TimeSpan(num / 2);
			Start = dateTime - timeSpan;
			End = dateTime + timeSpan;
			return this;
		}

		public bool Contains(DateTime value)
		{
			if (Start <= value)
			{
				return End >= value;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool Contains(DateTimeRange range)
		{
			if (Contains(range.Start))
			{
				return Contains(range.End);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool AdjacentWith(DateTimeRange value)
		{
			if (!IsEmpty && !value.IsEmpty && (Start == value.End || End == value.Start))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IntersectWith(DateTimeRange value)
		{
			if (!IsEmpty && !value.IsEmpty)
			{
				if (Start == value.Start && End == value.End)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (Contains(value.Start) || Contains(value.End))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (value.Contains(Start) || value.Contains(End))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public double GetPositionInFraction(DateTime value)
		{
			if (IsSingular)
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			return (double)(value - Start).Ticks / (double)Duration.Ticks;
		}

		public DateTime GetDateTimeAt(double factionPosition)
		{
			if (IsSingular)
			{
				return Start;
			}
			return Start + new TimeSpan((long)((double)Duration.Ticks * factionPosition));
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is DateTimeRange)
			{
				return Equals((DateTimeRange)obj);
			}
			return base.Equals(obj);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return Start.GetHashCode() ^ End.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return Start.ToString() + ";" + End.ToString();
		}

		public static DateTimeRange Parse(string range)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(range))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Specified range cannot be null or empty.");
			}
			if (!range.Contains(';'))
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Specified range is not a correct DateTimeRange format.");
			}
			int num = range.IndexOf(';');
			string s = range.Substring(0, num);
			return new DateTimeRange(end: DateTime.Parse(range.Substring(num + 1)), start: DateTime.Parse(s));
		}

		public static DateTime Min(DateTime value1, DateTime value2)
		{
			if (!(value1 < value2))
			{
				return value2;
			}
			return value1;
		}

		public static DateTime Max(DateTime value1, DateTime value2)
		{
			if (!(value1 > value2))
			{
				return value2;
			}
			return value1;
		}

		public bool IsSame(DateTimeRange dateTimeRange)
		{
			if (IsValid)
			{
				if (dateTimeRange != null && dateTimeRange.IsValid)
				{
					if (dateTimeRange.Start == _start)
					{
						return dateTimeRange.End == _end;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (!(dateTimeRange == null))
			{
				return !dateTimeRange.IsValid;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public int CompareTo(DateTimeRange other)
		{
			if (other == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			int num = Start.CompareTo(other.Start);
			if (num != 0)
			{
				return num;
			}
			return End.CompareTo(other.End);
		}

		public bool Equals(DateTimeRange other)
		{
			return CompareTo(other) == 0;
		}

		public static bool operator ==(DateTimeRange x, DateTimeRange y)
		{
			if ((object)x != null || (object)y != null)
			{
				return x.Equals(y);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool operator !=(DateTimeRange x, DateTimeRange y)
		{
			if ((object)x == null || x.Equals(y))
			{
				if ((object)x == null)
				{
					return (object)y != null;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool operator <(DateTimeRange x, DateTimeRange y)
		{
			return x.CompareTo(y) < 0;
		}

		public static bool operator <=(DateTimeRange x, DateTimeRange y)
		{
			return x.CompareTo(y) <= 0;
		}

		public static bool operator >(DateTimeRange x, DateTimeRange y)
		{
			return x.CompareTo(y) > 0;
		}

		public static bool operator >=(DateTimeRange x, DateTimeRange y)
		{
			return x.CompareTo(y) >= 0;
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeCollection : IList<DateTimeRange>, ICollection<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable, IDateTimeRangeCollection
	{
		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _datePropertyChanged;

		private bool _updating;

		private List<DateTimeRange> _list;

		public DateTimeRange this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return _list[index];
			}
			set
			{
				DateTimeRange dateTimeRange = _list[index];
				if (dateTimeRange != value)
				{
					dateTimeRange.PropertyChanged -= _datePropertyChanged;
					value.PropertyChanged += _datePropertyChanged;
					_list[index] = value;
					DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public int Count => _list.Count;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public event EventHandler DateTimeRangeChanged;

		public DateTimeRangeCollection()
		{
			_datePropertyChanged = DatePropertyChanged;
			_list = new List<DateTimeRange>();
		}

		public DateTimeRangeCollection(IEnumerable<DateTimeRange> collection)
		{
			_datePropertyChanged = DatePropertyChanged;
			_list = new List<DateTimeRange>(collection);
			foreach (DateTimeRange item in collection)
			{
				item.PropertyChanged += _datePropertyChanged;
			}
		}

		public DateTimeRangeCollection(params DateTimeRange[] collection)
		{
			_datePropertyChanged = DatePropertyChanged;
			_list = new List<DateTimeRange>(collection);
			for (int i = 0; i < collection.Length; i++)
			{
				collection[i].PropertyChanged += _datePropertyChanged;
			}
		}

		public void Add(DateTime start, DateTime end)
		{
			Add(new DateTimeRange(start, end));
		}

		public void Add(DateTimeRange range)
		{
			range.PropertyChanged += _datePropertyChanged;
			_list.Add(range);
			DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
		}

		public void AddRange(IEnumerable<DateTimeRange> range)
		{
			if (range == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DateTimeRange item in range)
			{
				item.PropertyChanged += _datePropertyChanged;
			}
			_list.AddRange(range);
			DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
		}

		public bool Remove(DateTimeRange range)
		{
			if (_list.Remove(range))
			{
				range.PropertyChanged -= _datePropertyChanged;
				DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			DateTimeRange dateTimeRange = _list[index];
			_list.RemoveAt(index);
			dateTimeRange.PropertyChanged -= _datePropertyChanged;
			DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
		}

		public void Sort()
		{
			_list.Sort();
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			using (IEnumerator<DateTimeRange> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					enumerator.Current.PropertyChanged -= _datePropertyChanged;
				}
			}
			_list.Clear();
			DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
		}

		public void Conflate()
		{
			bool flag = false;
			_updating = true;
			try
			{
				_list.Sort();
				for (int i = 1; i < Count; i++)
				{
					DateTimeRange dateTimeRange = this[i - 1];
					while (i < Count)
					{
						DateTimeRange dateTimeRange2 = this[i];
						if (dateTimeRange.End < dateTimeRange2.Start)
						{
							break;
						}
						dateTimeRange.Include(dateTimeRange2);
						RemoveAt(i);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_updating = false;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
			}
		}

		protected void DateTimeRangeHasChanged()
		{
			if (!_updating)
			{
				this.DateTimeRangeChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		private void DatePropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			string propertyName = e.PropertyName;
			if (propertyName == "Start" || propertyName == "End")
			{
				DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
			}
		}

		public int IndexOf(DateTimeRange item)
		{
			return _list.IndexOf(item);
		}

		public void Insert(int index, DateTimeRange item)
		{
			item.PropertyChanged += _datePropertyChanged;
			_list.Insert(index, item);
			DateTimeRangeHasChanged();
		}

		void IList<DateTimeRange>.RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			RemoveAt(index);
		}

		public bool Contains(DateTimeRange item)
		{
			return _list.Contains(item);
		}

		public void CopyTo(DateTimeRange[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			_list.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
		}

		public IEnumerator<DateTimeRange> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return _list.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return ((IEnumerable)_list).GetEnumerator();
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeRangeConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			if (sourceType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertFrom(context, sourceType);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			string text = value as string;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return DateTimeRange.Parse(text);
			}
			return base.ConvertFrom(context, culture, value);
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(string) && value is DateTimeRange)
			{
				return ((DateTimeRange)value).ToString();
			}
			return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
		}
	}
	public interface IDateTimeManager
	{
		object Tag { get; set; }

		System.Windows.Media.Brush Color { get; set; }

		event EventHandler DateTimeRangeChanged;

		IDateTimeRangeCollection GetTimeRanges(DateTime from, DateTime to, bool overlap);
	}
	public interface IDateTimeRangeCollection : IList<DateTimeRange>, ICollection<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable<DateTimeRange>, IEnumerable
	{
	}
	public interface IDateTimePosition
	{
		double DateTimeToPosition(DateTime dateTime);

		DateTime PositionToDateTime(double position);
	}
	public static class DateTimeSupport
	{
		public static DateTime ConvertToLocalPlant(string text, TimeSpan plantTimeZone)
		{
			return new DateTime(XmlConvert.ToDateTime(text, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc).Add(plantTimeZone).Ticks, DateTimeKind.Local);
		}

		public static string ConvertFromLocalPlant(DateTime date, TimeSpan plantTimeZone)
		{
			date = new DateTime(date.Ticks, DateTimeKind.Utc);
			date = date.Subtract(plantTimeZone);
			return XmlConvert.ToString(date, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc);
		}
	}
	public class WatchedPropertyChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public DependencyProperty Property { get; set; }

		public object OldValue { get; set; }

		public object NewValue { get; set; }

		public WatchedPropertyChangedEventArgs()
		{
		}

		public WatchedPropertyChangedEventArgs(DependencyProperty property, object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			Property = property;
			OldValue = oldValue;
			NewValue = newValue;
		}

		public WatchedPropertyChangedEventArgs(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			Property = args.Property;
			OldValue = args.OldValue;
			NewValue = args.NewValue;
		}
	}
	public class DependencyPropertyWatcher : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Value", typeof(object), typeof(DependencyPropertyWatcher), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnValuePropertyChanged));

		public DependencyObject WatchObject { get; private set; }

		public DependencyProperty WatchProperty { get; private set; }

		public object Value
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ValueProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ValueProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<WatchedPropertyChangedEventArgs> PropertyChanged;

		public DependencyPropertyWatcher(DependencyObject watchObject, DependencyProperty watchProperty)
		{
			WatchObject = watchObject;
			WatchProperty = watchProperty;
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, ValueProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = watchObject,
				Path = new PropertyPath(watchProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		private static void OnValuePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is DependencyPropertyWatcher dependencyPropertyWatcher)
			{
				dependencyPropertyWatcher.OnValueChanged(args.OldValue, args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnValueChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(WatchObject, new WatchedPropertyChangedEventArgs
				{
					Property = WatchProperty,
					OldValue = oldValue,
					NewValue = newValue
				});
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplatePart(Name = "ErrorBorder", Type = typeof(Border))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "ContentBorder", Type = typeof(Border))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "ErrorIcon", Type = typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Image))]
	[TemplatePart(Name = "RequiredAsterix", Type = typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Image))]
	public class ErrorPanel : ContentControl
	{
		private const string ErrorBorderElement = "ErrorBorder";

		private const string ContentBorderElement = "ContentBorder";

		private const string ErrorIconElement = "ErrorIcon";

		private const string RequiredAsterixElement = "RequiredAsterix";

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IconSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IconSource", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(ErrorPanel), null);

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ErrorTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ErrorText", typeof(string), typeof(ErrorPanel), new PropertyMetadata(ErrorTextPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty RequiredTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RequiredText", typeof(string), typeof(ErrorPanel), new PropertyMetadata(RequiredTextPropertyChanged));

		private Border ContentBorder { get; set; }

		private Border ErrorBorder { get; set; }

		private System.Windows.Controls.Image ErrorIcon { get; set; }

		private System.Windows.Controls.Image RequiredAsterix { get; set; }

		public ImageSource IconSource
		{
			get
			{
				return (ImageSource)GetValue(IconSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IconSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string ErrorText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(ErrorTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ErrorTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string RequiredText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(RequiredTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(RequiredTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Dock IconDock
		{
			get
			{
				return DockPanel.GetDock(ErrorIcon);
			}
			set
			{
				DockPanel.SetDock(ErrorIcon, value);
				DockPanel.SetDock(RequiredAsterix, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowingError
		{
			get
			{
				if (ErrorIcon != null)
				{
					return ErrorIcon.Visibility == Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool ShowingRequiredAsterix
		{
			get
			{
				if (RequiredAsterix != null)
				{
					return RequiredAsterix.Visibility == Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool ShowingAnything
		{
			get
			{
				if (!ShowingError)
				{
					return ShowingRequiredAsterix;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public ErrorPanel()
		{
			base.DefaultStyleKey = typeof(ErrorPanel);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			ContentBorder = GetTemplateChild("ContentBorder") as Border;
			ErrorBorder = GetTemplateChild("ErrorBorder") as Border;
			ErrorIcon = GetTemplateChild("ErrorIcon") as System.Windows.Controls.Image;
			RequiredAsterix = GetTemplateChild("RequiredAsterix") as System.Windows.Controls.Image;
			SetErrorState();
		}

		private static void ErrorTextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ErrorPanel errorPanel)
			{
				errorPanel.ErrorTextPropertyChanged(e);
			}
		}

		private void ErrorTextPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetErrorState();
		}

		private static void RequiredTextPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ErrorPanel errorPanel)
			{
				errorPanel.RequiredTextPropertyChanged(e);
			}
		}

		private void RequiredTextPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetErrorState();
		}

		private void SetErrorState()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ErrorText))
			{
				if (ErrorIcon != null)
				{
					ErrorIcon.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				if (ErrorBorder != null)
				{
					ErrorBorder.Opacity = 0.0;
				}
				if (RequiredAsterix != null)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(RequiredText))
					{
						RequiredAsterix.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					else
					{
						RequiredAsterix.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (ErrorIcon != null)
				{
					ErrorIcon.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (ErrorBorder != null)
				{
					ErrorBorder.Opacity = 1.0;
				}
				if (RequiredAsterix != null)
				{
					RequiredAsterix.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			string errorText = (RequiredText = string.Empty);
			ErrorText = errorText;
		}
	}

		{
			ShowDlg(Parent, "{C4C53DF2-F332-4377-942E-98C10A13D320}", e.Message, e);
		}

		public static void ShowDlg(Window Parent, string caption, string text, Exception e)
		{
			ExceptionDialog exceptionDialog = CreateDialog(caption, text, e);
			exceptionDialog.Owner = Parent;
			exceptionDialog.ShowDialog();
		}

		private static ExceptionDialog CreateDialog(string caption, string text, Exception e)
		{
			ExceptionDialog exceptionDialog = new ExceptionDialog();
			exceptionDialog.Title = ((caption == "{C4C53DF2-F332-4377-942E-98C10A13D320}") ? ((string)exceptionDialog.FindResource("UnexpectedError")) : caption);
			exceptionDialog.tbExceptionText.Text = text;
			if (e != null)
			{
				exceptionDialog.tbMessage.Text = e.Message;
				exceptionDialog.tbSource.Text = e.Source;
				exceptionDialog.tbStackTrace.Text = e.StackTrace;
				if (e.InnerException != null)
				{
					exceptionDialog._innerException = e.InnerException;
					exceptionDialog.tbInnerExceptionText.Text = e.InnerException.Message;
				}
				else
				{
					exceptionDialog.spInnerException.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				exceptionDialog.eDetails.IsExpanded = false;
				exceptionDialog.eDetails.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else
			{
				exceptionDialog.eDetails.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			return exceptionDialog;
		}

		public static void ShowDlg(Window Parent, string caption, string text, string message, string source, string stackTrace)
		{
			ExceptionDialog exceptionDialog = CreateDialog(caption, text, message, source, stackTrace);
			exceptionDialog.Owner = Parent;
			exceptionDialog.ShowDialog();
		}

		private static ExceptionDialog CreateDialog(string caption, string text, string message, string source, string stackTrace)
		{
			return new ExceptionDialog
			{
				Title = caption,
				tbExceptionText = 
				{
					Text = text
				},
				tbMessage = 
				{
					Text = message
				},
				tbSource = 
				{
					Text = source
				},
				tbStackTrace = 
				{
					Text = stackTrace
				},
				spInnerException = 
				{
					Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed
				},
				eDetails = 
				{
					IsExpanded = false,
					Visibility = Visibility.Visible
				}
			};
		}

		private void tbInnerException_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_innerExceptionClick = true;
		}

		private void tbInnerException_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_innerExceptionClick = false;
		}

		private void tbInnerException_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_innerExceptionClick && _innerException != null)
			{
				ShowDlg(this, base.Title, (string)FindResource("InnerExceptionCaption"), _innerException);
			}
			_innerExceptionClick = false;
		}

		private void bOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		private void bCopy_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}\r\n\r\nException message: {1}\r\nException source: {2}\r\nStack trace: {3}\r\n", tbExceptionText.Text, tbMessage.Text, tbSource.Text, tbStackTrace.Text));
			if (_innerException != null)
			{
				CopyInnerException(stringBuilder, _innerException);
			}
			Clipboard.SetText(stringBuilder.ToString(), TextDataFormat.UnicodeText);
		}

		private void CopyInnerException(StringBuilder sb, Exception innerException)
		{
			sb.Append(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "\r\nInner Exception:\r\nException message: {0}\r\nException source: {1}\r\nStack trace: {2}\r\n", innerException.Message, innerException.Source, innerException.StackTrace));
			if (innerException.InnerException != null)
			{
				CopyInnerException(sb, innerException.InnerException);
			}
		}
