	public static class ImageRenderer
	{
		public static BitmapSource RenderToBitmap(UIElement control, int pixelWidth, int pixelHeight, System.Windows.Media.Brush background = null)
		{
			System.Windows.Size size = control.RenderSize;
			bool flag = !control.IsVisible;
			if (size.Width != (double)pixelWidth || size.Height != (double)pixelHeight)
			{
				size = new System.Windows.Size(pixelWidth, pixelHeight);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				control.Measure(size);
				control.Arrange(new Rect(size));
				control.UpdateLayout();
			}
			RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(pixelWidth, pixelHeight, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
			DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
			using (DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
			{
				drawingContext.DrawRectangle(background ?? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent, null, new Rect(size));
			}
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(drawingVisual);
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(control);
			return renderTargetBitmap;
		}
	}
	public class ThreadSafeImageRenderer
	{
		private Func<object[], BitmapSource[]> _renderFunction;

		private object[] _renderFunctionParameters;

		private byte[] _imageData;

		private Exception _exception;

		private ThreadSafeImageRenderer()
		{
		}

		public static byte[] Render(Func<object[], BitmapSource[]> renderFunction, params object[] functionParameters)
		{
			ThreadSafeImageRenderer threadSafeImageRenderer = new ThreadSafeImageRenderer();
			byte[] result = threadSafeImageRenderer.InternalRender(renderFunction, functionParameters);
			if (threadSafeImageRenderer._exception == null)
			{
				return result;
			}
			throw threadSafeImageRenderer._exception;
		}

		private byte[] InternalRender(Func<object[], BitmapSource[]> renderFunction, object[] functionParameters)
		{
			_renderFunction = renderFunction;
			_renderFunctionParameters = functionParameters;
			_imageData = null;
			Thread thread = new Thread(ProcessImage);
			thread.SetApartmentState(ApartmentState.STA);
			thread.CurrentCulture = Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture;
			thread.CurrentUICulture = Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentUICulture;
			thread.IsBackground = true;
			thread.Start();
			thread.Join();
			return _imageData;
		}

		private void ProcessImage()
		{
			try
			{
				BitmapSource[] array = _renderFunction(_renderFunctionParameters);
				PngBitmapEncoder pngBitmapEncoder = new PngBitmapEncoder();
				BitmapSource[] array2 = array;
				foreach (BitmapSource bitmapSource in array2)
				{
					if (bitmapSource == null)
					{
						throw new ApplicationException("No image available.");
					}
					pngBitmapEncoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmapSource));
				}
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				pngBitmapEncoder.Save(memoryStream);
				_imageData = memoryStream.ToArray();
			}
			catch (Exception exception)
			{
				_exception = exception;
			}
			finally
			{
				try
				{
					Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.InvokeShutdown();
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class ProgressBoxCtrl : UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		[Flags]
		public enum ProgressBoxFormats
		{
			Prefix = 1,
			LowerBound = 2,
			Value = 4,
			UpperBound = 8,
			Percent = 0x10,
			DurationElapsed = 0x20,
			DurationToGo = 0x40,
			TimeStart = 0x80,
			TimeNow = 0x100,
			TimeEnd = 0x200,
			Suffix = 0x400
		}

		internal const ProgressBoxFormats _defaultFormat = ProgressBoxFormats.Prefix | ProgressBoxFormats.LowerBound | ProgressBoxFormats.Value | ProgressBoxFormats.UpperBound | ProgressBoxFormats.Suffix;

		private double _minimum;

		private double _maximum;

		private double _upperLimit = 1.0;

		private double _lowerLimit;

		private double _value;

		private string _suffix;

		private string _prefix;

		private double _actualLower;

		private double _actualUpper;

		private DateTime _started = DateTime.Now;

		private ProgressBoxFormats _format = ProgressBoxFormats.Prefix | ProgressBoxFormats.LowerBound | ProgressBoxFormats.Value | ProgressBoxFormats.UpperBound | ProgressBoxFormats.Suffix;

		private bool _enabled = true;

		internal Border UnderflowArea;

		internal Border DoneArea;

		internal Border ToDoArea;

		internal Border OverflowArea;

		internal TextBlock Prompt;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public double Minimum => _minimum;

		public double Maximum => _maximum;

		public double Upper
		{
			get
			{
				return _upperLimit;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _upperLimit)
				{
					_upperLimit = value;
					DoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double Lower
		{
			get
			{
				return _lowerLimit;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _lowerLimit)
				{
					_lowerLimit = value;
					DoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				return _format;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_format != value)
				{
					_format = value;
					DoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double Progress
		{
			get
			{
				return _value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == _actualLower)
				{
					_started = DateTime.Now;
				}
				if (_value != value)
				{
					_value = value;
					DoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public TimeSpan Duration => DateTime.Now - _started;

		public string Prefix
		{
			get
			{
				return _prefix;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_prefix != value)
				{
					_prefix = value;
					DoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public string Suffix
		{
			get
			{
				return _suffix;
			}
			set

				if (_suffix != value)
				{
					_suffix = value;
					DoChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Enabled
		{
			get
			{
				return _enabled;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_enabled != value)
				{
					_enabled = value;
					Rebuild();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler Changed;

		public ProgressBoxCtrl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public void Increment()
		{
			_value += 1.0;
			DoChanged();
		}

		private void UpdateRange()
		{
			_minimum = Math.Min(_actualLower = Math.Min(_upperLimit, _lowerLimit), _value);
			_maximum = Math.Max(_actualUpper = Math.Max(_upperLimit, _lowerLimit), _value);
		}

		private void DoChanged()
		{
			Rebuild();
			if (this.Changed != null)
			{
				this.Changed(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private bool IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats format)
		{
			return (_format & format) == format;
		}

		private string BuildControlText()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			string text = "N0";
			if (_actualUpper > 9.223372036854776E+18 || _actualLower < -9.223372036854776E+18 || Math.Truncate(_value) != _value)
			{
				text = "G4";
			}
			double num = _actualUpper - _actualLower;
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			TimeSpan timeSpan = now - _started;
			try
			{
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.Prefix) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_prefix))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(_prefix);
					stringBuilder.Append(' ');
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.LowerBound) && _actualLower != 0.0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(_actualLower.ToString(text, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
					stringBuilder.Append('/');
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.Value))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(_value.ToString(text, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.UpperBound) && _actualUpper != 0.0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append('/');
					stringBuilder.Append(_actualUpper.ToString(text, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture));
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.Percent) && num > 0.0)
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, (base.Width > 200.0) ? " {0:N1}%" : " {0:N0}%", 100.0 * (_value - _actualLower) / num);
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.DurationElapsed))
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(" -{0}", TimeSpan.FromSeconds(timeSpan.Seconds));
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.DurationToGo) && _value > _actualLower)
				{
					TimeSpan timeSpan2 = TimeSpan.FromSeconds((int)((double)timeSpan.Seconds * (_actualUpper - _value) / (_value - _actualLower)));
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(" +{0}", timeSpan2);
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.TimeStart))
				{
					if (now.DayOfYear != _started.DayOfYear)
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, " {0:g}»", _started);
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, " {0:T}»", _started);
					}
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.TimeNow))
				{
					if (!IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.TimeStart))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(' ');
					}
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:T}", now);
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.TimeEnd) && _value > _actualLower)
				{
					DateTime dateTime = _started + TimeSpan.FromTicks((long)((double)timeSpan.Ticks * num / (_value - _actualLower)));
					if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.TimeNow))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append('»');
					}
					else if (!IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.TimeStart))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(" »");
					}
					if (now.DayOfYear != dateTime.DayOfYear)
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:g}", dateTime);
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:T}", dateTime);
					}
				}
				if (IsFormat(ProgressBoxFormats.Suffix) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_suffix))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(" ");
					stringBuilder.Append(_suffix);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(" ???");
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString().Trim();
		}

		private new void SizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Rebuild();
		}

		private void Rebuild()
		{
			Visibility visibility;
			Visibility visibility3;
			Visibility visibility5;
			if (!_enabled)
			{
				Border underflowArea = UnderflowArea;
				Border doneArea = DoneArea;
				Border toDoArea = ToDoArea;
				visibility = (OverflowArea.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed);
				visibility3 = (toDoArea.Visibility = visibility);
				visibility5 = (doneArea.Visibility = visibility3);
				underflowArea.Visibility = visibility5;
				return;
			}
			UpdateRange();
			Prompt.Text = BuildControlText();
			double width = base.Width;
			double num = _maximum - _minimum;
			num = ((num != 0.0) ? (width / num) : width);
			double num2 = (_actualLower - _minimum) * num;
			double num3 = (_value - _minimum) * num;
			double num4 = (_actualUpper - _minimum) * num;
			UnderflowArea.Width = Math.Max(0.0, num2);
			DoneArea.Width = num3 - num2;
			ToDoArea.Width = num4 - num3;
			Border underflowArea2 = UnderflowArea;
			Border doneArea2 = DoneArea;
			Border toDoArea2 = ToDoArea;
			visibility = (OverflowArea.Visibility = Visibility.Visible);
			visibility3 = (toDoArea2.Visibility = visibility);
			visibility5 = (doneArea2.Visibility = visibility3);
			underflowArea2.Visibility = visibility5;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "5.0.17.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.Wpf;V31.0.0.5;component/progressboxcontrol.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "5.0.17.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((Grid)target).SizeChanged += SizeChanged;
				break;
			case 2:
				UnderflowArea = (Border)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				DoneArea = (Border)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				ToDoArea = (Border)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				OverflowArea = (Border)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				Prompt = (TextBlock)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public enum SortedCollectionDuplication
	{
		Allowed,
		Rejected,
		Ignored
	}
	public class SortedCollection<TValue> : IList<TValue>, ICollection<TValue>, IEnumerable<TValue>, IEnumerable
	{
		private List<TValue> _data;

		private IComparer<TValue> _comparer;

		private SortedCollectionDuplication _duplication = SortedCollectionDuplication.Ignored;

		public SortedCollectionDuplication Duplication => _duplication;

		public TValue this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return _data[index];
			}
			set
			{
				Add(value);
			}
		}

		public int Count => _data.Count;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public SortedCollection()
		{
			_data = new List<TValue>();
		}

		public SortedCollection(IComparer<TValue> comparer)
			: this()
		{
			if (comparer != null)
			{
				_comparer = comparer;
			}
		}

		public SortedCollection(SortedCollectionDuplication duplication)
			: this()
		{
			_duplication = duplication;
		}

		public SortedCollection(IComparer<TValue> comparer, SortedCollectionDuplication duplication)
			: this(comparer)
		{
			_duplication = duplication;
		}

		private int Find(TValue item)
		{
			if (_comparer == null)
			{
				return _data.BinarySearch(item);
			}
			return _data.BinarySearch(item, _comparer);
		}

		public void Sort()
		{
			if (_comparer == null)
			{
				_data.Sort();
			}
			else
			{
				_data.Sort(_comparer);
			}
		}

		public int IndexOf(TValue item)
		{
			int num = Find(item);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void Insert(int index, TValue item)
		{
			Add(item);
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			_data.RemoveAt(index);
		}

		public void Add(TValue item)
		{
			int num = Find(item);
			if (num < 0)
			{
				_data.Insert(~num, item);
				return;
			}
			switch (_duplication)
			{
			case SortedCollectionDuplication.Allowed:
				_data.Insert(num, item);
				break;
			case SortedCollectionDuplication.Rejected:
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("item");
			case SortedCollectionDuplication.Ignored:
				break;
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			_data.Clear();
		}

		public bool Contains(TValue item)
		{
			return Find(item) >= 0;
		}

		public void CopyTo(TValue[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			_data.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
		}

		public bool Remove(TValue item)
		{
			return _data.Remove(item);
		}

		public IEnumerator<TValue> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return _data.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return ((IEnumerable)_data).GetEnumerator();
		}
	}
	public class DropDownButton : ButtonBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DropDownContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DropDownContent", typeof(UIElement), typeof(DropDownButton), null);

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsDropDownOpenProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsDropDownOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(DropDownButton), new PropertyMetadata(false));

		private Popup _popUp;

		private Border _popUpBorder;

		private Border _buttonBorder;

		private DropShadowEffect _dropShadowEffect;

		public UIElement DropDownContent
		{
			get
			{
				return (UIElement)GetValue(DropDownContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DropDownContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsDropDownOpen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_popUp != null)
				{
					return _popUp.IsOpen;
				}
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					DisplayPopup();
				}
				else
				{
					CloseDropDown();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool HasDropShadow { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler<DropDownOpeningEventArgs> DropDownOpening;

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> DropDownClosed;

		public DropDownButton()
		{
			base.DefaultStyleKey = typeof(DropDownButton);
		}

		protected override void OnClick()
		{
			base.OnClick();
			IsDropDownOpen = !IsDropDownOpen;
		}

		private void DisplayPopup()
		{
			if (_dropShadowEffect == null)
			{
				_dropShadowEffect = new DropShadowEffect
				{
					Color = AspenCommonResources.Instance.AspenDarkBlueColour
				};
			}
			if (_popUp == null)
			{
				_popUp = new Popup();
				_popUp.PlacementTarget = this;
				_popUp.Closed += _popUp_Closed;
				_popUp.AllowsTransparency = true;
				_popUp.Placement = PlacementMode.Relative;
				_popUp.StaysOpen = false;
				DockPanel dockPanel = new DockPanel
				{
					LastChildFill = true
				};
				UIElementCollection children = dockPanel.Children;
				Border obj = new Border
				{
					Height = base.ActualHeight,
					Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White,
					BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0),
					Opacity = 0.01
				};
				Border element = obj;
				_buttonBorder = obj;
				children.Add(element);
				_buttonBorder.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown += _buttonBorder_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown;
				DockPanel.SetDock(_buttonBorder, Dock.Top);
				UIElementCollection children2 = dockPanel.Children;
				Border obj2 = new Border
				{
					Background = WpfSilverlightSupport.Make3DBrush(Colors.White),
					BorderBrush = WpfSilverlightSupport.Make3DBrush(Colors.Gray),
					BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.0),
					Padding = new Thickness(3.0),
					CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(0.0, 3.0, 3.0, 3.0)
				};
				element = obj2;
				_popUpBorder = obj2;
				children2.Add(element);
				_popUp.Child = dockPanel;
			}
			_popUpBorder.Effect = (HasDropShadow ? _dropShadowEffect : null);
			_popUpBorder.Margin = (HasDropShadow ? new Thickness(0.0, 0.0, 5.0, 5.0) : default(Thickness));
			DropDownOpeningEventArgs e = new DropDownOpeningEventArgs(DropDownContent);
			if (this.DropDownOpening != null)
			{
				this.DropDownOpening(this, e);
			}
			if (!e.Cancel && e.Content != null)
			{
				_popUpBorder.Child = e.Content;
				_popUp.IsOpen = true;
				SetValue(IsDropDownOpenProperty, true);
			}
		}

		private void _buttonBorder_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
			CloseDropDown();
		}

		private void _popUp_Closed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SetValue(IsDropDownOpenProperty, false);
			if (this.DropDownClosed != null)
			{
				this.DropDownClosed(this, e);
			}
		}

		public void CloseDropDown()
		{
			if (_popUp != null)
			{
				_popUp.IsOpen = false;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SplitButton : ButtonBase
	{
		private Button _contentButton;

		private Button _dropDownButton;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DropDownContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DropDownContent", typeof(UIElement), typeof(SplitButton), null);

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsDropDownOpenProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsDropDownOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(SplitButton), new PropertyMetadata(false));

		private Popup _popUp;

		private Border _popUpBorder;

		private Border _buttonBorder;

		public UIElement DropDownContent
		{
			get
			{
				return (UIElement)GetValue(DropDownContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DropDownContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsDropDownOpen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_popUp != null)
				{
					return _popUp.IsOpen;
				}
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					DisplayPopup();
				}
				else
				{
					CloseDropDown();
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<DropDownOpeningEventArgs> DropDownOpening;

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> DropDownClosed;

		public SplitButton()
		{
			base.DefaultStyleKey = typeof(SplitButton);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			_contentButton = GetTemplateChild("ContentButton") as Button;
			_dropDownButton = GetTemplateChild("DropDownButton") as Button;
			_contentButton.Click += _contentButton_Click;
			_dropDownButton.Click += _dropDownButton_Click;
		}

		private void _dropDownButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
			IsDropDownOpen = !IsDropDownOpen;
		}

		private void _contentButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
			OnClick();
		}

		private void DisplayPopup()
		{
			if (_popUp == null)
			{
				_popUp = new Popup();
				_popUp.PlacementTarget = this;
				_popUp.Closed += _popUp_Closed;
				_popUp.AllowsTransparency = true;
				_popUp.Placement = PlacementMode.Relative;
				_popUp.StaysOpen = false;
				DockPanel dockPanel = new DockPanel
				{
					LastChildFill = true
				};
				UIElementCollection children = dockPanel.Children;
				Border obj = new Border
				{
					Height = base.ActualHeight,
					Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White,
					BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0),
					Opacity = 0.01
				};
				Border element = obj;
				_buttonBorder = obj;
				children.Add(element);
				_buttonBorder.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown += _buttonBorder_MouseLeftButtonDown;
				DockPanel.SetDock(_buttonBorder, Dock.Top);
				UIElementCollection children2 = dockPanel.Children;
				Border obj2 = new Border
				{
					Background = WpfSilverlightSupport.Make3DBrush(Colors.White),
					BorderBrush = WpfSilverlightSupport.Make3DBrush(Colors.Gray),
					BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.0),
					Padding = new Thickness(3.0)
				};
				element = obj2;
				_popUpBorder = obj2;
				children2.Add(element);
				_popUp.Child = dockPanel;
			}
			DropDownOpeningEventArgs e = new DropDownOpeningEventArgs(DropDownContent);
			if (this.DropDownOpening != null)
			{
				this.DropDownOpening(this, e);
			}
			if (!e.Cancel && e.Content != null)
			{
				_popUpBorder.Child = e.Content;
				_popUp.IsOpen = true;
				SetValue(IsDropDownOpenProperty, true);
			}
		}

		private void _buttonBorder_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
			CloseDropDown();
		}

		private void _popUp_Closed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SetValue(IsDropDownOpenProperty, false);
			if (this.DropDownClosed != null)
			{
				this.DropDownClosed(this, e);
			}
		}

		public void CloseDropDown()
		{
			if (_popUp != null)
			{
				_popUp.IsOpen = false;
			}
		}
	}

		public bool Cancel { get; set; }

		internal DropDownOpeningEventArgs(UIElement content)
		{
			Content = content;
		}
	}

		{
			if (d is ColourPickerButton colourPickerButton)

				colourPickerButton._colourPalette.CurrentColour = (System.Windows.Media.Color)e.NewValue;
				colourPickerButton._colourPalette.SetSelectedColour((System.Windows.Media.Color)e.NewValue);
			}
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultColourProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultColour", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Color), typeof(ColourPickerButton), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ButtonContent", typeof(object), typeof(ColourPickerButton), null);

		public System.Windows.Media.Color SelectedColour
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Color)GetValue(SelectedColourProperty);
			}
			set

			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Color DefaultColour
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Color)GetValue(DefaultColourProperty);
			}
			set

			}
		}

		public object ButtonContent
		{

			}

			}

		public ColourPickerButton()
		{

			_colourPalette.SelectionChanged += _colourPalette_SelectionChanged;
			_colourPalette.MoreColoursClick += _colourPalette_MoreColoursClick;
		}

		private void _colourPalette_MoreColoursClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetSelectedColour(ColourPaletteDialog.ShowDialog(Window.GetWindow(this), _colourPalette.SelectedColour, _colourPalette.DefaultColour, expandFullPicker: true));
		}

		private void _colourPalette_SelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SetSelectedColour(_colourPalette.SelectedColour);
			CloseDropDown();
			OnClick();
		}

		private void SetSelectedColour(System.Windows.Media.Color colour)
		{
			SelectedColour = colour;
			CloseDropDown();
			OnClick();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			Binding binding = new Binding("DefaultColour");
			binding.Source = _colourPalette;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWayToSource;
			SetBinding(DefaultColourProperty, binding);
			_content = new StackPanel();
			Border obj = new Border
			{
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center
			};
			double height = (base.Width = double.NaN);
			obj.Height = height;
			obj.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0);
			ContentPresenter contentPresenter = (ContentPresenter)(obj.Child = new ContentPresenter());
			Border element = obj;
			binding = new Binding("ButtonContent");
			binding.Source = this;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			binding.Converter = new ObjectUIElementConverter();
			contentPresenter.SetBinding(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, binding);
			_content.Children.Add(element);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
			{
				Height = 4.0,
				Width = double.NaN,
				MinWidth = 15.0,
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
				Margin = new Thickness(0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0)
			};
			binding = new Binding("SelectedColour");
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			binding.Source = this;
			binding.Converter = new BrushColourConverter();
			rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, binding);
			_content.Children.Add(rectangle);
			base.DropDownContent = _colourPalette;
			base.Content = _content;
			_colourPalette.CurrentColour = SelectedColour;
			_colourPalette.SetSelectedColour(SelectedColour);
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("ButtonContent")]
	public class BrushPickerButton : SplitButton
	{

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedBrush", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(BrushPickerButton), new PropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is BrushPickerButton brushPickerButton)
			{
				brushPickerButton._fillPalette.CurrentFill = (System.Windows.Media.Brush)e.NewValue;
				brushPickerButton._fillPalette.SetSelectedFill((System.Windows.Media.Brush)e.NewValue);
			}
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultBrush", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(BrushPickerButton), null);

		public static DependencyProperty ButtonContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ButtonContent", typeof(object), typeof(BrushPickerButton), null);

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush SelectedBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Brush)GetValue(SelectedBrushProperty);

				return (System.Windows.Media.Brush)GetValue(DefaultBrushProperty);
			}

			}

		{
			get

				return GetValue(ButtonContentProperty);
			}
			set

			}
		}

		public BrushPickerButton()
		{
			_fillPalette = new FillPaletteControl();
			_fillPalette.SelectionChanged += _colourPalette_SelectionChanged;
			_fillPalette.MoreColoursClick += _fillPalette_MoreColoursClick;
		}

		private void _fillPalette_MoreColoursClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetSelectedBrush(FillPaletteDialog.ShowDialog(Window.GetWindow(this), _fillPalette.SelectedFill, _fillPalette.DefaultFill, expandFullPicker: true));
		}

		private void _colourPalette_SelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SetSelectedBrush(_fillPalette.SelectedFill);
		}

		private void SetSelectedBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			SelectedBrush = brush;
			CloseDropDown();
			OnClick();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			Binding binding = new Binding("DefaultFill");
			binding.Source = _fillPalette;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWayToSource;
			SetBinding(DefaultBrushProperty, binding);
			_content = new StackPanel();
			Border obj = new Border
			{
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center
			};
			double height = (base.Width = double.NaN);
			obj.Height = height;
			obj.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0);
			ContentPresenter contentPresenter = (ContentPresenter)(obj.Child = new ContentPresenter());
			Border element = obj;
			binding = new Binding("ButtonContent");
			binding.Source = this;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			binding.Converter = new ObjectUIElementConverter();
			contentPresenter.SetBinding(ContentPresenter.ContentProperty, binding);
			_content.Children.Add(element);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
			{
				Height = 4.0,
				Width = double.NaN,
				MinWidth = 15.0,
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center,
				Margin = new Thickness(0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0)
			};
			binding = new Binding("SelectedBrush");
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
			binding.Source = this;
			rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, binding);
			_content.Children.Add(rectangle);
			base.DropDownContent = _fillPalette;
			base.Content = _content;
			_fillPalette.CurrentFill = SelectedBrush;
			_fillPalette.SetSelectedFill(SelectedBrush);
		}
	}

	public class BrushPaletteButton : DropDownButton
	{
		private FillPaletteControl _fillPalette;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedBrush", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(BrushPaletteButton), new PropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(d is BrushPaletteButton { _ignoreColourPaletteSelectionChange: var ignoreColourPaletteSelectionChange } brushPaletteButton))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				brushPaletteButton._ignoreColourPaletteSelectionChange = true;
				brushPaletteButton._fillPalette.CurrentFill = (System.Windows.Media.Brush)e.NewValue;
				brushPaletteButton._fillPalette.SetSelectedFill((System.Windows.Media.Brush)e.NewValue);
			}
			finally
			{
				brushPaletteButton._ignoreColourPaletteSelectionChange = ignoreColourPaletteSelectionChange;
			}
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultBrush", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(BrushPaletteButton), null);

		private bool _ignoreColourPaletteSelectionChange;

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush SelectedBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Brush)GetValue(SelectedBrushProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedBrushProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush DefaultBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Brush)GetValue(DefaultBrushProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefaultBrushProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public BrushPaletteButton()
		{
			_fillPalette = new FillPaletteControl();
			_fillPalette.SelectionChanged += _colourPalette_SelectionChanged;
			_fillPalette.MoreColoursClick += _colourPalette_MoreColoursClick;

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			base.MinHeight = 0.0;
			Binding binding = new Binding("DefaultFill");
			binding.Source = _fillPalette;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWayToSource;
			SetBinding(DefaultBrushProperty, binding);
			if (base.Content == null)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
				{
					Height = 10.0,
					Width = 20.0
				};
				binding = new Binding("SelectedBrush");
				binding.Source = this;
				rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, binding);
				base.Content = new Border
				{
					BorderBrush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White,
					BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.0),
					CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(2.0),
					Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent,
					Child = rectangle,
					Margin = new Thickness(1.0)
				};
			}
			base.DropDownContent = _fillPalette;
		}

		private void _colourPalette_MoreColoursClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{

		}

		private void _colourPalette_SelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{

				SetSelectedBrush(_fillPalette.SelectedFill);
			}
		}

		{
			SelectedBrush = brush;

		}

	public static class StandardValues
	{
		public static readonly double MouseWheelZoomExponentFactor = 0.05;

		public static double MouseWheelZoomFactor(double mouseWheelDelta)
		{
			return Math.Exp(mouseWheelDelta * MouseWheelZoomExponentFactor);
		}
	}
	public static class Support
	{
		public static string Location(Assembly assembly)
		{
			if (assembly == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = assembly.CodeBase;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = assembly.Location;
			}
			if (text.StartsWith("file:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
			{
				text = text.Remove(0, 5);
				while (text.StartsWith(System.IO.Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || text.StartsWith(System.IO.Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar.ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
				{
					text = text.Remove(0, 1);
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public static void EnsureFolder(string folder)
		{
			if (!Directory.Exists(folder))
			{
				EnsureFolder(System.IO.Path.GetDirectoryName(folder));
				Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
			}
		}

		public static void DoEvents()
		{
			DispatcherFrame dispatcherFrame = new DispatcherFrame();
			Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, new DispatcherOperationCallback(ExitFrame), dispatcherFrame);
			Dispatcher.PushFrame(dispatcherFrame);
		}

		private static object ExitFrame(object f)
		{
			((DispatcherFrame)f).Continue = false;
			return null;
		}
	}
	public static class StringExtensions
	{
		public static bool Match(this string text, string compare)
		{
			return string.Compare(text, compare, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0;
		}
	}
	public class ThreadSafeDictionary<TKey, TValue> : IDictionary<TKey, TValue>, ICollection<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>, IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>, IEnumerable
	{
		private readonly object _sync = new object();

		private Dictionary<TKey, TValue> _dictionary = new Dictionary<TKey, TValue>();

		public ICollection<TKey> Keys
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_sync)
				{
					return _dictionary.Keys;
				}
			}
		}

		public ICollection<TValue> Values
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_sync)
				{
					return _dictionary.Values;
				}
			}
		}

		public TValue this[TKey key]
		{
			get
			{
				return _dictionary[key];
			}
			set
			{
				lock (_sync)
				{
					_dictionary[key] = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public int Count => _dictionary.Count;

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public void Add(TKey key, TValue value)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_dictionary.Add(key, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ContainsKey(TKey key)
		{
			return _dictionary.ContainsKey(key);
		}

		public bool Remove(TKey key)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return _dictionary.Remove(key);
			}
		}

		public bool TryGetValue(TKey key, out TValue value)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return _dictionary.TryGetValue(key, out value);
			}
		}

		public void Add(KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue> item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				((ICollection<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>)_dictionary).Add(item);
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_dictionary.Clear();
			}
		}

		public bool Contains(KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue> item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return ((ICollection<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>)_dictionary).Contains(item);
			}
		}

		public void CopyTo(KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				((ICollection<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>)_dictionary).CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
			}
		}

		public bool Remove(KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue> item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return ((ICollection<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>)_dictionary).Remove(item);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerator<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>> GetEnumerator()
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return ((IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<TKey, TValue>>)_dictionary).GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return ((IEnumerable)_dictionary).GetEnumerator();
			}
		}
	}
	public class ThreadSafeList<TValue> : IList<TValue>, ICollection<TValue>, IEnumerable<TValue>, IEnumerable
	{
		private readonly object _sync = new object();

		private List<TValue> _list = new List<TValue>();

		public TValue this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_sync)
				{
					return _list[index];
				}
			}
			set
			{
				lock (_sync)
				{
					_list[index] = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public int Count
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_sync)
				{
					return _list.Count;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		public int IndexOf(TValue item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return _list.IndexOf(item);
			}
		}

		public void Insert(int index, TValue item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_list.Insert(index, item);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_list.RemoveAt(index);
			}
		}

		public void Add(TValue item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_list.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_list.Clear();
			}
		}

		public bool Contains(TValue item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return _list.Contains(item);
			}
		}

		public void CopyTo(TValue[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				_list.CopyTo(array, arrayIndex);
			}
		}

		public bool Remove(TValue item)
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return _list.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerator<TValue> GetEnumerator()
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return ((IEnumerable<TValue>)_list).GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			lock (_sync)
			{
				return ((IEnumerable)_list).GetEnumerator();
			}
		}
	}
	public static class TimeCriticalLog
	{
		private static List<string> _info = new List<string>();

		public static void Log(string message)
		{
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t{1:HH:mm:ss.fff}\t{2}", now.Ticks, now, message);
			lock (_info)
			{
				_info.Add(message);
			}
		}

		public static void Log(string format, params object[] data)
		{
			Log(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, format, data));
		}

		private static string Text()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			lock (_info)
			{
				foreach (string item in _info)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(item);
					stringBuilder.Append("\r\n");
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static void CopyToClipboard()
		{
			Clipboard.SetText(Text());
		}

		public static void CopyToFile(string fileName)
		{
			File.WriteAllText(fileName, Text());
		}

		public static void Clear()
		{
			lock (_info)
			{
				_info.Clear();
			}
		}
	}
	public static class Utilities
	{
		public static DependencyObject GetDescendantFromType(DependencyObject parent, Type type, bool allowSubclassOfType)
		{
			return GetDescendantFromType<DependencyObject>(parent, type, allowSubclassOfType, null, null);
		}

		public static T GetDescendantFromType<T>(DependencyObject parent, bool allowSubclassOfType, Func<T, bool> callback) where T : DependencyObject
		{
			return GetDescendantFromType(parent, allowSubclassOfType, callback, null);
		}

		public static T GetDescendantFromType<T>(DependencyObject parent, bool allowSubclassOfType, Func<T, bool> callback, Type[] typesToIgnore) where T : DependencyObject
		{
			return GetDescendantFromType(parent, typeof(T), allowSubclassOfType, callback, typesToIgnore);
		}

		private static T GetDescendantFromType<T>(DependencyObject parent, Type type, bool allowSubclassOfType, Func<T, bool> callback, Type[] typesToIgnore) where T : DependencyObject
		{
			int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent);
			if (childrenCount >= 1)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++)
				{
					if (VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, i) is T val)
					{
						Type type2 = val.GetType();
						if ((type2 == type || (allowSubclassOfType && type.IsAssignableFrom(type2))) && (callback == null || callback(val)))
						{
							return val;
						}
					}
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < childrenCount; j++)
				{
					DependencyObject child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, j);
					if (child != null && (typesToIgnore == null || !ContainsType(typesToIgnore, child.GetType())))
					{
						child = GetDescendantFromType(child, type, allowSubclassOfType, callback, typesToIgnore);
						if (child != null)
						{
							return (T)child;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static bool ContainsType(Type[] types, Type typeToLocate)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < types.Length; i++)
			{
				if (typeToLocate == types[i] || types[i].IsAssignableFrom(typeToLocate))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public abstract class ViewModelBase : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _propertyChangedEventHandler;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_propertyChangedEventHandler = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(_propertyChangedEventHandler, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_propertyChangedEventHandler = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(_propertyChangedEventHandler, value);
			}
		}

		protected void RaisePropertyChanged(string property)
		{
			_propertyChangedEventHandler?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(property));
		}
	}
	public class WaitingControl : UserControl, IWaitingControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private readonly int _number = 15;

		private readonly int _circleSize = 5;

		private bool _active = true;

		private string _caption;

		private Dictionary<object, string> _pending = new Dictionary<object, string>();

		private object _syncLock = new object();

		internal WaitingControl GridParent;

		internal Grid LayoutRoot;

		internal TextBlock WorkingMessage;

		internal Canvas AnimationCanvas;

		internal RotateTransform Rotation;

		internal Storyboard RotationStoryboard;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string Caption
		{
			get
			{
				return _caption;
			}
			set
			{
				_caption = value;
				Rebuild();
			}
		}

		public bool Active
		{
			get
			{
				return _active;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_active == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_active = value;
				if (_active)
				{
					base.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					RotationStoryboard.Resume();
					if (!(base.Parent is Panel panel))
					{
						return;
					}
					int num = -1;
					foreach (object child in panel.Children)
					{
						if (child != this)
						{
							num = Math.Max(num, Panel.GetZIndex(child as UIElement));
						}
					}
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						Panel.SetZIndex(this, num + 1);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					base.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					RotationStoryboard.Pause();
				}
			}
		}

		public WaitingControl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			System.Windows.Media.Color color = (System.Windows.Media.Color)base.Resources["AspenGoldColour"];
			System.Windows.Media.Color color2 = (System.Windows.Media.Color)base.Resources["AspenGreenColour"];
			double num = AnimationCanvas.Width / 2.0 - (double)(_circleSize / 2);
			double num2 = AnimationCanvas.Width / 2.0 - (double)(_circleSize / 2);
			double num3 = Math.PI * 2.0 / (double)_number;
			for (int i = 0; i < _number; i++)
			{
				Ellipse element = new Ellipse
				{
					Height = _circleSize,
					Width = _circleSize,
					Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(new System.Windows.Media.Color
					{
						A = (byte)((color.A * i + color2.A * (_number - 1 - i)) / _number),
						R = (byte)((color.R * i + color2.R * (_number - 1 - i)) / _number),
						G = (byte)((color.G * i + color2.G * (_number - 1 - i)) / _number),
						B = (byte)((color.B * i + color2.B * (_number - 1 - i)) / _number)
					})
				};
				Canvas.SetLeft(element, num - num2 * Math.Cos((double)i * num3));
				Canvas.SetTop(element, num + num2 * Math.Sin((double)i * num3));
				AnimationCanvas.Children.Add(element);
			}
			RotationStoryboard.Begin();
		}

		public object AddMessage(string message)
		{
			object obj = new object();
			lock (_syncLock)
			{
				_pending.Add(obj, message);
			}
			Rebuild();
			return obj;
		}

		public void RemoveMessage(object tag)
		{
			lock (_syncLock)
			{
				if (tag == null)
				{
					_pending.Clear();
				}
				else
				{
					_pending.Remove(tag);
				}
			}
			Rebuild();
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			RemoveMessage(null);
			Caption = null;
		}

		private void Rebuild()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(_caption);
			lock (_syncLock)
			{
				foreach (string value in _pending.Values)
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("\r\n");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
				}
			}
			WorkingMessage.Text = stringBuilder.ToString();
			((IWaitingControl)this).Active = stringBuilder.Length > 0;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "5.0.17.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.Wpf;V31.0.0.5;component/waitingcontrol.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "5.0.17.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				GridParent = (WaitingControl)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				LayoutRoot = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				WorkingMessage = (TextBlock)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				AnimationCanvas = (Canvas)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				Rotation = (RotateTransform)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				RotationStoryboard = (Storyboard)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	{
		string Caption { get; set; }

		bool Active { get; set; }

		object AddMessage(string message);

		void RemoveMessage(object tag);

		void Clear();
	}

			public static SolidColorBrush Black => MakeBrush(Colors.Black);

			public static SolidColorBrush Blue => MakeBrush(Colors.Blue);

			public static SolidColorBrush Brown => MakeBrush(Colors.Brown);

			public static SolidColorBrush Cyan => MakeBrush(Colors.Cyan);

			public static SolidColorBrush DarkGray => MakeBrush(Colors.DarkGray);

			public static SolidColorBrush Gray => MakeBrush(Colors.Gray);

			public static SolidColorBrush Green => MakeBrush(Colors.Green);

			public static SolidColorBrush LightGray => MakeBrush(Colors.LightGray);

			public static SolidColorBrush Magenta => MakeBrush(Colors.Magenta);

			public static SolidColorBrush Orange => MakeBrush(Colors.Orange);

			public static SolidColorBrush Purple => MakeBrush(Colors.Purple);

			public static SolidColorBrush Red => MakeBrush(Colors.Red);

			public static SolidColorBrush Transparent => MakeBrush(Colors.Transparent);

			public static SolidColorBrush White => MakeBrush(Colors.White);

			public static SolidColorBrush Yellow => MakeBrush(Colors.Yellow);

			private static SolidColorBrush MakeBrush(System.Windows.Media.Color color)
			{
				SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush(color);
				solidColorBrush.Freeze();
				return solidColorBrush;
			}
		}

		private static Panel _rootVisualPanel;

		public static Panel RootVisualPanel
		{
			get
			{
				return _rootVisualPanel;
			}
			set
			{
				_rootVisualPanel = value;

		public static bool IsControlKeyDown => (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) == ModifierKeys.Control;

		public static bool IsShiftKeyDown => (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) == ModifierKeys.Shift;

		public static Grid AddModalBlockingGrid()
		{
			Grid grid = new Grid
			{
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch,
				Background = Brushes.Transparent
			};
			AddChildAtFront(RootVisualPanel, grid);
			return grid;
		}

		public static bool RemoveModalBlockingGrid(Grid grid)
		{
			return RemoveChild(RootVisualPanel, grid);
		}

		public static bool AddChildAtFront(Panel parent, FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (parent != null && child != null)
			{
				if (parent is Grid grid)
				{
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(child, Math.Max(1, grid.ColumnDefinitions.Count));
					Grid.SetRowSpan(child, Math.Max(1, grid.RowDefinitions.Count));
				}
				parent.Children.Add(child);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool RemoveChild(Panel parent, FrameworkElement child)
		{
			if (parent != null && child != null && parent.Children.Contains(child))
			{
				parent.Children.Remove(child);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Brush Make3DBrush(System.Windows.Media.Color color)
		{
			return Make3DBrush(color, Orientation.Vertical);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Brush Make3DBrush(System.Windows.Media.Color color, Orientation orientation)
		{
			return Make3DBrush(color, orientation, graduatedTransparency: false);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Brush Make3DBrush(System.Windows.Media.Color color, Orientation orientation, bool graduatedTransparency)
		{
			LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush();
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb((byte)(graduatedTransparency ? 75 : color.A), (byte)(0.5 * (double)(int)color.R + 127.5), (byte)(0.5 * (double)(int)color.G + 127.5), (byte)(0.5 * (double)(int)color.B + 127.5)),
				Offset = 0.0
			});
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb((byte)(graduatedTransparency ? 200 : color.A), (byte)(0.95 * (double)(int)color.R + 12.75), (byte)(0.95 * (double)(int)color.G + 12.75), (byte)(0.95 * (double)(int)color.B + 12.75)),
				Offset = 0.49
			});
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb((byte)(graduatedTransparency ? 225 : color.A), color.R, color.G, color.B),
				Offset = 0.51
			});
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(graduatedTransparency ? byte.MaxValue : color.A, (byte)(0.9 * (double)(int)color.R + 1.6), (byte)(0.9 * (double)(int)color.G + 1.6), (byte)(0.9 * (double)(int)color.B + 1.6)),
				Offset = 1.0
			});
			if (orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.5);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(1.0, 0.5);
			}
			else
			{
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 1.0);
			}
			return linearGradientBrush;
		}

		public static void CollectionRemoveRange<T>(IList<T> collection, int index, int count)
		{
			if (collection == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("collection");
			}
			while (count > 0 && collection.Count > index)
			{
				collection.RemoveAt(index);
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Size GetTextSize(TextBlock textBlock)
		{
			textBlock.UpdateLayout();
			return textBlock.DesiredSize;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point TranslatePoint(UIElement fromElement, UIElement toElement, System.Windows.Point fromPoint)
		{
			try
			{
				if (fromElement != null && toElement != null)
				{
					return fromElement.TransformToVisual(toElement).Transform(fromPoint);
				}
				return fromPoint;
			}
			catch
			{
				return fromPoint;
			}
		}

		public static int IndexOfItem(ItemCollection items, object item)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i++)
			{
				if (items[i].Equals(item))
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		public static void AnimateProperty(double from, double to, TimeSpan duration, DependencyObject target, DependencyProperty property)
		{
			Storyboard storyboard = new Storyboard();
			DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation
			{
				Duration = new Duration(duration),
				From = from,
				To = to
			};
			storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
			Storyboard.SetTarget(doubleAnimation, target);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(property));
			storyboard.Begin();
		}
	}

	public static class XmlSupport
	{
		public static System.Windows.Media.Color ColourFromXml(string value)
		{
			byte b = byte.MaxValue;
			byte b2 = byte.MaxValue;
			byte b3 = byte.MaxValue;
			if (value.Length == 9 && value[0] == '#')
			{
				value = value.Substring(value.Length - 8);
				byte a = System.Convert.ToByte(value.Substring(0, 2), 16);
				value = value.Substring(2);
				b = System.Convert.ToByte(value.Substring(0, 2), 16);
				value = value.Substring(2);
				b2 = System.Convert.ToByte(value.Substring(0, 2), 16);
				value = value.Substring(2);
				b3 = System.Convert.ToByte(value.Substring(0, 2), 16);
				return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(a, b, b2, b3);
			}
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(Colors);
			if (typeFromHandle.GetProperty(value) != null)
			{
				object obj = typeFromHandle.InvokeMember(value, BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, null, null);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return (System.Windows.Media.Color)obj;
				}
			}
			return Colors.Black;
		}

		public static object GetInstance(string type, string value)
		{
			switch (type)
			{
			case "System.Boolean":
				return XmlConvert.ToBoolean(value);
			case "System.Byte":
				return XmlConvert.ToByte(value);
			case "System.Char":
				return XmlConvert.ToChar(value);
			case "System.DateTime":
				return XmlConvert.ToDateTime(value, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc);
			case "System.Decimal":
				return XmlConvert.ToDecimal(value);
			case "System.Double":
				return XmlConvert.ToDouble(value);
			case "System.Guid":
				return XmlConvert.ToGuid(value);
			case "System.Int16":
				return XmlConvert.ToInt16(value);
			case "System.Int32":
			case "System.Int":
				return XmlConvert.ToInt32(value);
			case "System.Int64":
				return XmlConvert.ToInt64(value);
			case "System.SByte":
				return XmlConvert.ToSByte(value);
			case "System.Single":
				return XmlConvert.ToSingle(value);
			case "System.String":
				return value;
			case "System.TimeSpan":
				return XmlConvert.ToTimeSpan(value);
			case "System.UInt16":
				return XmlConvert.ToUInt16(value);
			case "System.UInt32":
				return XmlConvert.ToUInt32(value);
			case "System.UInt64":
				return XmlConvert.ToUInt64(value);
			default:
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static string ToXml(object value)
		{
			if (value is bool)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((bool)value);
			}
			if (value is byte)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((byte)value);
			}
			if (value is char)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((char)value);
			}
			if (value is DateTime)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((DateTime)value, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc);
			}
			if (value is decimal)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((decimal)value);
			}
			if (value is double)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((double)value);
			}
			if (value is Guid)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((Guid)value);
			}
			if (value is short)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((short)value);
			}
			if (value is int)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((int)value);
			}
			if (value is long)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((long)value);
			}
			if (value is sbyte)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((sbyte)value);
			}
			if (value is float)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((float)value);
			}
			if (value is string)
			{
				return (string)value;
			}
			if (value is TimeSpan)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((TimeSpan)value);
			}
			if (value is ushort)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((ushort)value);
			}
			if (value is uint)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((uint)value);
			}
			if (value is ulong)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((ulong)value);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static void WriteBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush, XmlWriter writer)
		{
			if (brush is SolidColorBrush || brush is GradientBrush)
			{
				writer.WriteRaw(SilverlightContrib.Xaml.XamlWriter.Save(brush));
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Markup.XamlWriter.Save(brush, writer);
			}
		}

		public static XElement WriteBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			XElement xElement = null;
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			using (XmlWriter writer = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream))
			{
				WriteBrush(brush, writer);
			}
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			using XmlReader reader = XmlReader.Create(memoryStream);
			return XElement.Load(reader);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Brush ReadBrush(XElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = element.Name.LocalName.ToLowerInvariant();
			switch (text)
			{
			case "solidcolorbrush":
			case "lineargradientbrush":
			case "radialgradientbrush":
			{
				System.Windows.Media.Brush brush = null;
				GradientBrush gradientBrush;
				switch (text)
				{
				case "solidcolorbrush":
					brush = new SolidColorBrush
					{
						Color = ColourFromXml(element.AttributeValue("Color", "#FFFFFFFF"))
					};
					break;
				case "lineargradientbrush":
					gradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush
					{
						StartPoint = Convert(element.AttributeValue("StartPoint"), default(System.Windows.Point)),
						EndPoint = Convert(element.AttributeValue("EndPoint"), default(System.Windows.Point))
					};
					goto IL_01ae;
				case "radialgradientbrush":
					gradientBrush = new RadialGradientBrush
					{
						Center = Convert(element.AttributeValue("Center"), new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5)),
						GradientOrigin = Convert(element.AttributeValue("GradientOrigin"), new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5)),
						RadiusX = Convert(element.AttributeValue("RadiusX"), 0.5),
						RadiusY = Convert(element.AttributeValue("RadiusY"), 0.5)
					};
					goto IL_01ae;
				default:
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Brush type not recognised", "brush");
					}
					IL_01ae:
					gradientBrush.ColorInterpolationMode = (ColorInterpolationMode)Enum.Parse(typeof(ColorInterpolationMode), element.AttributeValue("ColorInterpolationMode", ColorInterpolationMode.SRgbLinearInterpolation.ToString()), ignoreCase: true);
					gradientBrush.MappingMode = (BrushMappingMode)Enum.Parse(typeof(BrushMappingMode), element.AttributeValue("MappingMode", BrushMappingMode.RelativeToBoundingBox.ToString()), ignoreCase: true);
					gradientBrush.SpreadMethod = (GradientSpreadMethod)Enum.Parse(typeof(GradientSpreadMethod), element.AttributeValue("SpreadMethod", GradientSpreadMethod.Pad.ToString()), ignoreCase: true);
					foreach (XElement item in element.Elements(element.Name.Namespace + "GradientStop"))
					{
						gradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
						{
							Color = ColourFromXml(item.AttributeValue("Color", "#FFFFFFFF")),
							Offset = Convert(item.AttributeValue("Offset"), 0.0)
						});
					}
					brush = gradientBrush;
					break;
				}
				brush.Opacity = Convert(element.AttributeValue("Opacity"), 1.0);
				return brush;
			}
			default:
			{
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
				xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
				using (XmlWriter writer = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream, xmlWriterSettings))
				{
					element.WriteTo(writer);
				}
				memoryStream.Position = 0L;
				return (System.Windows.Media.Brush)XamlReader.Load(memoryStream);
			}
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point Convert(string value, System.Windows.Point defaultValue)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			try
			{
				string[] array = value.Split(',');
				return new System.Windows.Point(double.Parse(array[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), double.Parse(array[1], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static bool Convert(string value, bool defaultValue)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))

				return defaultValue;
			}
			try
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToBoolean(value);
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static XPathNavigator StringToXPathNavigator(string xml)
		{
			XmlReaderSettings xmlReaderSettings = new XmlReaderSettings();
			xmlReaderSettings.ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Auto;
			using StringReader input = new StringReader(xml);
			using XmlReader reader = XmlReader.Create(input, xmlReaderSettings);
			return new XPathDocument(reader).CreateNavigator();
		}

		public static string XmlToString(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
			using (XmlWriter w = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, xmlWriterSettings))
			{
				doc.WriteTo(w);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string XmlToString(XPathNavigator doc)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
			using (XmlWriter writer = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder, xmlWriterSettings))
			{
				doc.WriteSubtree(writer);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}

		private static readonly string GLOBAL_PREFIX = "$global|";

		private static int[] Crc16Table = new int[16]
		{
			0, 52225, 55297, 5120, 61441, 15360, 10240, 58369, 40961, 27648,
			30720, 46081, 20480, 39937, 34817, 17408
		};

		public static LocalSupport GetAssemblyLocalSupport(LocalisationBehaviour behaviour, Assembly assembly)
		{
			switch (behaviour)
			{
			case LocalisationBehaviour.Self:
				return Create(assembly);
			case LocalisationBehaviour.Default:
				return GetMyLocalSupport();
			case LocalisationBehaviour.LocaliseTextCallbackOnly:
				if (SingleAssembly != null)
				{
					return Create(assembly, behaviour);
				}
				return Create(assembly);
			default:
				return null;
			}
		}

		private static LocalSupport Create(Assembly assembly)
		{
			return Create(assembly, LocalisationBehaviour.Self);
		}

		private static LocalSupport Create(Assembly assembly, LocalisationBehaviour behaviour)
		{
			AssemblyTranslations value;
			lock (_assemblies)
			{
				if (behaviour != LocalisationBehaviour.Self)
				{
					value = null;
				}
				else if (!_assemblies.TryGetValue(assembly, out value))
				{
					value = new AssemblyTranslations(DownloadTranslation(LoadPath(assembly, null)) ?? new Translations());
					_assemblies.Add(assembly, value);
				}
			}
			return new LocalSupport(assembly, value);
		}

		private LocalSupport(Assembly assembly, AssemblyTranslations assemblyData)
		{
			_assembly = assembly;
			_assemblyTranslations = assemblyData;
		}

		public static void Save()
		{
			if (!BuildResourceFile)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Assembly, AssemblyTranslations> assembly in _assemblies)
			{
				if (!assembly.Value.IsDirty)
				{
					continue;
				}
				XElement xElement = new XElement(_xmlRootElementName);
				XDocument xDocument = new XDocument(xElement);
				Translations master = assembly.Value.Master;
				List<string> list = new List<string>(master.Keys);
				list.Sort(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
				string text = null;
				XElement xElement2 = null;
				foreach (string item in list)
				{
					int num = item.IndexOf('|');
					if (text != item.Substring(0, num))
					{
						text = item.Substring(0, num);
						xElement2 = new XElement(_xmlClassElementName, new XAttribute(_xmlIdAttributeName, text));
						xElement.Add(xElement2);
					}
					string text2 = master[item];
					XElement xElement3 = TryXml(text2);
					if (xElement3 != null)
					{
						xElement2.Add(new XElement(_xmlItemElementName, new XAttribute(_xmlIdAttributeName, item.Substring(num + 1)), xElement3));
					}
					else
					{
						xElement2.Add(new XElement(_xmlItemElementName, new XAttribute(_xmlIdAttributeName, item.Substring(num + 1)), new XAttribute(_xmlTextAttributeName, text2)));
					}
				}
				XmlWriterSettings settings = new XmlWriterSettings
				{
					ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Document,
					Encoding = Encoding.UTF8,
					Indent = true,
					CloseOutput = true
				};
				using (XmlWriter writer = XmlWriter.Create(LoadPath(assembly.Key, null), settings))
				{
					xDocument.Save(writer);
				}
				assembly.Value.IsDirty = false;
			}
		}

		public static string BuildId(string defaultValue)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(defaultValue))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("defaultValue");
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = true;
			foreach (char c in defaultValue)
			{
				if (char.IsLetterOrDigit(c))
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(char.ToUpper(c, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(char.ToLower(c, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
					}
				}
				else
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static int HashId(string text)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("text");
			}
			int num = 65535;
			string text2 = text.ToUpper(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			for (int i = 0; i < text2.Length; i++)
			{
				byte b = Convert.ToByte(text2[i]);
				num = ((num >> 4) & 0xFFF) ^ Crc16Table[num & 0xF] ^ Crc16Table[b & 0xF];
				num = ((num >> 4) & 0xFFF) ^ Crc16Table[num & 0xF] ^ Crc16Table[(b >> 4) & 0xF];
			}
			return num;
		}

		private string Get(CultureInfo culture, string resourceName, string defaultValue)
		{
			string value = null;
			if (BuildResourceFile && defaultValue != null && _assemblyTranslations != null && !_assemblyTranslations.Master.ContainsKey(resourceName))
			{
				_assemblyTranslations.Master.Add(resourceName, defaultValue);
				_assemblyTranslations.IsDirty = true;
			}
			List<Translations> translation = GetTranslation(culture);
			for (int num = translation.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (translation[num].TryGetValue(resourceName, out value))
				{
					if (!CheckInternationalisation)
					{
						break;
					}
					return PseudoLocalise(string.IsNullOrEmpty(defaultValue) ? value : defaultValue);
				}
			}
			EventHandler<LocaliseTextEventArgs> localiseText = LocalSupport.LocaliseText;
			if (localiseText != null)
			{
				LocaliseTextEventArgs e = new LocaliseTextEventArgs(culture, resourceName, value, defaultValue);
				localiseText(this, e);
				if (e.Handled)
				{
					value = e.Text;
				}
			}
			if (value != null)
			{
				return value;
			}
			EventHandler<LocalisationNotFoundEventArgs> localisationNotFound = LocalSupport.LocalisationNotFound;
			if (localisationNotFound != null)
			{
				if (_problems == null)
				{
					_problems = new List<string>();
				}
				if (!_problems.Contains(resourceName))
				{
					_problems.Add(resourceName);
					localisationNotFound(this, new LocalisationNotFoundEventArgs(resourceName, defaultValue));
				}
			}
			return defaultValue;
		}

		public string Get(CultureInfo culture, string tag, string resourceName, string defaultValue)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tag))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("tag");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resourceName))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("resourceName");
			}
			return Get(culture, tag + "|" + resourceName, defaultValue);
		}

		public string Localise(CultureInfo culture, object rootParent, string id, string defaultValue)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(id))
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("id");
			}
			if (rootParent == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("rootParent");
			}
			return Get(culture, ((rootParent is Type) ? rootParent.ToString() : rootParent.GetType().ToString()) + "|" + id, defaultValue);
		}

		public void Localise(CultureInfo culture, FrameworkElement root, FrameworkElement element, DependencyProperty property, string id)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (property == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("property");
			}
			if (element.GetValue(property) is string defaultValue)
			{
				if (id == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("id");
				}
				if (root == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("root");
				}
				element.SetValue(property, Get(culture, root.GetType().ToString() + '|' + element.Name + "." + id, defaultValue));
			}
		}

		public void Localise(CultureInfo culture, FrameworkElement root, TextBox textBox)
		{
			if (textBox != null)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBox.Text))
				{
					Localise(culture, root, textBox, TextBox.TextProperty, "Text");
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBox.GetValue(ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty) as string))
				{
					Localise(culture, root, textBox, ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty, "ToolTip");
				}
			}
		}

		public void Localise(FrameworkElement root, TextBlock textBlock)
		{
			Localise(DefaultCulture, root, textBlock);
		}

		public void Localise(CultureInfo culture, FrameworkElement root, TextBlock textBlock)
		{
			if (textBlock == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBlock.GetValue(ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty) as string))
			{
				Localise(culture, root, textBlock, ToolTipService.ToolTipProperty, "ToolTip");
			}
			int num = 0;
			if (textBlock.Inlines != null)
			{
				foreach (Inline inline in textBlock.Inlines)
				{
					if (inline is Run run && Localise(culture, root, run))
					{
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num == 0 && textBlock.Inlines.Count == 1 && textBlock.Inlines.FirstInline is Run && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(textBlock.Text))
			{
				Localise(culture, root, textBlock, TextBlock.TextProperty, "Text");
			}
		}

		public bool Localise(CultureInfo culture, FrameworkElement root, Run run)
		{
			if (run != null)
			{
				if (root == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("root");
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(run.Text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(run.Name))
				{
					run.Text = Get(culture, root.GetType().ToString() + '|' + run.Name + ".Text", run.Text);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public string NoChange(string text)
		{
			return text;
		}

		private static XElement TryXml(string x)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x) || !x.StartsWith("<") || !x.EndsWith(">"))
			{
				return null;
			}
			try
			{
				return XElement.Parse(x);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static string PseudoLocalise(string text)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(text);
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				bool flag = true;
				for (int num = stringBuilder.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					if (!flag)
					{
						if (stringBuilder[num] == '<')
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						switch (stringBuilder[num])
						{
						case '>':
							flag = false;
							break;
						case 'A':
						case 'E':
						case 'I':
						case 'O':
						case 'U':
						case 'a':
						case 'e':
						case 'i':
						case 'o':
						case 'u':
						{
							char c = stringBuilder[num] switch
							{
								'a' => 'â', 
								'A' => 'Â', 
								'e' => 'é', 
								'E' => 'É', 
								'i' => 'í', 
								'I' => 'Í', 
								'o' => 'ó', 
								'O' => 'Ó', 
								'u' => 'ü', 
								'U' => 'Ü', 
								_ => stringBuilder[num], 
							};
							if (text2.IndexOf(stringBuilder[num]) < 0)
							{
								text2 += stringBuilder[num];
								int index = num;
								char value = (stringBuilder[num] = c);
								stringBuilder.Insert(index, value);
							}
							else
							{
								stringBuilder[num] = c;
							}
							break;
						}
						case 'n':
							stringBuilder[num] = 'ñ';
							break;
						case 'N':
							stringBuilder[num] = 'Ñ';
							break;
						case 'c':
							stringBuilder[num] = 'ç';
							break;
						case 'C':
							stringBuilder[num] = 'Ç';
							break;
						case '!':
						case ':':
						case '?':
							if (num > 0 && stringBuilder[num - 1] != ' ')
							{
								stringBuilder.Insert(num, ' ');
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (text[0] != '<')
				{
					stringBuilder.Insert(0, '≤');
					stringBuilder.Append('≥');
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return text;
		}

		private static string LoadPath(Assembly assembly, string cultureId)
		{
			string text = assembly.CodeBase;
			if (text.StartsWith("file:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
			{
				text = text.Substring(5);
				while (text.Length > 0 && text[0] == '/')
				{
					text = text.Substring(1);
				}
				text = text.Replace('/', '\\');
			}
			int num = text.LastIndexOf('.');
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cultureId))
			{
				return text.Substring(0, num) + ".atres.xml";
			}
			return text.Substring(0, num + 1) + cultureId + ".atres.xml";
		}

		private Translations GetTranslation(string cultureId)
		{
			if (_assemblyTranslations == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Translations value = null;
			if (_assemblyTranslations.Other.TryGetValue(cultureId, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			value = DownloadTranslation(LoadPath(_assembly, cultureId));
			if (value == null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(cultureId) && BuildResourceFile)
			{
				value = new Translations();
			}
			lock (_assemblyTranslations)
			{
				_assemblyTranslations.Other[cultureId] = value;
				return value;
			}
		}

		private static Translations DownloadTranslation(string path)
		{
			XElement xElement = null;
			if (File.Exists(path))
			{
				xElement = XDocument.Load(path).Root;
			}
			if (xElement != null)
			{
				Translations translations = new Translations();
				{
					foreach (XElement item in xElement.Elements(_xmlClassElementName))
					{
						foreach (XElement item2 in item.Elements(_xmlItemElementName))
						{
							if (item2.Attribute(_xmlTextAttributeName) == null)
							{
								if (item2.HasElements)
								{
									translations.Add(item.Attribute(_xmlIdAttributeName).Value + "|" + item2.Attribute(_xmlIdAttributeName).Value, item2.Elements().First().ToString(SaveOptions.DisableFormatting));
								}
							}
							else
							{
								translations.Add(item.Attribute(_xmlIdAttributeName).Value + "|" + item2.Attribute(_xmlIdAttributeName).Value, item2.Attribute(_xmlTextAttributeName).Value);
							}
						}
					}
					return translations;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private List<Translations> GetTranslation(CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (_assemblyTranslations == null)
			{
				return new List<Translations>();
			}
			List<Translations> list = new List<Translations>(4);
			list.Add(_assemblyTranslations.Master);
			if (culture == null)
			{
				culture = CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture;
			}
			while (culture != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(culture.Name))
			{
				Translations translation = GetTranslation(culture.Name);
				if (translation != null)
				{
					list.Insert(1, translation);
				}
				culture = culture.Parent;
			}
			return list;
		}

			Id = id;
			Default = defaultValue;
		}
	}

		public string Text { get; set; }

		public bool Handled { get; set; }

		public LocaliseTextEventArgs(CultureInfo culture, string id, string text, string defaultValue)
		{
			Culture = culture;
			Id = id;
			Text = text;
			Default = defaultValue;
		}
