	public static class VisualisationControlsLocalisation
	{
		private static LocalisationBehaviour _behaviour = LocalisationBehaviour.Self;

		public static LocalisationBehaviour Behaviour
		{
			get
			{
				return _behaviour;
			}
			set
			{
				_behaviour = value;
			}
		}

		public static string LocaliseDefault(LocalSupport local, object root, string text)
		{
			if (local == null)
			{
				return text;
			}
			return local.LocaliseDefault(root, text);
		}
	}
	public enum LocalisationBehaviour
	{
		None,
		Self,
		Default,
		LocaliseTextCallbackOnly
	}
	internal class Translations : Dictionary<string, string>
	{
		internal Translations()
			: base((IEqualityComparer<string>)StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
		{
		}
	}
	internal class AssemblyTranslations
	{
		internal Translations Master { get; private set; }

		internal Dictionary<string, Translations> Other { get; private set; }

		internal bool IsDirty { get; set; }

		internal AssemblyTranslations(Translations master)
		{
			Master = master;
			Other = new Dictionary<string, Translations>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
		}
	}
	internal class Assemblies : Dictionary<Assembly, AssemblyTranslations>
	{
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.Internal
{
	public class XamlWriterSettings
	{
		public bool WriteDefaultValues { get; set; }

		public int MaxUIElements { get; set; }

		public XamlWriterSettings()
		{
			WriteDefaultValues = false;
			MaxUIElements = int.MaxValue;
		}
	}
	public static class XElementExtensions
	{
		public static string AttributeValue(this XElement element, XName name)
		{
			return element.AttributeValue(name, null);
		}

		public static string AttributeValue(this XElement element, XName name, string defaultValue)
		{
			XAttribute xAttribute = element.Attribute(name);
			if (xAttribute == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return xAttribute.Value;
		}

		public static bool AttributeValue(this XElement element, XName name, bool defaultValue)
		{
			string text = element.AttributeValue(name);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			try
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToBoolean(text.ToLowerInvariant());
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static TimeSpan AttributeValue(this XElement element, XName name, TimeSpan defaultValue)
		{
			string text = element.AttributeValue(name);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			try
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToTimeSpan(text);
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static int AttributeValue(this XElement element, XName name, int defaultValue)
		{
			string text = element.AttributeValue(name);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			try
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToInt32(text);
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static double AttributeValue(this XElement element, XName name, double defaultValue)
		{
			string text = element.AttributeValue(name);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			try
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToDouble(text);
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static XElement ElementWithAttribute(this XElement element, XName elementName, XName attributeName)
		{
			foreach (XElement item in element.Elements(elementName))
			{
				if (item.Attribute(attributeName) != null)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static IEnumerable<XElement> ElementsWithAttribute(this XElement element, XName elementName, XName attributeName)
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			foreach (XElement item in element.Elements(elementName))
			{
				if (item.Attribute(attributeName) != null)
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static XElement ElementWithAttributeValue(this XElement element, XName elementName, XName attributeName, string attributeValue)
		{
			foreach (XElement item in element.Elements(elementName))
			{
				if (string.Compare(item.AttributeValue(attributeName), attributeValue, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static IEnumerable<XElement> ElementsWithAttribute(this XElement element, XName elementName, XName attributeName, string attributeValue)
		{
			List<XElement> list = new List<XElement>();
			foreach (XElement item in element.Elements(elementName))
			{
				if (string.Compare(item.AttributeValue(attributeName), attributeValue, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Color AttributeValue(this XElement element, string tag, System.Windows.Media.Color defaultValue)
		{
			try
			{
				string text = element.AttributeValue(tag);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (text.StartsWith("#", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						text = text.Remove(0, 1);
					}
					Type typeFromHandle = typeof(Colors);
					if (typeFromHandle.GetProperty(text) != null)
					{
						object obj = typeFromHandle.InvokeMember(text, BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, null, null);
						if (obj != null)
						{
							return (System.Windows.Media.Color)obj;
						}
					}
					byte result = 0;
					byte result2 = 0;
					byte result3 = 0;
					byte result4 = 0;
					if (text.Length > 1)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(0, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
					}
					if (text.Length > 3)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(2, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2);
					}
					if (text.Length > 5)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(4, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result3);
					}
					if (text.Length > 7)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(6, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result4);
					}
					switch (text.Length)
					{
					case 2:
						return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, result, result, result);
					case 6:
						return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, result, result2, result3);
					case 8:
						return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(result, result2, result3, result4);
					}
				}
				return defaultValue;
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static string ToXml(this System.Windows.Media.Color colour)
		{
			return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "#{0:X2}{1:X2}{2:X2}{3:X2}", colour.A, colour.R, colour.G, colour.B);
		}

		public static System.Drawing.Color AttributeValue(this XElement element, string tag, System.Drawing.Color defaultValue)
		{
			try
			{
				string text = element.AttributeValue(tag);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (text.StartsWith("#", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
					{
						text = text.Remove(0, 1);
					}
					Type typeFromHandle = typeof(System.Drawing.Color);
					if (typeFromHandle.GetProperty(text) != null)
					{
						object obj = typeFromHandle.InvokeMember(text, BindingFlags.GetProperty, null, null, null);
						if (obj != null)
						{
							return (System.Drawing.Color)obj;
						}
					}
					byte result = 0;
					byte result2 = 0;
					byte result3 = 0;
					byte result4 = 0;
					if (text.Length > 1)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(0, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
					}
					if (text.Length > 3)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(2, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2);
					}
					if (text.Length > 5)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(4, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result3);
					}
					if (text.Length > 7)
					{
						byte.TryParse(text.Substring(6, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result4);
					}
					switch (text.Length)
					{
					case 2:
						return System.Drawing.Color.FromArgb(255, result, result, result);
					case 6:
						return System.Drawing.Color.FromArgb(255, result, result2, result3);
					case 8:
						return System.Drawing.Color.FromArgb(result, result2, result3, result4);
					}
				}
				return defaultValue;
			}
			catch
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
		}

		public static string ToXml(this System.Drawing.Color colour)
		{
			return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "#{0:X2}{1:X2}{2:X2}{3:X2}", colour.A, colour.R, colour.G, colour.B);
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.Converters
{
	public class CollectionCountToBooleanConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is ICollection collection)
			{
				return collection.Count > 0;
			}
			if (value is IEnumerable enumerable)
			{
				{
					IEnumerator enumerator = enumerable.GetEnumerator();
					try
					{
						if (enumerator.MoveNext())
						{
							_ = enumerator.Current;
							return true;
						}
					}
					finally
					{
						IDisposable disposable = enumerator as IDisposable;
						if (disposable != null)
						{
							disposable.Dispose();
						}
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class EmptyCollectionToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is IList list)
			{
				return (list.Count <= 0) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
			}
			if (value is IEnumerable enumerable)
			{
				IEnumerator enumerator = enumerable.GetEnumerator();
				if (enumerator != null && enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class EmptyStringToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return string.IsNullOrEmpty(value as string) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class NullValueToBooleanConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value != null;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class NullValueToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (value == null) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.Commands
{
	public class ActionCommand<T> : ICommand
	{
		private CommandKeyBinding _keyBinding;

		private Func<T, bool> _canExecute;

		private Action<T> _execute;

		public CommandKeyBinding KeyBinding
		{
			get
			{
				return _keyBinding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_keyBinding != value)
				{
					CommandKeyBinding.Unregister(_keyBinding, this);
					_keyBinding = value;
					CommandKeyBinding.Register(_keyBinding, this);
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged;

		public ActionCommand(Action<T> execute)
		{
			if (execute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
			}
			_execute = execute;
		}

		public ActionCommand(Action<T> execute, Func<T, bool> canExecute)
			: this(execute)
		{
			if (canExecute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("canExecute");
			}
			_canExecute = canExecute;
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (_canExecute != null)
			{
				return _canExecute((T)parameter);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void RaiseCanExecuteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanExecuteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanExecuteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			_execute((T)parameter);
		}
	}

	public class BoundCommand : DependencyObject, ICommand, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty KeyBindingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("KeyBinding", typeof(CommandKeyBinding), typeof(BoundCommand), new UIPropertyMetadata(OnKeyBindingPropertyChanged));

		public static DependencyProperty CommandProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Command", typeof(ICommand), typeof(BoundCommand), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnCommandPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsEnabled", typeof(bool?), typeof(BoundCommand), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnIsEnabledChanged));

		public Action<BoundCommand, object> BeforeExecuteAction { get; set; }

		public Action<BoundCommand, object> AfterExecuteAction { get; set; }

		public Func<BoundCommand, object, bool?> CanExecuteFilter { get; set; }

		public CommandKeyBinding KeyBinding
		{
			get
			{
				return (CommandKeyBinding)GetValue(KeyBindingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(KeyBindingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ICommand Command
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(CommandProperty) as ICommand;
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool? IsEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool?)GetValue(IsEnabledProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsEnabledProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CommandChanged;

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged;

		public BoundCommand()
		{
		}

		public BoundCommand(Binding binding)
		{
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, CommandProperty, binding);
		}

		public BoundCommand(object bindingSource, string bindingPath)
		{
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, CommandProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = bindingSource,
				Path = new PropertyPath(bindingPath),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		private static void OnKeyBindingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BoundCommand command = d as BoundCommand;
			CommandKeyBinding.Unregister(e.OldValue as CommandKeyBinding, command);
			CommandKeyBinding.Register(e.NewValue as CommandKeyBinding, command);
		}

		private static void OnCommandPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is BoundCommand boundCommand)
			{
				boundCommand.OnCommandChanged(args.OldValue as ICommand, args.NewValue as ICommand);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandChanged(ICommand oldValue, ICommand newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				CommandCanExecuteEventManager.RemoveListener(oldValue, this);
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				CommandCanExecuteEventManager.AddListener(newValue, this);
			}
			RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			if (this.CommandChanged != null)
			{
				this.CommandChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public void RaiseCanExecuteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanExecuteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanExecuteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (IsEnabled.HasValue)
			{
				return IsEnabled.Value;
			}
			bool? flag = WrapperCanExecute(parameter);
			if (flag.HasValue)
			{
				return flag.Value;
			}
			if (Command == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Command.CanExecute(parameter);
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			WrapperBeforeExecute(parameter);
			if (Command != null)
			{
				Command.Execute(parameter);
			}
			WrapperAfterExecute(parameter);
		}

		private static void OnIsEnabledChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is BoundCommand boundCommand)
			{
				boundCommand.OnIsEnabledChanged((bool?)args.OldValue, (bool?)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsEnabledChanged(bool? oldValue, bool? newValue)
		{
			RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		protected virtual bool? WrapperCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (CanExecuteFilter == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return CanExecuteFilter(this, parameter);
		}

		protected virtual void WrapperBeforeExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (BeforeExecuteAction != null)
			{
				BeforeExecuteAction(this, parameter);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void WrapperAfterExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (AfterExecuteAction != null)
			{
				AfterExecuteAction(this, parameter);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandCanExecuteChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		public virtual bool ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (managerType == typeof(CommandCanExecuteEventManager))
			{
				result = true;
				if (sender == Command)
				{
					OnCommandCanExecuteChanged(sender, e);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class CommandKeyBinding : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty BindingTargetProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BindingTarget", typeof(UIElement), typeof(CommandKeyBinding));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty KeyGestureProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("KeyGesture", typeof(KeyGesture), typeof(CommandKeyBinding));

		public UIElement BindingTarget
		{
			get
			{
				return (UIElement)GetValue(BindingTargetProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(BindingTargetProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public KeyGesture KeyGesture
		{
			get
			{
				return (KeyGesture)GetValue(KeyGestureProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(KeyGestureProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (dp == BindingTargetProperty)
			{
				return BindingOperations.GetBindingBase(this, dp) != null;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		internal static void Register(CommandKeyBinding binding, ICommand command)
		{
			if (binding != null && command != null && binding.BindingTarget != null && binding.KeyGesture != null)
			{
				binding.BindingTarget.InputBindings.Add(new KeyBinding(command, binding.KeyGesture));
			}
		}

		internal static void Unregister(CommandKeyBinding binding, ICommand command)
		{
			if (binding == null || command == null || binding.BindingTarget == null || binding.KeyGesture == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyBinding item in (from x in binding.BindingTarget.InputBindings.OfType<KeyBinding>()
				let kg = x.Gesture as KeyGesture
				where x.Command == command && kg != null && kg.Key == binding.KeyGesture.Key && kg.Modifiers == binding.KeyGesture.Modifiers
				select x).ToList())
			{
				binding.BindingTarget.InputBindings.Remove(item);
			}
		}
	}
	public class DependencyActionCommandBase : DependencyObject, ICommand
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(DependencyActionCommandBase), new PropertyMetadata(OnIsEnabledChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty KeyBindingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("KeyBinding", typeof(CommandKeyBinding), typeof(DependencyActionCommandBase), new UIPropertyMetadata(OnKeyBindingPropertyChanged));

		public bool IsEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsEnabledProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsEnabledProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public CommandKeyBinding KeyBinding
		{
			get
			{
				return (CommandKeyBinding)GetValue(KeyBindingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(KeyBindingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged;

		private static void OnIsEnabledChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			(d as DependencyActionCommandBase).RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private static void OnKeyBindingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BoundCommand command = d as BoundCommand;
			CommandKeyBinding.Unregister(e.OldValue as CommandKeyBinding, command);
			CommandKeyBinding.Register(e.NewValue as CommandKeyBinding, command);
		}

		public virtual bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			return IsEnabled;
		}

		public void RaiseCanExecuteChanged()
		{
			if (this.CanExecuteChanged != null)
			{
				this.CanExecuteChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Execute(object parameter)
		{
		}
	}
	public class DependencyActionCommand<T> : DependencyActionCommandBase
	{
		private Action<T> _execute;

		public DependencyActionCommand(Action<T> execute)
		{
			if (execute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
			}
			_execute = execute;
		}

		public DependencyActionCommand(Action<T> execute, Binding isEnabledBinding)
			: this(execute)
		{
			if (isEnabledBinding != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, DependencyActionCommandBase.IsEnabledProperty, isEnabledBinding);
			}
		}

		public override void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			_execute((T)parameter);
		}
	}
	public class DependencyActionCommand : DependencyActionCommandBase
	{
		private Action _execute;

		public DependencyActionCommand(Action execute)
		{
			if (execute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
			}
			_execute = execute;
		}

		public DependencyActionCommand(Action execute, Binding isEnabledBinding)
			: this(execute)
		{
			if (isEnabledBinding != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, DependencyActionCommandBase.IsEnabledProperty, isEnabledBinding);
			}
		}

		public override void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			_execute();
		}
	}
	public class RelayActionCommand<T> : ICommand
	{
		private Func<T, bool> _canExecute;

		private Action<T> _execute;

		private CommandKeyBinding _keyBinding;

		public CommandKeyBinding KeyBinding
		{
			get
			{
				return _keyBinding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_keyBinding != value)
				{
					CommandKeyBinding.Unregister(_keyBinding, this);
					_keyBinding = value;
					CommandKeyBinding.Register(_keyBinding, this);
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged
		{
			add
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested -= value;
			}
		}

		public RelayActionCommand(Action<T> execute)
		{
			if (execute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
			}
			_execute = execute;
		}

		public RelayActionCommand(Action<T> execute, Func<T, bool> canExecute)
			: this(execute)
		{
			if (canExecute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("canExecute");
			}
			_canExecute = canExecute;
		}

		public void RaiseCanExecuteChanged()
		{
			CommandManager.InvalidateRequerySuggested();
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (_canExecute != null)
			{
				return _canExecute((T)parameter);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			_execute((T)parameter);
		}
	}
	public class RelayActionCommand : ICommand
	{
		private Func<bool> _canExecute;

		private Action _execute;

		private CommandKeyBinding _keyBinding;

		public CommandKeyBinding KeyBinding
		{
			get
			{
				return _keyBinding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_keyBinding != value)
				{
					CommandKeyBinding.Unregister(_keyBinding, this);
					_keyBinding = value;
					CommandKeyBinding.Register(_keyBinding, this);
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged
		{
			add
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested -= value;
			}
		}

		public RelayActionCommand(Action execute)
		{
			if (execute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
			}
			_execute = execute;
		}

		public RelayActionCommand(Action execute, Func<bool> canExecute)
			: this(execute)
		{
			if (canExecute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("canExecute");
			}
			_canExecute = canExecute;
		}

		public void RaiseCanExecuteChanged()
		{
			CommandManager.InvalidateRequerySuggested();
		}

		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (_canExecute != null)
			{
				return _canExecute();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			_execute();
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.ColorPicker
{
	public abstract class BasePaletteControl : UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private static ShadedColourCollection _standardColours;

		private static ShadedColourCollection _standardFills;

		private static ColourCollection _customColours;

		private static BrushCollection _customFills;

		private AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport _local;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonWidthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ButtonWidth", typeof(double), typeof(BasePaletteControl), new PropertyMetadata(12.0));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonHeightProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ButtonHeight", typeof(double), typeof(BasePaletteControl), new PropertyMetadata(12.0));

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler _standardPaletteColoursChangedHandler;

		private ShadedColourCollection _standardPalette;

		private List<ColourRadioButton> _standardColourButtons = new List<ColourRadioButton>();

		private NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler _customPaletteColoursChangedHandler;

		private BrushCollection _customPalette;

		private ColourRadioButton[] _customColourButtons = new ColourRadioButton[16];

		private System.Windows.Media.Brush _selectedBrush;

		private System.Windows.Media.Brush _currentBrush;

		private System.Windows.Media.Brush _defaultBrush;

		private ColourRadioButton _selectedButton;

		internal BasePaletteControl UserControl;

		internal Grid LayoutRoot;

		internal Grid gStandardColours;

		internal TextBlock tbStdPalette;

		internal Grid gStandardPalette;

		internal Grid gCustomColours;

		internal TextBlock tbCustomPalette;

		internal Hyperlink hlMore;

		internal Grid gCustomPalette;

		internal Grid gCurrentColour;

		internal ColourRadioButton crbCurrentColour;

		internal TextBlock tbCurrent;

		internal Grid gDefaultColour;

		internal ColourRadioButton crbDefaultColour;

		internal TextBlock tbDefault;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public static ShadedColourCollection StandardColourRanges
		{
			get
			{
				if (_standardColours == null)
				{
					_standardColours = new ShadedColourCollection();
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Black, "Black", new int[5] { 45, 35, 25, 15, 5 }));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.White, "White", new int[5] { -10, -20, -30, -40, -50 }));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Red, "Red"));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Orange, "Orange"));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(XmlSupport.ColourFromXml("#FF00D000"), "Green"));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Cyan, "Cyan"));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Blue, "Blue"));
					_standardColours.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Magenta, "Magenta"));
				}
				return _standardColours;
			}
		}

		public static ShadedColourCollection StandardFillRanges
		{
			get
			{
				if (_standardFills == null)
				{
					_standardFills = new ShadedColourCollection();
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Black, "Black", new int[5] { 45, 35, 25, 15, 5 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.White, "White", new int[5] { -10, -20, -30, -40, -50 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Red, "Red", new int[5] { 90, 70, 50, 25, -20 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Yellow, "Yellow", new int[5] { 90, 70, 50, 25, -20 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(XmlSupport.ColourFromXml("#FF00D000"), "Green", new int[5] { 90, 70, 50, 25, -20 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Cyan, "Cyan", new int[5] { 90, 70, 50, 25, -20 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Blue, "Blue", new int[5] { 90, 70, 50, 25, -20 }));
					_standardFills.Add(new ShadedColour(Colors.Magenta, "Magenta", new int[5] { 90, 70, 50, 25, -20 }));
				}
				return _standardFills;
			}
		}

		public static ColourCollection CustomColours
		{
			get
			{
				if (_customColours == null)
				{
					_customColours = new ColourCollection();
				}
				return _customColours;
			}
			set
			{
				_customColours = value;
			}
		}

		public static BrushCollection CustomFills
		{
			get
			{
				if (_customFills == null)
				{
					_customFills = new BrushCollection();
				}
				return _customFills;
			}
			set
			{
				_customFills = value;
			}
		}

		protected AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport Local => _local;

		public double ButtonWidth => (double)GetValue(ButtonWidthProperty);

		public double ButtonHeight => (double)GetValue(ButtonHeightProperty);

		public System.Windows.Size ButtonSize
		{
			get
			{
				return new System.Windows.Size((double)GetValue(ButtonWidthProperty), (double)GetValue(ButtonHeightProperty));
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ButtonWidthProperty, value.Width);
				SetValue(ButtonHeightProperty, value.Height);
			}
		}

		public ShadedColourCollection StandardPalette
		{
			get
			{
				return _standardPalette;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_standardPalette == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_standardPalette != null)
				{
					_standardPalette.PropertyChanged -= _standardPaletteColoursChangedHandler;
				}
				_standardPalette = value;
				if (_standardPalette != null)
				{
					if (_standardPaletteColoursChangedHandler == null)
					{
						_standardPaletteColoursChangedHandler = _standardPaletteColours_PropertyChanged;
					}
					_standardPalette.PropertyChanged += _standardPaletteColoursChangedHandler;
				}
				BuildStandardColourGrid();
			}
		}

		protected BrushCollection InternalCustomPalette
		{
			get
			{
				return _customPalette;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_customPalette == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_customPalette != null)
				{
					_customPalette.CollectionChanged -= _customPaletteColoursChangedHandler;
				}
				_customPalette = value;
				if (_customPalette != null)
				{
					if (_customPaletteColoursChangedHandler == null)
					{
						_customPaletteColoursChangedHandler = CustomPaletteColours_CollectionChanged;
					}
					_customPalette.CollectionChanged += _customPaletteColoursChangedHandler;
				}
				BuildCustomColourGrid();
			}
		}

		protected System.Windows.Media.Brush SelectedBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedBrush;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_selectedBrush != value)
				{
					_selectedBrush = value;
					OnSelectionChanged(new EventArgs());
				}
			}
		}

		protected System.Windows.Media.Brush CurrentBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return _currentBrush;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_currentBrush != value)
				{
					_currentBrush = value;
					SelectBrush(_currentBrush, isCurrentBrush: true);
				}
			}
		}

		protected System.Windows.Media.Brush DefaultBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return _defaultBrush;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_defaultBrush != value)
				{
					_defaultBrush = value;
					SetDefaultBrush();
					SelectBrush(_selectedBrush, isCurrentBrush: false);
				}
			}
		}

		public PaletteCategory SelectedCategory
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedButton == crbCurrentColour)
				{
					return PaletteCategory.Current;
				}
				if (_selectedButton == crbDefaultColour)
				{
					return PaletteCategory.Default;
				}
				if (_standardColourButtons.Contains(_selectedButton))
				{
					return PaletteCategory.Standard;
				}
				return PaletteCategory.Custom;
			}
		}

		public event RoutedEventHandler MoreColoursClick;

		public event EventHandler SelectionChanged;

		public BasePaletteControl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_local = AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport.GetAssemblyLocalSupport(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.Behaviour, Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			if (_local != null)
			{
				_local.Localise(this, tbCurrent);
				_local.Localise(this, tbCustomPalette);
				_local.Localise(this, tbDefault);
				_local.Localise(this, tbStdPalette);
				hlMore.ToolTip = _local.LocaliseDefault(this, hlMore.ToolTip.ToString());
				hlMore.Inlines.Clear();
				hlMore.Inlines.Add(new Run(_local.LocaliseDefault(this, "More...")));
			}
			crbCurrentColour.GroupName = (crbDefaultColour.GroupName = Guid.NewGuid().ToString());
			StandardPalette = StandardColourRanges;
			InternalCustomPalette = CustomFills;
			CurrentBrush = WpfSilverlightSupport.Brushes.Black;
		}

		private void _standardPaletteColours_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BuildStandardColourGrid();
		}

		private void BuildStandardColourGrid()
		{
			gStandardColours.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			if (StandardPalette != null && StandardPalette.Count > 0)
			{
				gStandardPalette.Children.Clear();
				gStandardPalette.ColumnDefinitions.Clear();
				gStandardPalette.RowDefinitions.Clear();
				_standardColourButtons.Clear();
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = 0;
				foreach (ShadedColour item in StandardPalette)
				{
					gStandardPalette.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
					{
						Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star)
					});
					gStandardPalette.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition
					{
						Height = default(GridLength)
					});
					System.Windows.Media.Color colour = item.Colour;
					ColourRadioButton colourRadioButton = MakeColourButton(colour, num, 0, VisualisationControlsLocalisation.LocaliseDefault(_local, this, item.ToString()), new Thickness(1.0), new Thickness(2.0, 2.0, 2.0, 4.0));
					gStandardPalette.Children.Add(colourRadioButton);
					_standardColourButtons.Add(colourRadioButton);
					int[] shades = item.Shades;
					for (int i = 0; i < shades.Length; i++)
					{
						if (i == num2)
						{
							gStandardPalette.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition
							{
								Height = default(GridLength)
							});
							num2++;
						}
						double num3 = (double)shades[i] / 100.0;
						System.Windows.Media.Color colour2 = new System.Windows.Media.Color
						{
							A = colour.A,
							R = ((num3 < 0.0) ? Convert.ToByte(Math.Max(0.0, (double)(int)colour.R + num3 * (double)(int)colour.R)) : Convert.ToByte(Math.Min(255.0, (double)(int)colour.R + num3 * (double)(255 - colour.R)))),
							G = ((num3 < 0.0) ? Convert.ToByte(Math.Max(0.0, (double)(int)colour.G + num3 * (double)(int)colour.G)) : Convert.ToByte(Math.Min(255.0, (double)(int)colour.G + num3 * (double)(255 - colour.G)))),
							B = ((num3 < 0.0) ? Convert.ToByte(Math.Max(0.0, (double)(int)colour.B + num3 * (double)(int)colour.B)) : Convert.ToByte(Math.Min(255.0, (double)(int)colour.B + num3 * (double)(255 - colour.B))))
						};
						string text = VisualisationControlsLocalisation.LocaliseDefault(_local, this, item.ToString());
						if (num3 < 0.0)
						{
							text += string.Format(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.LocaliseDefault(_local, this, "  {0}% darker"), -shades[i]);
						}
						else if (num3 > 0.0)
						{
							text += string.Format(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.LocaliseDefault(_local, this, "  {0}% lighter"), shades[i]);
						}
						Thickness border = ((i != 0) ? ((i != shades.Length - 1) ? new Thickness(1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0) : new Thickness(1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0)) : new Thickness(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0));
						colourRadioButton = MakeColourButton(colour2, num, i + 1, text, border, new Thickness(0.0));
						gStandardPalette.Children.Add(colourRadioButton);
						_standardColourButtons.Add(colourRadioButton);
					}
					num++;
				}
				gStandardColours.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			SelectBrush(_selectedBrush, isCurrentBrush: false);
		}

		private ColourRadioButton MakeColourButton(System.Windows.Media.Color colour, int column, int row, string hintText, Thickness border, Thickness margin)
		{
			return MakeColourButton(new SolidColorBrush(colour), column, row, string.IsNullOrEmpty(hintText) ? colour.ToString() : hintText, border, margin);
		}

		private ColourRadioButton MakeColourButton(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush, int column, int row, string hintText, Thickness border, Thickness margin)
		{
			ColourRadioButton colourRadioButton = new ColourRadioButton
			{
				Foreground = brush,
				ToolTip = hintText,
				BorderThickness = border,
				Margin = margin,
				GroupName = crbCurrentColour.GroupName
			};
			BindButtonSize(colourRadioButton);
			colourRadioButton.Checked += crb_Checked;
			Grid.SetColumn(colourRadioButton, column);
			Grid.SetRow(colourRadioButton, row);
			return colourRadioButton;
		}

		private void BindButtonSize(ColourRadioButton crb)
		{
			BindButtonProperty(crb, "ButtonHeight", FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
			BindButtonProperty(crb, "ButtonWidth", FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
		}

		private void BindButtonProperty(ColourRadioButton crb, string path, DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding(path);
			binding.Source = this;
			crb.SetBinding(dp, binding);
		}

		private void CustomPaletteColours_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnCustomPaletteChanged(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnCustomPaletteChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BuildCustomColourGrid();
		}

		private void BuildCustomColourGrid()
		{
			if (InternalCustomPalette == null)
			{
				_customPalette = new BrushCollection();
			}
			gCustomPalette.Children.Clear();
			gCustomPalette.ColumnDefinitions.Clear();
			gCustomPalette.RowDefinitions.Clear();
			int num = 8;
			int num2 = 2;
			if (StandardPalette != null && StandardPalette.Count != num)
			{
				num = StandardPalette.Count;
			}
			num2 = (int)Math.Ceiling(Math.Max(16.0, InternalCustomPalette.Count) / (double)num);
			_customColourButtons = new ColourRadioButton[num2 * num];
			for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
			{
				gCustomPalette.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition
				{
					Height = default(GridLength)
				});
				for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
				{
					if (gCustomPalette.ColumnDefinitions.Count == j)
					{
						gCustomPalette.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
						{
							Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star)
						});
					}
					int num3 = i * num + j;
					if (num3 < InternalCustomPalette.Count)
					{
						System.Windows.Media.Brush brush = InternalCustomPalette[num3];
						string hintText = null;
						if (brush is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush)
						{
							hintText = VisualisationControlsLocalisation.LocaliseDefault(_local, this, solidColorBrush.Color.ToString());
						}
						ColourRadioButton colourRadioButton = MakeColourButton(brush, j, i, hintText, new Thickness(1.0), new Thickness(2.0));
						_customColourButtons[num3] = colourRadioButton;
						gCustomPalette.Children.Add(colourRadioButton);
					}
					else
					{
						gCustomPalette.Children.Add(MakeColourButton(Colors.Transparent, j, i, VisualisationControlsLocalisation.LocaliseDefault(_local, this, "Unspecified"), new Thickness(1.0), new Thickness(2.0)));
					}
				}
			}
			SelectBrush(_selectedBrush, isCurrentBrush: false);
		}

		protected void SetSelectedBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			SelectBrush(brush, isCurrentBrush: false);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged(EventArgs eventArgs)
		{
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(this, eventArgs);
			}
		}

		private void SelectBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush, bool isCurrentBrush)
		{
			if (isCurrentBrush)
			{
				gCurrentColour.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (gDefaultColour.IsVisible && AreBrushesSame(crbDefaultColour.Foreground, brush))
			{
				crbDefaultColour.IsChecked = true;
				return;
			}
			foreach (ColourRadioButton standardColourButton in _standardColourButtons)
			{
				if (AreBrushesSame(standardColourButton.Foreground, brush))
				{
					standardColourButton.IsChecked = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			ColourRadioButton[] customColourButtons = _customColourButtons;
			foreach (ColourRadioButton colourRadioButton in customColourButtons)
			{
				if (colourRadioButton != null && AreBrushesSame(colourRadioButton.Foreground, brush))
				{
					colourRadioButton.IsChecked = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			if (isCurrentBrush)
			{
				crbCurrentColour.Foreground = brush;
				crbCurrentColour.IsChecked = true;
				crbCurrentColour.ToolTip = CurrentBrush.ToString();
				gCurrentColour.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		private bool AreBrushesSame(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush1, System.Windows.Media.Brush brush2)
		{
			if (brush1 == null || brush2 == null)
			{
				return brush1 == brush2;
			}
			if (brush1.Equals(brush2))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (brush1.GetType() != brush2.GetType())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (brush1 is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush)
			{
				return solidColorBrush.Color == (brush2 as SolidColorBrush).Color;
			}
			if (brush1 is GradientBrush gradientBrush)
			{
				GradientBrush gradientBrush2 = brush2 as GradientBrush;
				if (gradientBrush.GradientStops.Count != gradientBrush2.GradientStops.Count)
				{
					return false;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < gradientBrush.GradientStops.Count; i++)
				{
					GradientStop gradientStop = gradientBrush.GradientStops[i];
					GradientStop gradientStop2 = gradientBrush2.GradientStops[i];
					if (gradientStop.Color != gradientStop2.Color || gradientStop.Offset != gradientStop2.Offset)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				if (gradientBrush is LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush)
				{
					LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush2 = gradientBrush2 as LinearGradientBrush;
					if (linearGradientBrush.StartPoint != linearGradientBrush2.StartPoint || linearGradientBrush.EndPoint != linearGradientBrush2.EndPoint)
					{
						return false;
					}
					return true;
				}
				if (gradientBrush is RadialGradientBrush radialGradientBrush)
				{
					RadialGradientBrush radialGradientBrush2 = gradientBrush2 as RadialGradientBrush;
					if (radialGradientBrush.Center != radialGradientBrush2.Center || radialGradientBrush.GradientOrigin != radialGradientBrush2.GradientOrigin || radialGradientBrush.RadiusX != radialGradientBrush2.RadiusX || radialGradientBrush.RadiusY != radialGradientBrush2.RadiusX)
					{
						return false;
					}
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected void AddCustomBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush, bool selectIt)
		{
			foreach (ColourRadioButton standardColourButton in _standardColourButtons)
			{
				if (AreBrushesSame(standardColourButton.Foreground, brush))
				{
					if (selectIt)
					{
						standardColourButton.IsChecked = true;
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			ColourRadioButton[] customColourButtons = _customColourButtons;
			foreach (ColourRadioButton colourRadioButton in customColourButtons)
			{
				if (colourRadioButton != null && AreBrushesSame(colourRadioButton.Foreground, brush))
				{
					if (selectIt)
					{
						colourRadioButton.IsChecked = true;
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			InternalCustomPalette.Add(brush);
			if (selectIt)
			{
				SelectBrush(brush, isCurrentBrush: false);
			}
		}

		protected void ReplaceCustomBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			ColourRadioButton[] customColourButtons = _customColourButtons;
			foreach (ColourRadioButton colourRadioButton in customColourButtons)
			{
				if (colourRadioButton == _selectedButton)
				{
					InternalCustomPalette[InternalCustomPalette.IndexOf(colourRadioButton.Foreground)] = brush;
					SelectBrush(brush, isCurrentBrush: false);
					return;
				}
			}
			AddCustomBrush(brush, selectIt: true);
		}

		private void SetDefaultBrush()
		{
			gDefaultColour.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			crbDefaultColour.Foreground = DefaultBrush;
			crbDefaultColour.ToolTip = DefaultBrush.ToString();
			gDefaultColour.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
		}

		private void crb_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SelectedBrush = (_selectedButton = sender as ColourRadioButton).Foreground;
		}

		private void MoreColours_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.MoreColoursClick != null)
			{
				this.MoreColoursClick(this, e);
			}
		}

	public class ColourPaletteControl : BasePaletteControl
	{
		private NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler _customPaletteColoursChangedHandler;

		private ColourCollection _customPalette;

		private bool _customPaletteChanging;

		public ColourCollection CustomPalette
		{
			get
			{
				return _customPalette;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_customPalette == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_customPalette != null)
				{
					_customPalette.CollectionChanged -= _customPaletteColoursChangedHandler;
				}
				_customPalette = value;
				if (_customPalette != null)
				{
					if (_customPaletteColoursChangedHandler == null)
					{
						_customPaletteColoursChangedHandler = CustomPaletteColours_CollectionChanged;
					}
					_customPalette.CollectionChanged += _customPaletteColoursChangedHandler;
				}
				BuildInternalCustomPalette();
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Color CurrentColour
		{
			get
			{
				return (base.CurrentBrush as SolidColorBrush).Color;
			}
			set
			{
				base.CurrentBrush = new SolidColorBrush(value);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Color DefaultColour
		{
			get
			{
				return (base.DefaultBrush as SolidColorBrush).Color;
			}
			set
			{
				base.DefaultBrush = new SolidColorBrush(value);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Color SelectedColour
		{
			get
			{
				return (base.SelectedBrush as SolidColorBrush).Color;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (SelectedColour != value)
				{
					base.SelectedBrush = new SolidColorBrush(value);
				}
			}
		}

		public ColourPaletteControl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.StandardPalette = BasePaletteControl.StandardColourRanges;
			CustomPalette = BasePaletteControl.CustomColours;
			CurrentColour = Colors.Black;
		}

		public ColourPaletteControl(ShadedColourCollection standardColours, ColourCollection customColours, System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.StandardPalette = standardColours;
			CustomPalette = customColours;
			CurrentColour = currentColour;
		}

		public ColourPaletteControl(ShadedColourCollection standardColours, ColourCollection customColours, System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour, System.Windows.Media.Color defaultColour)
			: this(standardColours, customColours, currentColour)
		{
			DefaultColour = defaultColour;
		}

		public ColourPaletteControl(System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour)
			: this(BasePaletteControl.StandardColourRanges, BasePaletteControl.CustomColours, currentColour)
		{
		}

		public ColourPaletteControl(System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour, System.Windows.Media.Color defaultColour)
			: this(currentColour)
		{
			DefaultColour = defaultColour;
		}

		private void CustomPaletteColours_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BuildInternalCustomPalette();
		}

		private void BuildInternalCustomPalette()
		{
			if (_customPaletteChanging)
			{
				return;
			}
			_customPaletteChanging = true;
			try
			{
				if (CustomPalette != null)
				{
					base.InternalCustomPalette = new BrushCollection();
					{
						foreach (System.Windows.Media.Color item in CustomPalette)
						{
							base.InternalCustomPalette.Add(new SolidColorBrush(item));
						}
						return;
					}
				}
				base.InternalCustomPalette = null;
			}
			finally
			{
				_customPaletteChanging = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCustomPaletteChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnCustomPaletteChanged(e);
			if (CustomPalette == null || _customPaletteChanging)
			{
				return;
			}
			_customPaletteChanging = true;
			try
			{
				CustomPalette.Clear();
				foreach (SolidColorBrush item in base.InternalCustomPalette)
				{
					CustomPalette.Add(item.Color);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_customPaletteChanging = false;
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedColour(System.Windows.Media.Color colour)
		{
			SetSelectedBrush(new SolidColorBrush(colour));
		}

		public void AddCustomColour(System.Windows.Media.Color colour, bool selectIt)
		{
			AddCustomBrush(new SolidColorBrush(colour), selectIt);
		}

		public void ReplaceCustomColour(System.Windows.Media.Color colour)
		{
			ReplaceCustomBrush(new SolidColorBrush(colour));
		}
	}

	public class ShadedColourCollection : List<ShadedColour>, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public new void Add(ShadedColour item)
		{
			base.Add(item);
			item.PropertyChanged += shadeRange_PropertyChanged;
			OnPropertyChanged("Collection");
		}

		public new bool Remove(ShadedColour item)
		{
			bool num = base.Remove(item);
			if (num)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged("Collection");
			}
			return num;
		}

		private void shadeRange_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnPropertyChanged("ShadedColour");
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string property)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(property));
			}
		}
	}
	public class ShadedColour : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private System.Windows.Media.Color _colour;

		private int[] _shades;

		private string _label;

		public System.Windows.Media.Color Colour
		{
			get
			{
				return _colour;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_colour != value)
				{
					_colour = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Colour");
				}
			}
		}

		public int[] Shades
		{
			get
			{
				return _shades;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_shades != value)
				{
					_shades = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("Shades");
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public ShadedColour(System.Windows.Media.Color colour, string label)
		{
			_colour = colour;
			_label = label;
			_shades = new int[5] { 80, 50, 20, -25, -50 };
		}

		public ShadedColour(System.Windows.Media.Color colour, string label, int[] shades)
		{
			_colour = colour;
			_label = label;
			_shades = shades;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_label))
			{
				return _label;
			}
			return _colour.ToString();
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string property)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(property));
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class BasePaletteDialog : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport _local;

		private BasePaletteControl _paletteControl;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty FullPickerVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FullPickerVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(BasePaletteDialog), new PropertyMetadata(Visibility.Collapsed));

		internal Border bPaletteControl;

		internal StackPanel spFullColourPicker;

		internal BrushPickerControl cpcFullPicker;

		internal Button bAddToCustom;

		internal Button bReplaceCustom;

		internal Button bOK;

		internal Button bCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		protected AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport Local => _local;

		public abstract ShadedColourCollection StandardColourPalette { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Size ButtonSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (_paletteControl == null)
				{
					return System.Windows.Size.Empty;
				}
				return _paletteControl.ButtonSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_paletteControl != null)
				{
					_paletteControl.ButtonSize = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected BrushPickerControl BrushPickerControl => cpcFullPicker;

		protected Visibility FullPickerVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)GetValue(FullPickerVisibilityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(FullPickerVisibilityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public BasePaletteDialog()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_local = AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport.GetAssemblyLocalSupport(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.Behaviour, Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			if (_local != null)
			{
				base.Title = _local.LocaliseDefault(this, base.Title.ToString());
				_local.Localise(this, bAddToCustom);
				_local.Localise(this, bReplaceCustom);
				_local.Localise(this, bOK);
				_local.Localise(this, bCancel);
			}
			Binding binding = new Binding("Visibility")
			{
				Source = spFullColourPicker,
				Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
			};
			SetBinding(FullPickerVisibilityProperty, binding);
		}

		private void bOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DialogResult = true;
		}

		private void bAddToCustom_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnAddToCustomClick();
		}

		private void bReplaceCustom_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnReplaceCustomClick();
		}

		protected abstract void OnAddToCustomClick();

		protected abstract void OnReplaceCustomClick();

		protected void SetPaletteControl(BasePaletteControl paletteControl)
		{
			bPaletteControl.Child = (_paletteControl = paletteControl);
			paletteControl.SelectionChanged += paletteControl_SelectionChanged;
			paletteControl.MoreColoursClick += paletteControl_MoreColoursClick;
		}

		private void paletteControl_MoreColoursClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnMoreColoursClick();
		}

		protected virtual void OnMoreColoursClick()
		{
			spFullColourPicker.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
		}

		private void paletteControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnSelectionChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
			bReplaceCustom.IsEnabled = _paletteControl != null && _paletteControl.SelectedCategory == PaletteCategory.Custom;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Color ShowDialog(DependencyObject parent, System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour)
		{
			return ShowDialog(parent, BasePaletteControl.StandardColourRanges, BasePaletteControl.CustomColours, currentColour, expandFullPicker: false);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Color ShowDialog(DependencyObject parent, System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour, bool expandFullPicker)
		{
			return ShowDialog(parent, BasePaletteControl.StandardColourRanges, BasePaletteControl.CustomColours, currentColour, expandFullPicker);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Color ShowDialog(DependencyObject parent, ShadedColourCollection standardColours, ColourCollection customColours, System.Windows.Media.Color currentColour, bool expandFullPicker)
		{
			ColourPaletteDialog colourPaletteDialog = new ColourPaletteDialog();
			colourPaletteDialog.StandardColourPalette = standardColours;
			colourPaletteDialog.CustomPaletteColours = customColours;
			colourPaletteDialog.CurrentColour = currentColour;
			colourPaletteDialog.ButtonSize = new System.Windows.Size(15.0, 15.0);
			if (expandFullPicker)
			{
				colourPaletteDialog.FullPickerVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			try
			{
				colourPaletteDialog.Owner = Window.GetWindow(parent);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (colourPaletteDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				return colourPaletteDialog.SelectedColour;
			}
			return currentColour;
		}

	public class BrushPickerControl : UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport _local;

		internal BeginStoryboard OnChecked1_BeginStoryboard;

		internal Grid gBrushType;

		internal ImageRadioButton irbSolidBrush;

		internal ImageRadioButton irbGradientBrush;

		internal ImageRadioButton irbRadialBrush;

		internal Grid gGradient;

		internal ColourRadioButton tbStartColour;

		internal ColourRadioButton tbEndColour;

		internal Slider sGradient;

		internal StackPanel spAngle;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Image iAngle;

		internal TextBox tbAngle;

		internal ColorPickerCtrl cpColourPicker;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public System.Windows.Media.Color SelectedColour => cpColourPicker.SelectedColour;

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush SelectedBrush
		{
			get
			{
				if (irbGradientBrush.IsChecked == true)
				{
					LinearGradientBrush obj = (LinearGradientBrush)ColorUtilities.CloneBrush(sGradient.Background);
					double num = 0.0;
					double num2 = 0.5;
					double num3 = Math.PI * double.Parse(tbAngle.Text, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) / 180.0;
					double num4 = Math.Max(0.5, Math.Min(0.5 / Math.Sin(num3), 0.5 / Math.Cos(num3)));
					num = num4 * Math.Cos(num3);
					num2 = num4 * Math.Sin(num3);
					obj.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5 - num, 0.5 - num2);
					obj.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5 + num, 0.5 + num2);
					return obj;
				}
				if (irbRadialBrush.IsChecked == true)
				{
					return new RadialGradientBrush((ColorUtilities.CloneBrush(sGradient.Background) as LinearGradientBrush).GradientStops);
				}
				return new SolidColorBrush(cpColourPicker.SelectedColour);
			}
		}

		public BrushPickerControl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_local = AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport.GetAssemblyLocalSupport(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.Behaviour, Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			if (_local != null)
			{
				object toolTip = ToolTipService.GetToolTip(tbAngle);
				if (toolTip != null)
				{
					ToolTipService.SetToolTip(tbAngle, _local.LocaliseDefault(this, toolTip.ToString()));
				}
				object toolTip2 = ToolTipService.GetToolTip(irbSolidBrush);
				if (toolTip2 != null)
				{
					ToolTipService.SetToolTip(irbSolidBrush, _local.LocaliseDefault(this, toolTip2.ToString()));
				}
				object toolTip3 = ToolTipService.GetToolTip(irbRadialBrush);
				if (toolTip3 != null)
				{
					ToolTipService.SetToolTip(irbRadialBrush, _local.LocaliseDefault(this, toolTip3.ToString()));
				}
				object toolTip4 = ToolTipService.GetToolTip(irbGradientBrush);
				if (toolTip4 != null)
				{
					ToolTipService.SetToolTip(irbGradientBrush, _local.LocaliseDefault(this, toolTip4.ToString()));
				}
				object toolTip5 = ToolTipService.GetToolTip(tbStartColour);
				if (toolTip5 != null)
				{
					ToolTipService.SetToolTip(tbStartColour, _local.LocaliseDefault(this, toolTip5.ToString()));
				}
				object toolTip6 = ToolTipService.GetToolTip(tbEndColour);
				if (toolTip6 != null)
				{
					ToolTipService.SetToolTip(tbEndColour, _local.LocaliseDefault(this, toolTip6.ToString()));
				}
			}
		}

		public void Initialise(System.Windows.Media.Color colour)
		{
			gBrushType.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			irbSolidBrush.IsChecked = true;
			ColorPickerCtrl colorPickerCtrl = cpColourPicker;
			System.Windows.Media.Color selectedColour = (cpColourPicker.OldColour = colour);
			colorPickerCtrl.SelectedColour = selectedColour;
		}

		public void Initialise(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			if (brush == null)
			{
				brush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White;
			}
			if (brush is SolidColorBrush { Color: var color })
			{
				ColorPickerCtrl colorPickerCtrl = cpColourPicker;
				System.Windows.Media.Color selectedColour = (cpColourPicker.OldColour = color);
				colorPickerCtrl.SelectedColour = selectedColour;
				irbSolidBrush.IsChecked = true;
				irbGradientBrush.IsChecked = false;
				irbRadialBrush.IsChecked = false;
				gGradient.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			else if (brush is LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush)
			{
				GradientStop gradientStop = linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[0];
				System.Windows.Media.Color color3 = gradientStop.Color;
				if (gradientStop.Offset != 0.0)
				{
					sGradient.Value = Math.Min(1.0, Math.Max(0.0, gradientStop.Offset * 0.5 + 0.5));
				}
				tbStartColour.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(color3);
				gradientStop = linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Count - 1];
				color3 = gradientStop.Color;
				if (gradientStop.Offset != 1.0)
				{
					sGradient.Value = Math.Min(1.0, Math.Max(0.0, gradientStop.Offset * 0.5));
				}
				tbEndColour.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(color3);
				irbSolidBrush.IsChecked = false;
				irbGradientBrush.IsChecked = true;
				irbRadialBrush.IsChecked = false;
				gGradient.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				System.Windows.Point startPoint = linearGradientBrush.StartPoint;
				System.Windows.Point endPoint = linearGradientBrush.EndPoint;
				double num = ((Math.Abs(endPoint.Y - startPoint.Y) < 1E-08) ? ((!(endPoint.X > startPoint.X)) ? 180.0 : 0.0) : ((!(Math.Abs(endPoint.X - startPoint.X) < 1E-08)) ? (180.0 / Math.PI * Math.Atan((linearGradientBrush.EndPoint.Y - linearGradientBrush.StartPoint.Y) / (linearGradientBrush.EndPoint.X - linearGradientBrush.StartPoint.X))) : ((!(endPoint.Y > startPoint.Y)) ? (-90.0) : 90.0)));
				tbAngle.Text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:f}", num);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(brush is RadialGradientBrush radialGradientBrush))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Invalid brush", "brush");
				}
				GradientStop gradientStop2 = radialGradientBrush.GradientStops[0];
				System.Windows.Media.Color color3 = gradientStop2.Color;
				if (gradientStop2.Offset != 0.0)
				{
					sGradient.Value = Math.Min(1.0, Math.Max(0.0, gradientStop2.Offset * 0.5 + 0.5));
				}
				tbStartColour.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(color3);
				gradientStop2 = radialGradientBrush.GradientStops[radialGradientBrush.GradientStops.Count - 1];
				color3 = gradientStop2.Color;
				if (gradientStop2.Offset != 1.0)
				{
					sGradient.Value = Math.Min(1.0, Math.Max(0.0, gradientStop2.Offset * 0.5));
				}
				tbEndColour.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(color3);
				irbSolidBrush.IsChecked = false;
				irbGradientBrush.IsChecked = false;
				irbRadialBrush.IsChecked = true;
				gGradient.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			gBrushType.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
		}

		private void tbSolidBrush_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetGradientBrushBinding();
		}

		private void tbGradientBrush_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetGradientBrushBinding();
		}

		private void tbRadialBrush_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetGradientBrushBinding();
		}

		private void tbStartColour_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetGradientBrushBinding();
		}

		private void tbEndColour_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			SetGradientBrushBinding();
		}

		private void SetGradientBrushBinding()
		{
			if (irbGradientBrush == null || tbStartColour == null || tbEndColour == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (irbGradientBrush.IsChecked == true || irbRadialBrush.IsChecked == true)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding("Foreground");
				System.Windows.Media.Color color;
				if (tbStartColour.IsChecked == true)
				{
					binding.Source = tbStartColour;
					color = (tbStartColour.Foreground as SolidColorBrush).Color;
				}
				else
				{
					binding.Source = tbEndColour;
					color = (tbEndColour.Foreground as SolidColorBrush).Color;
				}
				binding.Converter = new BrushColourConverter();
				binding.Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay;
				cpColourPicker.cPicker.SetBinding(ColorPicker.SelectedColorProperty, binding);
				cpColourPicker.OldColour = color;
				cpColourPicker.SelectedColour = color;
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Media.Color selectedColour = cpColourPicker.SelectedColour;
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(cpColourPicker.cPicker, ColorPicker.SelectedColorProperty);
				cpColourPicker.SelectedColour = selectedColour;
			}
			cpColourPicker.InvalidateVisual();
		}

		private void Slider_ValueChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Slider slider = sender as Slider;
			if (slider.Background is LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush)
			{
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[0].Offset = Math.Max(0.0, (slider.Value - 0.5) / 0.5);
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[1].Offset = Math.Min(1.0, slider.Value / 0.5);
			}
		}

		private void tbAngle_PreviewTextInput(object sender, TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			TextBox textBox = sender as TextBox;
			string s = textBox.Text.Substring(0, textBox.SelectionStart) + e.Text + textBox.Text.Substring(textBox.SelectionStart);
			e.Handled = !double.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, out var _);
		}

		static ColorPicker()
		{
			SelectedColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedColor", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Color), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent, selectedColor_changed));
			OldColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("OldColor", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Color), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent, oldColor_changed));
			ScAProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ScA", typeof(float), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(1f, ScAChanged));
			ScRProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ScR", typeof(float), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(1f, ScRChanged));
			ScGProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ScG", typeof(float), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(1f, ScGChanged));
			ScBProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ScB", typeof(float), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(1f, ScBChanged));
			AProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("A", typeof(byte), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(byte.MaxValue, AChanged));
			RProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("R", typeof(byte), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(byte.MaxValue, RChanged));
			GProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("G", typeof(byte), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(byte.MaxValue, GChanged));
			BProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("B", typeof(byte), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata(byte.MaxValue, BChanged));
			HexadecimalStringProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HexadecimalString", typeof(string), typeof(ColorPicker), new PropertyMetadata("#FFFFFFFF", HexadecimalStringChanged));
			SelectedColorChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("SelectedColorChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<System.Windows.Media.Color>), typeof(ColorPicker));
			OldColorChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("OldColorChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedPropertyChangedEventHandler<System.Windows.Media.Color>), typeof(ColorPicker));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ColorPicker), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(ColorPicker)));
		}

		public ColorPicker()
		{
			_local = AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.LocalSupport.LocalSupport.GetAssemblyLocalSupport(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.Behaviour, Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			_oldColor = (m_color = Colors.White);
			shouldFindPoint = true;
			SetValue(AProperty, m_color.A);
			SetValue(RProperty, m_color.R);
			SetValue(GProperty, m_color.G);
			SetValue(BProperty, m_color.B);
			SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
			SetValue(OldColorProperty, _oldColor);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (_local != null && GetTemplateChild("tbOpacity") is TextBlock textBlock)
			{
				_local.Localise(this, textBlock);
			}
			m_ColorDetail = GetTemplateChild("PART_ColorDetail") as FrameworkElement;
			m_ColorMarker = GetTemplateChild("PART_ColorMarker") as System.Windows.Shapes.Path;
			m_ColorSlider = GetTemplateChild("PART_ColorSlider") as SpectrumSlider;
			m_ColorSlider.ValueChanged += BaseColorChanged;
			m_ColorMarker.RenderTransform = markerTransform;
			m_ColorMarker.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			m_ColorDetail.MouseLeftButtonDown += OnMouseLeftButtonDown;
			m_ColorDetail.PreviewMouseMove += OnMouseMove;
			m_ColorDetail.SizeChanged += ColorDetailSizeChanged;
			templateApplied = true;
			shouldFindPoint = true;
			isAlphaChange = false;
			SelectedColor = m_color;
			OldColor = _oldColor;
		}

		private static void AChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnAChanged((byte)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnAChanged(byte newValue)
		{
			m_color.A = newValue;
			SetValue(ScAProperty, m_color.ScA);
			SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
		}

		private static void RChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnRChanged((byte)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnRChanged(byte newValue)
		{
			m_color.R = newValue;
			SetValue(ScRProperty, m_color.ScR);
			SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
		}

		private static void GChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnGChanged((byte)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnGChanged(byte newValue)
		{
			m_color.G = newValue;
			SetValue(ScGProperty, m_color.ScG);
			SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
		}

		private static void BChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnBChanged((byte)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnBChanged(byte newValue)
		{
			m_color.B = newValue;
			SetValue(ScBProperty, m_color.ScB);
			SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
		}

		private static void ScAChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnScAChanged((float)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnScAChanged(float newValue)
		{
			isAlphaChange = true;
			if (shouldFindPoint)
			{
				m_color.ScA = newValue;
				SetValue(AProperty, m_color.A);
				SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
				SetValue(HexadecimalStringProperty, m_color.ToString());
			}
			isAlphaChange = false;
		}

		private static void ScRChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnScRChanged((float)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnScRChanged(float newValue)
		{
			if (shouldFindPoint)
			{
				m_color.ScR = newValue;
				SetValue(RProperty, m_color.R);
				SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
				SetValue(HexadecimalStringProperty, m_color.ToString());
			}
		}

		private static void ScGChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnScGChanged((float)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnScGChanged(float newValue)
		{
			if (shouldFindPoint)
			{
				m_color.ScG = newValue;
				SetValue(GProperty, m_color.G);
				SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
				SetValue(HexadecimalStringProperty, m_color.ToString());
			}
		}

		private static void ScBChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnScBChanged((float)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnScBChanged(float newValue)
		{
			if (shouldFindPoint)
			{
				m_color.ScB = newValue;
				SetValue(BProperty, m_color.B);
				SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, m_color);
				SetValue(HexadecimalStringProperty, m_color.ToString());
			}
		}

		private static void HexadecimalStringChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnHexadecimalStringChanged((string)e.OldValue, (string)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnHexadecimalStringChanged(string oldValue, string newValue)
		{
			try
			{
				if (shouldFindPoint)
				{
					m_color = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(newValue);
				}
				SetValue(AProperty, m_color.A);
				SetValue(RProperty, m_color.R);
				SetValue(GProperty, m_color.G);
				SetValue(BProperty, m_color.B);
				if (shouldFindPoint && !isAlphaChange && templateApplied)
				{
					updateMarkerPosition(m_color);
				}
			}
			catch (FormatException)
			{
				SetValue(HexadecimalStringProperty, oldValue);
			}
		}

		private static void selectedColor_changed(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnSelectedColorChanged((System.Windows.Media.Color)e.OldValue, (System.Windows.Media.Color)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void oldColor_changed(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ColorPicker)d).OnOldColorChanged((System.Windows.Media.Color)e.OldValue, (System.Windows.Media.Color)e.NewValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnOldColorChanged(System.Windows.Media.Color oldColor, System.Windows.Media.Color newColor)
		{
			RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<System.Windows.Media.Color> e = new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<System.Windows.Media.Color>(oldColor, newColor);
			e.RoutedEvent = OldColorChangedEvent;
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedColorChanged(System.Windows.Media.Color oldColor, System.Windows.Media.Color newColor)
		{
			RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<System.Windows.Media.Color> e = new RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<System.Windows.Media.Color>(oldColor, newColor);
			e.RoutedEvent = SelectedColorChangedEvent;
			RaiseEvent(e);
		}

		protected override void OnTemplateChanged(ControlTemplate oldTemplate, ControlTemplate newTemplate)
		{
			templateApplied = false;
			if (oldTemplate != null && m_ColorSlider != null)
			{
				m_ColorSlider.ValueChanged -= BaseColorChanged;
				m_ColorDetail.MouseLeftButtonDown -= OnMouseLeftButtonDown;
				m_ColorDetail.PreviewMouseMove -= OnMouseMove;
				m_ColorDetail.SizeChanged -= ColorDetailSizeChanged;
				m_ColorDetail = null;
				m_ColorMarker = null;
				m_ColorSlider = null;
			}
			base.OnTemplateChanged(oldTemplate, newTemplate);
		}

		private void BaseColorChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			if (m_ColorPosition.HasValue)
			{
				determineColor(m_ColorPosition.Value);
			}
		}

		private void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(m_ColorDetail);
			updateMarkerPosition(position);
		}

		private void OnMouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(m_ColorDetail);
				updateMarkerPosition(position);
				Mouse.Synchronize();
			}
		}

		private void ColorDetailSizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (args.PreviousSize != System.Windows.Size.Empty && args.PreviousSize.Width != 0.0 && args.PreviousSize.Height != 0.0)
			{
				double num = args.NewSize.Width / args.PreviousSize.Width;
				double num2 = args.NewSize.Height / args.PreviousSize.Height;
				markerTransform.X *= num;
				markerTransform.Y *= num2;
			}
			else if (m_ColorPosition.HasValue)
			{
				markerTransform.X = m_ColorPosition.Value.X * args.NewSize.Width;
				markerTransform.Y = m_ColorPosition.Value.Y * args.NewSize.Height;
			}
		}

		private void setColor(System.Windows.Media.Color theColor)
		{
			m_color = theColor;
			if (templateApplied)
			{
				SetValue(AProperty, m_color.A);
				SetValue(RProperty, m_color.R);
				SetValue(GProperty, m_color.G);
				SetValue(BProperty, m_color.B);
				updateMarkerPosition(theColor);
			}
		}

		private void setOldColor(System.Windows.Media.Color theColor)
		{
			_oldColor = theColor;
		}

		private void updateMarkerPosition(System.Windows.Point p)
		{
			p.X = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(p.X, m_ColorDetail.ActualWidth));
			p.Y = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(p.Y, m_ColorDetail.ActualHeight));
			markerTransform.X = p.X;
			markerTransform.Y = p.Y;
			p.X = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, p.X / m_ColorDetail.ActualWidth));
			p.Y = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, p.Y / m_ColorDetail.ActualHeight));
			m_ColorPosition = p;
			determineColor(p);
		}

		private void updateMarkerPosition(System.Windows.Media.Color theColor)
		{
			m_ColorPosition = null;
			HsvColor hsvColor = ColorUtilities.ConvertRgbToHsv(theColor.R, theColor.G, theColor.B);
			m_ColorSlider.Value = hsvColor.H;
			System.Windows.Point value = new System.Windows.Point(hsvColor.S, 1.0 - hsvColor.V);
			m_ColorPosition = value;
			value.X *= m_ColorDetail.ActualWidth;
			value.Y *= m_ColorDetail.ActualHeight;
			markerTransform.X = value.X;
			markerTransform.Y = value.Y;
		}

		private void determineColor(System.Windows.Point p)
		{
			HsvColor hsvColor = new HsvColor(360.0 - m_ColorSlider.Value, 1.0, 1.0);
			hsvColor.S = p.X;
			hsvColor.V = 1.0 - p.Y;
			m_color = ColorUtilities.ConvertHsvToRgb(hsvColor.H, hsvColor.S, hsvColor.V);
			shouldFindPoint = false;
			m_color.ScA = (float)GetValue(ScAProperty);
			SetValue(HexadecimalStringProperty, m_color.ToString());
			shouldFindPoint = true;
		}
	}
