	internal class SpectrumSlider : Slider
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedColorProperty;

		private static string SpectrumDisplayName;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle m_spectrumDisplay;

		private LinearGradientBrush pickerBrush;

		public System.Windows.Media.Color SelectedColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Color)GetValue(SelectedColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static SpectrumSlider()
		{
			SelectedColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedColor", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Color), typeof(SpectrumSlider), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent));
			SpectrumDisplayName = "PART_SpectrumDisplay";
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(SpectrumSlider), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(SpectrumSlider)));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			m_spectrumDisplay = GetTemplateChild(SpectrumDisplayName) as System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle;
			updateColorSpectrum();
			OnValueChanged(double.NaN, base.Value);
		}

		protected override void OnValueChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			base.OnValueChanged(oldValue, newValue);
			System.Windows.Media.Color color = ColorUtilities.ConvertHsvToRgb(360.0 - newValue, 1.0, 1.0);
			SetValue(SelectedColorProperty, color);
		}

		private void updateColorSpectrum()
		{
			if (m_spectrumDisplay != null)
			{
				createSpectrum();
			}
		}

		private void createSpectrum()
		{
			pickerBrush = new LinearGradientBrush();
			pickerBrush.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.0);
			pickerBrush.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 1.0);
			pickerBrush.ColorInterpolationMode = ColorInterpolationMode.SRgbLinearInterpolation;
			List<System.Windows.Media.Color> list = ColorUtilities.GenerateHsvSpectrum();
			double num = 1.0 / (double)list.Count;
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				pickerBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop(list[i], (double)i * num));
			}
			pickerBrush.GradientStops[i - 1].Offset = 1.0;
			m_spectrumDisplay.Fill = pickerBrush;
		}
	}
	internal static class ColorUtilities
	{
		public static HsvColor ConvertRgbToHsv(int r, int b, int g)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = Math.Min(Math.Min(r, g), b);
			double num3 = Math.Max(Math.Max(r, g), b);
			double num4 = num3 - num2;
			double num5 = ((num3 != 0.0) ? (num4 / num3) : 0.0);
			if (num5 == 0.0)
			{
				num = 0.0;
			}
			else
			{
				if ((double)r == num3)
				{
					num = (double)(g - b) / num4;
				}
				else if ((double)g == num3)
				{
					num = 2.0 + (double)(b - r) / num4;
				}
				else if ((double)b == num3)
				{
					num = 4.0 + (double)(r - g) / num4;
				}
				num *= 60.0;
				if (num < 0.0)
				{
					num += 360.0;
				}
			}
			return new HsvColor
			{
				H = num,
				S = num5,
				V = num3 / 255.0
			};
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Color ConvertHsvToRgb(double h, double s, double v)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			if (s == 0.0)
			{
				num = v;
				num2 = v;
				num3 = v;
			}
			else
			{
				h = ((h != 360.0) ? (h / 60.0) : 0.0);
				int num4 = (int)Math.Truncate(h);
				double num5 = h - (double)num4;
				double num6 = v * (1.0 - s);
				double num7 = v * (1.0 - s * num5);
				double num8 = v * (1.0 - s * (1.0 - num5));
				switch (num4)
				{
				case 0:
					num = v;
					num2 = num8;
					num3 = num6;
					break;
				case 1:
					num = num7;
					num2 = v;
					num3 = num6;
					break;
				case 2:
					num = num6;
					num2 = v;
					num3 = num8;
					break;
				case 3:
					num = num6;
					num2 = num7;
					num3 = v;
					break;
				case 4:
					num = num8;
					num2 = num6;
					num3 = v;
					break;
				default:
					num = v;
					num2 = num6;
					num3 = num7;
					break;
				}
			}
			return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, (byte)(num * 255.0), (byte)(num2 * 255.0), (byte)(num3 * 255.0));
		}

		public static List<System.Windows.Media.Color> GenerateHsvSpectrum()
		{
			List<System.Windows.Media.Color> list = new List<System.Windows.Media.Color>(8);
			for (int i = 0; i < 29; i++)
			{
				list.Add(ConvertHsvToRgb(i * 12, 1.0, 1.0));
			}
			list.Add(ConvertHsvToRgb(0.0, 1.0, 1.0));
			return list;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Media.Brush CloneBrush(System.Windows.Media.Brush brush)
		{
			if (brush is ImageBrush imageBrush)
			{
				return new ImageBrush
				{
					AlignmentX = AlignmentX.Left,
					AlignmentY = AlignmentY.Top,
					Stretch = Stretch.None,
					ImageSource = imageBrush.ImageSource
				};
			}
			if (brush is GradientBrush gradientBrush)
			{
				LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush;
				GradientBrush gradientBrush2;
				if ((linearGradientBrush = gradientBrush as LinearGradientBrush) != null)
				{
					gradientBrush2 = new LinearGradientBrush
					{
						StartPoint = linearGradientBrush.StartPoint,
						EndPoint = linearGradientBrush.EndPoint,
						GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection(),
						Opacity = linearGradientBrush.Opacity
					};
				}
				else
				{
					if (!(gradientBrush is RadialGradientBrush radialGradientBrush))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Unrecognised gradient brush.", "brush");
					}
					gradientBrush2 = new RadialGradientBrush
					{
						Center = radialGradientBrush.Center,
						RadiusX = radialGradientBrush.RadiusX,
						RadiusY = radialGradientBrush.RadiusY,
						Opacity = radialGradientBrush.Opacity
					};
				}
				foreach (GradientStop gradientStop in linearGradientBrush.GradientStops)
				{
					gradientBrush2.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
					{
						Color = gradientStop.Color,
						Offset = gradientStop.Offset
					});
				}
				return gradientBrush2;
			}
			if (brush is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush)
			{
				return new SolidColorBrush(solidColorBrush.Color)
				{
					Opacity = solidColorBrush.Opacity
				};
			}
			throw new ArgumentException("Unrecognised brush type.", "brush");
		}
	}
	internal struct HsvColor
	{
		public double H;

		public double S;

		public double V;

		public HsvColor(double h, double s, double v)
		{
			H = h;
			S = s;
			V = v;
		}
	}
	public class ColorThumb : Thumb
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ThumbColorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PointerOutlineThicknessProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PointerOutlineBrushProperty;

		public System.Windows.Media.Color ThumbColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Color)GetValue(ThumbColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ThumbColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double PointerOutlineThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(PointerOutlineThicknessProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PointerOutlineThicknessProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush PointerOutlineBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Brush)GetValue(PointerOutlineBrushProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PointerOutlineBrushProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static ColorThumb()
		{
			ThumbColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ThumbColor", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Color), typeof(ColorThumb), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent));
			PointerOutlineThicknessProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PointerOutlineThickness", typeof(double), typeof(ColorThumb), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1.0));
			PointerOutlineBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PointerOutlineBrush", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(ColorThumb), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ColorThumb), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(ColorThumb)));
		}
	}
	public class ColourRadioButton : RadioButton
	{
		public static DependencyProperty CornerRadiusProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CornerRadius", typeof(CornerRadius), typeof(ColourRadioButton), new PropertyMetadata(new CornerRadius(0.0)));

		public CornerRadius CornerRadius
		{
			get
			{
				return (CornerRadius)GetValue(CornerRadiusProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CornerRadiusProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ColourRadioButton()
		{
			base.DefaultStyleKey = typeof(ColourRadioButton);
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Support.AutomationPeers
{
	public class UIABaseCommandProcessor : IValueProvider
	{
		private bool _returnCommandResult;

		private IValueProvider _baseValueProvider;

		private string _commandResult;

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				if (_baseValueProvider == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return _baseValueProvider.IsReadOnly;
			}
		}

		public string Value
		{
			get
			{
				string result = null;
				if (_returnCommandResult)
				{
					result = _commandResult;
					_commandResult = null;
					_returnCommandResult = false;
				}
				else if (_baseValueProvider != null)
				{
					result = _baseValueProvider.Value;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public DependencyObject Owner { get; private set; }

		public static string UIACommandPrefix => "!#UIA:";

		public UIABaseCommandProcessor(UIElement owner, IValueProvider baseValueProvider)
		{
			Owner = owner;
			_baseValueProvider = baseValueProvider;
		}

		public void SetValue(string value)
		{
			if (IsUIACommand(value))
			{
				_returnCommandResult = true;
				_commandResult = TryProcessUIACommand(value);
			}
			else if (_baseValueProvider != null)
			{
				_baseValueProvider.SetValue(value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsUIACommand(string data)
		{
			return string.Compare(data, 0, UIACommandPrefix, 0, UIACommandPrefix.Length, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0;
		}

		private string TryProcessUIACommand(string data)
		{
			string result = null;
			try
			{
				string[] array = data.Substring(UIACommandPrefix.Length).Split(new char[1] { ':' }, 2);
				if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
				{
					string commandKey = array[0];
					string commandData = string.Empty;
					if (array.Length > 1)
					{
						commandData = array[1];
					}
					result = ProcessUIACommand(commandKey, commandData);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected virtual string ProcessUIACommand(string commandKey, string commandData)
		{
			string result = null;
			switch (commandKey.ToUpperInvariant())
			{
			case "SETPROPERTY":
				result = ProcessSetPropertyCommand(commandData);
				break;
			case "GETPROPERTY":
				result = ProcessGetPropertyCommand(commandData);
				break;
			case "EXECUTECOMMANDPROPERTY":
				ProcessExecuteCommandProperty(commandData);
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected string ProcessExecuteCommandProperty(string commandData)
		{
			if (Owner == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string result = "false";
			try
			{
				string propertyPath = commandData;
				object parameter = null;
				string[] array = commandData.Split(new char[1] { '=' }, 2);
				if (array != null && array.Length == 2)
				{
					propertyPath = array[0];
					parameter = array[1];
				}
				if (ResolvePropertyPath(Owner, propertyPath) is ICommand command)
				{
					command.Execute(parameter);
					result = "true";
				}
				else
				{
					result = "false";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected string ProcessGetPropertyCommand(string commandData)
		{
			if (Owner == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			object obj = ResolvePropertyPath(Owner, commandData);
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return obj.ToString();
		}

		protected string ProcessSetPropertyCommand(string commandData)
		{
			if (Owner == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			string result = "false";
			try
			{
				string[] array = commandData.Split(new char[1] { '=' }, 2);
				if (array == null || array.Length != 2)
				{
					return null;
				}
				string path = array[0];
				object value = array[1];
				ResolveUpdatePropertyPath(Owner, path, value);
				result = "true";
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static object ResolvePropertyPath(object start, string propertyPath)
		{
			if (start == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(propertyPath))
			{
				return start;
			}
			if (propertyPath.IndexOf('.') < 0 && propertyPath.IndexOf('[') < 0)
			{
				return GetPropertyValue(start, propertyPath);
			}
			object obj = start;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = true;
			bool flag3 = false;
			foreach (char c in propertyPath)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					if (c == ']' && !flag3)
					{
						string text = stringBuilder.ToString();
						stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
						int result = 0;
						if (flag2)
						{
							flag2 = int.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							IList list = obj as IList;
							obj = ((result < 0 || result >= list.Count) ? null : list[result]);
						}
						else
						{
							IDictionary dictionary = obj as IDictionary;
							try
							{
								obj = dictionary[text];
							}
							catch
							{
								obj = null;
							}
						}
						flag = false;
					}
					else if (flag3)
					{
						if (c == '"')
						{
							flag3 = false;
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(c);
						}
					}
					else if (c == '"' && stringBuilder.Length == 0)
					{
						flag2 = false;
						flag3 = true;
					}
					else
					{
						if (!char.IsDigit(c))
						{
							flag2 = false;
						}
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
				}
				else if (c == '.' || c == '[')
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						string property = stringBuilder.ToString();
						stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
						obj = GetPropertyValue(obj, property);
						if (obj == null)
						{
							return obj;
						}
						if (c == '[')
						{
							flag = true;
							flag2 = true;
							flag3 = false;
						}
					}
					else if (c != '.')
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Invalid syntax in property path", "propertyPath");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				obj = GetPropertyValue(obj, stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public static void ResolveUpdatePropertyPath(object start, string path, object value)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) || start == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (path.IndexOf('.') < 0 && path.IndexOf('[') < 0)
			{
				SetPropertyValue(start, path, value);
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = true;
			bool flag3 = false;
			object obj = start;
			int length = path.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				char c = path[i];
				bool flag4 = i == length - 1;
				if (!flag)
				{
					if (c != '.' && c != '[')
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
					else
					{
						if (stringBuilder.Length <= 0 && c == '.')
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text = stringBuilder.ToString();
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							throw new ArgumentException("Invalid syntax in property path");
						}
						stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
						if (flag4)
						{
							SetPropertyValue(obj, text, value);
						}
						else
						{
							obj = GetPropertyValue(obj, text);
							if (obj == null)
							{
								return;
							}
						}
						if (c == '[')
						{
							flag = true;
							flag2 = true;
							flag3 = false;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (c == ']' && !flag3)
				{
					string text2 = stringBuilder.ToString();
					stringBuilder.Remove(0, stringBuilder.Length);
					int result = 0;
					if (flag2)
					{
						flag2 = int.TryParse(text2, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
					}
					if (flag2)
					{
						IList list = obj as IList;
						if (flag4)
						{
							list[result] = value;
						}
						else
						{
							obj = list[result];
						}
					}
					else
					{
						IDictionary dictionary = obj as IDictionary;
						if (flag4)
						{
							dictionary[text2] = value;
						}
						else
						{
							obj = dictionary[text2];
						}
					}
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (flag3)
				{
					if (c == '"')
					{
						flag3 = false;
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
				}
				else if (c == '"' && stringBuilder.Length == 0)
				{
					flag2 = false;
					flag3 = true;
				}
				else
				{
					if (!char.IsDigit(c))
					{
						flag2 = false;
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Invalid format in property path");
				}
				string property = stringBuilder.ToString();
				SetPropertyValue(obj, property, value);
			}
		}

		private static object GetPropertyValue(object component, string property)
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(component).Find(property, ignoreCase: true)?.GetValue(component);
		}

		private static void SetPropertyValue(object component, string property, object value)
		{
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(component).Find(property, ignoreCase: true);
			if (propertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Property does not exist", "property");
			}
			if (!propertyDescriptor.PropertyType.IsAssignableFrom(value.GetType()))
			{
				TypeConverter converter = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(propertyDescriptor.PropertyType);
				if (converter != null)
				{
					value = converter.ConvertFrom(value);
				}
			}
			propertyDescriptor.SetValue(component, value);
		}
	}
	public class UIACustomControlAutomationPeer : FrameworkElementAutomationPeer
	{
		private IValueProvider _valueProviderImpl;

		public string ClassName { get; set; }

		public AutomationControlType ControlType { get; set; }

		public Func<FrameworkElement, string> GetNameFunction { get; set; }

		public Func<FrameworkElement, IValueProvider, IValueProvider> CreateCustomCommandProcessorFunction { get; set; }

		public UIACustomControlAutomationPeer(FrameworkElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			ControlType = AutomationControlType.Custom;
			CreateCustomCommandProcessorFunction = (FrameworkElement control, IValueProvider baseValueProvider) => new UIABaseCommandProcessor(control, baseValueProvider);
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			string text = null;
			if (GetNameFunction != null)
			{
				return GetNameFunction(base.Owner as FrameworkElement);
			}
			return base.GetNameCore();
		}

		protected override string GetClassNameCore()
		{
			string text = ClassName;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = base.GetClassNameCore();
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return ControlType;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (CreateCustomCommandProcessorFunction != null && patternInterface == PatternInterface.Value)
			{
				if (_valueProviderImpl == null)
				{
					IValueProvider arg = base.GetPattern(patternInterface) as IValueProvider;
					_valueProviderImpl = CreateCustomCommandProcessorFunction(base.Owner as FrameworkElement, arg);
				}
				return _valueProviderImpl;
			}
			return base.GetPattern(patternInterface);
		}
	}
